




|| namau tassa bhagavatau arahatau sammāsambuddhassa||

vinayapiṭaka

saṃgāyanassa pucchā vissaj‍janā

aukāsa dānakathā

suṇātu  mai bhantai saṅghau, mahatī ayaṃ parisā, aivarūpāya parisāya na sukarā sabbasau kathaṃ sāvaituṃ, tasmā āyasmatau ca javanatthairassa pakhuk‍kūnagaravāsinau āyasmatau ca vicittasārābhivaṃsassa tipiṭakadhara dhammabhaṇḍāgārikassa vinayapucchanabhārañ‍ca vinayavissaj‍jana bhārañ‍ca āvahituṃ aukāsaṃ dammi, karautha tumhai āvusau chaṭṭhasaṃgītipubbaṅgamāni vinayapucchana vinayavissaj‍jana kic‍cāni yathādhammaṃ yathāvinayaṃ|

aggamahāpaṇḍita  bhadantajavana mahāthairaina

ṭhapitā

pucchaka sammuti ñātti

suṇātu mai bhantai saṅghau, yadi saṅghassa pattakal‍laṃ, ahaṃ āyasmantaṃ vicittasārābhivaṃsaṃ tipiṭakadhara dhammabhaṇḍāgārikaṃ vinayaṃ pucchaiyyaṃ|

tipiṭakadhara dhammabhaṇḍāgārika

bhadantavicittasārābhivaṃsaina

ṭhapitā vissaj‍jaka sammuti ñātti

suṇātu mai bhantai saṅghau, yadi saṅghassa pattakal‍laṃ, ahaṃ āyasmatā javanatthairaina saṅghanāyaka mahāthairassa paṭinidhibhūtaina vinayaṃ puṭṭhau vissaj‍jaiyyaṃ|

paṭhama pārājika sikkhāpucchā

pucchā – yaṃ  taina āvusau bhagavatā jānatā passatā arahatā sammāsambuddhaina paṭhamaṃ pārājikaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – vaisāliyaṃ bhantai pañ‍ñattaṃ|

pucchā – kaṃ āvusau ārabbha pañ‍ñattaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – sudin‍naṃ bhantai kalandaputtaṃ ārabbha pañ‍ñattaṃ|

pucchā – kismiṃ āvusau vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – sudindau bhantai kalandaputtau purāṇadutiyikāya maithunaṃ dhammaṃ paṭisaivi, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

pucchā – atthi āvusau tattha pañ‍ñatti anupañ‍ñatti anuppan‍napañ‍ñatti|

vissaj‍janā – aikā bhantai pañ‍ñatti dvai anupañ‍ñattiyau, anuppan‍napañ‍ñatti tasmiṃ natthi|

pucchā – sabbatthapañ‍ñattinukhau  āvusau padaisapañ‍ñattinukhau|

vissaj‍janā – sabbatthapañ‍ñatti bhantai|

pucchā – sādhāraṇapañ‍ñattinukhau āvusau asādhāraṇapañ‍ñattinukhau|

vissaj‍janā – sādhāraṇapañ‍ñatti bhantai|

pucchā – aikataupañ‍ñattinukhau āvusau ubhataupañ‍ñattinukhau|

vissaj‍janā – ubhataupañ‍ñatti bhantai|

pucchā – pañ‍can‍naṃ āvusau pātimaukkhuddaisānaṃ katthaugadhaṃ kattha pariyāpan‍naṃ|

vissaj‍janā – nidānaugadhaṃ  bhantai nidānapariyāpan‍naṃ|

pucchā – katamaina āvusau uddaisaina uddaisaṃ āgacchati|

vissaj‍janā – dutiyaina bhantai uddaisaina uddaisaṃ āgacchati|

pucchā – catun‍naṃ āvusau vipattīnaṃ katamā vipatti|

vissaj‍janā – sīlavipatti bhantai|

pucchā – kā āvusau vipatti|

vissaj‍janā – asaṃvarau bhantai vipatti|

pucchā – kā āvusau sampatti|

vissaj‍janā – saṃvarau bhantai sammatti|

pucchā – kā  āvusau paṭipatti|

vissaj‍janā – na aivarūpaṃ karissāmīti yāvajīvaṃ āpāṇakauṭikaṃ samādāya sikkhati sikkhāpadaisu, ayaṃ bhantai paṭipatti|

pucchā – kati āvusau atthavasai paṭic‍ca bhagavatā paṭhamaṃ pārājikaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – dasa bhantai atthavasai paṭic‍ca bhagavatā paṭhamaṃ pārājikaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ, 1 - saṅghasuṭṭhutāya, 2 - saṅghaphāsutāya, 3 - dummaṅkūnaṃ puggalānaṃ niggahāya, 4 - paisalānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ phāsuvihārāya, 5 - diṭṭhadhammikānaṃ āsavānaṃ saṃvarāya, 6 - samparāyikānaṃ āsavānaṃ paṭighātāya, 7 - appasan‍nānaṃ pasādāya, 8 - pasan‍nānaṃ bhiyyaubhāvāya, 9 - saddhammaṭṭhitiyā, 10 - vinayānuggahāya|

pucchā – kai  āvusau sikkhanti|

vissaj‍janā – saikkhāca bhantai puthuj‍janakalyāṇakāca sikkhanti|

pucchā – kai āvusau sikkhitasikkhā|

vissaj‍janā – arahantau bhantai sikkhitasikkhā|

pucchā – kattha āvusau ṭhitaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – sikkhākāmaisu bhantai ṭhitaṃ|

pucchā – kai āvusau dhārainti|

vissaj‍janā – yaisaṃ bhantai vattati tai dhārainti|

pucchā – kassa  āvusau vacanaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – bhagavatau bhantai vacanaṃ arahatau sammāsambuddhassa|

pucchā – kaināvusau ābhataṃ|

vissaj‍janā – paramparāya bhantai ābhataṃ|

saṃgāyanatthāya saṅghassa ñāpanaṃ

suṇātu mai bhantai saṅghau ahaṃ bhantai āyasmantaṃ vicitthasārābhivaṃsaṃ tipiṭakadhara dhammabhaṇḍāgārikaṃ paṭhamassa pārājikassa nidānampi pucchiṃ, puggalampi pucchiṃ, vatthumpi pucchiṃ, pañ‍ñatti anupañ‍ñattiādīnipi pucchiṃ, puṭṭhau puṭṭhauca sau āyasmāvicittasārābhivaṃsau vissaj‍jaisi, itihidaṃ bhantai paṭhamaṃ pārājika sikkhāpadaṃ nimmalaṃ suparisuddhaṃ tassaiva bhagavatau vacanaṃ arahatau sammāsambuddhassa, tasmā yathāpurai mahākassapādayau mahāthairavarā paurāṇasaṃgītikārā brahmacariya saṅkhātassa buddhasāsanassa ciraṭṭhitiyā bahujanahitāya bahujanasukhāya laukānukampāya atthāya hitāya sukhāya daivamanussānaṃ dhammavinayaṃ saṅgāyiṃsucaiva anusaṅgāyiṃsuca, aivamaiva mayampi dāni sabbaiva chaṭṭhasaṃgītimahādhammasabhāpariyāpan‍nā idaṃ paṭhama pārājika sikkhāpadaṃ aikatausajjhāyanavasaina saṃgāyaiyyāma|

raivatābhi paṇḍitadhaja sāsanavaṃsa mahādhammarājagurunau

abhidhajamahāraṭṭhagurunauca saṅghanāyaka mahāthairassa

aukāsa dānakathā

suṇātu  mai bhantai saṅghau, mahatī ayaṃ parisā, aivarūpāya parisāya na sukarā sabbasau kathaṃ sāvaituṃ, tasmā āyasmatauca saubhanassa sāsanari sārāmādhivāsinau āyasmatauca vicittasārābhivaṃsassa tipiṭakadhara dhammabhaṇḍāgārikassa vinayapucchanabhārañ‍ca vinayavissaj‍janabhārañ‍ca āvahituṃ aukāsaṃ dammi, karautha tumhai āvusau chaṭṭhasaṃgītipubbaṅgamāni vinayapucchana vinayavissaj‍jana kic‍cāni yathādhammaṃ yathāvinayaṃ|

aggamahāpaṇḍita bhadanta saubhanatthairaina

ṭhapitā pucchaka sammuti ñātti

suṇātu mai bhantai saṅghau, yadi saṅghassa pattakal‍laṃ, ahaṃ āyasmantaṃ vicittasārābhivaṃsaṃ tipiṭakadhara dhammabhaṇḍāgārikaṃ vinayaṃ pucchaiyyaṃ|

tipiṭakadhara dhammabhaṇḍāgārika

bhadantavicittasārābhivaṃsaina

ṭhapitā vissaj‍jaka sammuti ñātti

suṇātu  mai bhantai saṅghau, yadisaṅghassa pattakal‍laṃ, ahaṃ āyasmatā saubhanatthairaina saṅghanāyaka mahāthairassa paṭinidhibhūtaina vinayaṃ puṭṭhau vissaj‍jaiyyaṃ|

dutiya pārājika sikkhāpucchā

pucchā – yaṃ  taina āvusau bhagavatā jānatā passatā arahatā sammāsambuddhaina dutiyaṃ pārājikaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – rājagahai bhantai pañ‍ñattaṃ|

pucchā – kaṃ āvusau ārabbha pañ‍ñattaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – dhaniyaṃ bhantai kumbhakāraputtaṃ ārabbha pañ‍ñattaṃ|

pucchā – kismiṃ āvusau vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – dhaniyau bhantai kumbhakāraputtau rañ‍ñau dārūni adin‍naṃ ādiyi, tasmiṃ vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

pucchā – atthi  āvusau tattha pañ‍ñatti anupañ‍ñatti anuppan‍napañ‍ñatti|

vissaj‍janā – aikā bhantai pañ‍ñatti aikā anupañ‍ñatti anuppan‍napañ‍ñatti tasmiṃ natthi|

pucchā – kā āvusau tattha mūlapañ‍ñatti|

vissaj‍janā – yau pana bhikkhu adin‍naṃ thaiyyasaṅkhātaṃ ādiyaiyya, yathārūpai adin‍nādānai rājānau cauraṃ gahaitvā hanaiyyuṃvā bandhaiyyuṃvā pabbājaiyyuṃvā caurausi bālausi muḷhausi thainausīti tathārūpaṃ bhikkhu adin‍naṃ ādiyamānau ayampi pārājikau hauti asaṃvāsauti ayaṃ bhantai tattha mūlapañ‍ñatti|

pucchā – kā  āvusau tattha anupañ‍ñatti|

vissaj‍janā – gāmāvā arañ‍ñāvāti ayaṃ bhantai tattha anupañ‍ñatti|

pucchā – kā āvusau tattha paripuṇṇapañ‍ñatti|

vissaj‍janā – yau pana bhikkhu gāmāvā arañ‍ñāvā adin‍naṃ thaiyyasaṅkhātaṃ ādiyaiyya, kathārūpai adin‍nādānai rājānau cauraṃ gahaitvā hanaiyyuṃvā bandhaiyyuṃvā pabbājaiyyuṃvā caurausi bālausi mūḷhausi thainausīti tathārūpaṃ bhikkhu adin‍naṃ ādiyamānau ayampi pārājikau hauti asaṃvāsauti ayaṃ bhantai tattha paripuṇṇapañ‍ñatti|

pucchā – sabbatthapañ‍ñatti  nukhau āvusau padaisapañ‍ñattinukhau|

vissaj‍janā – sabbatthapañ‍ñatti bhantai|

pucchā – sādhāraṇapañ‍ñattinukhau āvusau asādhāraṇapañ‍ñatti nukhau|

vissaj‍janā – sādhāraṇapañ‍ñatti bhantai|

pucchā – aikataupañ‍ñattinukhau āvusau ubhataupañ‍ñattinukhau|

vissaj‍janā – ubhataupañ‍ñatti bhantai|

pucchā – tasmiṃ  āvusau dutiya pārājikai adin‍naṃ ādiyantau kati āpattiyau āpaj‍jati vibhajitvā vissaj‍jaihi|

vissaj‍janā – adin‍naṃ bhantai ādiyantau tissau āpattiyau āpaj‍jati, pañ‍ca māsakaṃvā atiraika pañ‍ca māsakaṃvā agghanakaṃ adin‍naṃ thaiyyasaṅkhātaṃ ādiyati āpatti pārājikassa, ūnapañ‍camāsakaṃvā atiraikamāsakaṃvā adin‍naṃ thaiyyasaṅkhātaṃ ādiyati āpatti thul‍lac‍cayassa, māsakaṃvā ūnamāsakavā agghanakaṃ adin‍naṃ thaiyyasaṅkhātaṃ ādiyati āpatti duk‍kaṭassa, adin‍naṃ bhantai ādiyantau imā tissau āpattiyau āpaj‍jati|

pucchā – kaisaṃ  āvusau anāpatti|

vissaj‍janā – dasan‍naṃ bhantai puggalānaṃ anāpatti, sakasañ‍ñissa, vissāsaggāhaina gaṇhantassa, tāvakālikaṃ gaṇhantassa, paitapariggahaṃ gaṇhantassa, tiracchānagatapariggahaṃ gaṇhantassa, paṃsukūlasañ‍ñissa, ummattakassa, khittacittassa, vaidanāṭṭassa, ādikammikassāti|

pucchā – pañ‍can‍naṃ āvusau pātimaukkhuddaisānaṃ katthaugadhaṃ katthapariyāpan‍naṃ|

vissaj‍janā – nidānaugadhaṃ bhantai nidānapariyāpan‍naṃ|

pucchā – katamaina  āvusau uddaisaina uddaisaṃ āgacchati|

vissaj‍janā – dutiyaina bhantai uddaisaina uddaisaṃ āgacchati|

pucchā – catun‍naṃ āvusau vipattīnaṃ katamā vipatti|

vissaj‍janā – sīlavipatti bhantai|

pucchā – sattan‍naṃ āvusau āpattikkhandhānaṃ katamau āpattikkhandhau|

vissaj‍janā – pārājikāpattikkhandhau bhantai|

pucchā – chan‍naṃ āvusau āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ katihi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhāti|

vissaj‍janā – tīhi bhantai samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhāti, siyā kāyatauca cittatauca samuṭṭhāti, na vācātau, siyā vācātauca

cittatauca  samuṭṭhāti, na kāyatau, siyā kāyatauca vācātauca cittatauca samuṭṭhāti|

pucchā – catun‍naṃ āvusau adhikaraṇānaṃ katamaṃ adhikaraṇaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – āpattādhikaraṇaṃ bhantai|

pucchā – sattan‍naṃ āvusau samathānaṃ katihi samathaihi sammati|

vissaj‍janā – dvihi bhantai samathaihi sammati sammukhāvinayainaca paṭiñ‍ñāta karaṇainaca|

pucchā – kau  āvusau tattha vinayau, kau tattha abhivinayau|

vissaj‍janā – pañ‍ñatti bhantai vinayau vibhatti abhivinayau|

pucchā – kiṃāvusau tattha pātimaukkhaṃ kiṃ tattha adhipātimaukkhaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – pañ‍ñatti bhantai pātimaukkhaṃ, vibhatti adhipātimaukkhaṃ|

pucchā – kā āvusau vipatti|

vissaj‍janā – asaṃvarau bhantai vipatti|

pucchā – kā  āvusau sampatti|

vissaj‍janā – saṃvarau bhantai sampatti|

pucchā – kā āvusau paṭipatti|

vissaj‍janā – na aivarūpaṃ karissāmīti yāvajīvaṃ āpāṇakauṭikaṃ samādāya sikkhati sikkhāpadaisu ayaṃ bhantai tattha paṭipatti|

pucchā – kati āvusau atthavasai paṭic‍ca bhagavatā dutiyaṃ pārājikaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – dasa bhantai atthavasai paṭic‍ca bhagavatā dutiyaṃ pārājikaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ| saṅghasuṭṭhutāya saṅghaphāsutāya dummaṅkūnaṃ puggalānaṃ niggahāya paisalānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ phāsuvihārāya diṭṭhadhammikānaṃ āsavānaṃ saṃvarāya samparāyikānaṃ āsavānaṃ paṭighātāya appasan‍nānaṃ pasādāya pasan‍nānaṃ bhiyyaubhāvāya saddhammaṭṭhitiyā vinayānuggahāya|

pucchā – kau  āvusau sikkhanti|

vissaj‍janā – saikkhāca bhantai puthuj‍janakalyāṇakāca sikkhanti|

pucchā – kai āvusau sikkhitasikkhā|

vissaj‍janā – arahantau bhantai sikkhitasikkhā|

pucchā – kattha  āvusau ṭhitaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – sikkhākāmaisu bhantai ṭhitaṃ|

pucchā – kai āvusau dhārainti|

vissaj‍janā – yaisaṃ bhantai vattati tai dhārainti|

pucchā – kassa āvusau vacanaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – bhagavatā bhantai vacanaṃ arahatau sammāsammuddhassa|

pucchā – kaināvusau  ābhataṃ|

vissaj‍janā – paramparāya bhantai ābhataṃ|

pucchā – atthi nu khau āvusau tattha upanaitabbaṃ vā apanaitabbaṃ vā akkharapadapac‍cābhaṭṭhaṃ vā viraddhapadabyañ‍janaṃ vā|

vissaj‍janā – natthi bhantai|

‘‘saṃgāyanatthāya saṅghassa ñāpanaṃ’’

suṇātu  mai bhantai saṅghau ahaṃ bhantai āyasmantaṃ vicittasārābhivaṃsa tipiṭakadharadhammabhaṇḍāgārikaṃ dutiyassa pārājikassa nidānampi pucchiṃ, puggalampi pucchiṃ, vatthumpi pucchiṃ, pañ‍ñattimpi pucchiṃ, anupañ‍ñattimpi pucchiṃ, āpattimpi pucchiṃ, anāpattimpi pucchiṃ, añ‍ñānipi īdisāni pucchitabbaṭṭhānāni anaikāni pucchiṃ, puṭṭhau puṭṭhauca sau āyasmā vicittasārābhivaṃsau vissaj‍jaisi| iti hidaṃ bhantai idaṃ dutiyaṃ pārājika sikkhāpadaṃ nimmalaṃ, suparisuddhaṃ, tassaiva bhagavatau vacanaṃ arahatau sammāsambuddhassa, tasmā yathā purai mahākassa pādayau mahāthairavarā paurāṇasaṃgītikārā brahmacariyasaṅkhātassa buddhasāsanassa ciraṭṭhitiyā, bahujanahitāya, bahujanasukhāya laukānukampāya atthāya, hitāya, sukhāya, daivamanussānaṃ dhammavinayaṃ saṅgāyiṃsucaiva anusaṅgāyiṃsuca, aivamaiva mayampi dāni sabbaiva chaṭṭhasaṃgītimahādhammasabhāpariyāpan‍nā idaṃ dutiya pārājika sikkhāpadaṃ aikatau gaṇasajjhāyaṃ katvā samaggā sammaudamānā hutvā saṅgāyaiyyāma|

tatiya pārājika sikkhāpucchā

pucchā – yaṃ  taina āvusau bhagavatā (pa) sammāsambuddhaina tatiyaṃ pārājikaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – vaisāliyaṃ bhantai pañ‍ñattaṃ|

pucchā – kaṃ āvusau ārabbha pañ‍ñattaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – sambahulai bhantai bhikkhū ārabbha pañ‍ñattaṃ|

pucchā – kismiṃ āvusau vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – sambahulā bhantai bhikkhu añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ jīvitā vauraupaisuṃ, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

pucchā – atthi  nukhau āvusau tattha pañ‍ñatti anupañ‍ñatti anuppan‍napañ‍ñatti|

vissaj‍janā – aikā bhantai pañ‍ñatti, aikā anupañ‍ñatti, anuppan‍napañ‍ñatti tasmiṃ natthi|

pucchā – kā āvusau tattha mūlapañ‍ñatti|

vissaj‍janā – yaupana bhikkhu sañ‍cic‍ca manussaviggahaṃ jīvitā vauraupaiyya, satthahārakaṃvāssa pariyaisaiyya ayampi pārājikau hauti asaṃvāsauti ayaṃ bhantai tattha mūlapañ‍ñatti|

pucchā – kā āvusau tattha anupañ‍ñatti|

vissaj‍janā – maraṇavaṇṇaṃ vā saṃvaṇṇaiyya maraṇāya vā samādapaiyya ambhaupurisa kiṃ tuyhiminā pāpakaina duj‍jīvitaina matantai jīvitā

saiyyāti  iti cittamanau cittasaṅkappau anaikapariyāyaina maraṇavaṇṇaṃ vā saṃvaṇṇaiyya maraṇāya vā samādapaiyyāti ayaṃ bhantai tattha anupañ‍ñatti|

pucchā – kā āvusau tattha paripuṇṇapañ‍ñatti|

vissaj‍janā – yau pana bhikkhu sañ‍cic‍ca manussaviggahaṃ jīvitā vauraupaiyya, satthahārakaṃvāssa pariyaisaiyya, maraṇavaṇṇaṃ vā saṃvaṇṇaiyya, maraṇāya vā samādapaiyya, ambhau purisa kiṃ tuyhiminā pāpakaina duj‍jīvitaina matantai jīvitā saiyyauti iti cittamanau cittasaṅkappau anaikapariyāyaina maraṇavaṇṇaṃvā saṃvaṇṇaiyya, maraṇāya vā samādapaiyya, ayampi pārājikau hauti asaṃvāsauti ayaṃ bhantai tattha paripuṇṇapañ‍ñatti|

pucchā – sabbatthapañ‍ñatti  nukhau āvusaupadaisapañ‍ñatti nukhau|

vissaj‍janā – sabbatthapañ‍ñatti bhantai|

pucchā – sādhāraṇapañ‍ñattinukhau āvusau asādhāraṇapañ‍ñatti nukhau|

vissaj‍janā – sādhāraṇapañ‍ñatti bhantai|

pucchā – aikataupañ‍ñatti  nukhau āvusau ubhataupañ‍ñatti nukhau|

vissaj‍janā – ubhataupañ‍ñatti bhantai|

pucchā – tasmiṃ āvusau tatiya pārājikai sañ‍cic‍ca manussaviggahaṃ jīvitā vauraupaintau kati āpattiyau āpaj‍jati|

vissaj‍janā – sañ‍cissa bhantai manussaviggahaṃ jīvitā vauraupaintau tissau āpattiyau āpaj‍jati, manussaṃ audissa papātaṃ khaṇati papatitvā marissatīti āpatti duk‍kaṭassa, papatitai dukkhā vaidanā uppaj‍jati āpatti thul‍lac‍cayassa, marati āpatti pārājikassa, sañ‍cic‍ca bhantai manussaviggahaṃ jīvitā vauraupaintau imā tissau āpattiyau āpaj‍jati|

pucchā – kaisaṃ  āvusau anāpatti|

vissaj‍janā – sattan‍naṃ bhantai puggalānaṃ anāpatti, asañ‍cic‍ca māraintassa, ajānantassa, na maraṇādhippāyassa, ummattakassa, khittacittassa, vaidanāṭṭassa, ādikammikassa|

pucchā – pañ‍can‍naṃ āvusau pātimaukkhuddaisānaṃ katthaugadhaṃ kattha pariyāpan‍naṃ|

vissaj‍janā – nidānaugadhaṃ bhantai nidānapariyāpan‍naṃ|

pucchā – katamaina  āvusau uddaisaina uddaisaṃ āgacchati|

vissaj‍janā – dutiyaina bhantai uddaisaina uddaisaṃ āgacchati|

pucchā – catun‍naṃ āvusau vipattīnaṃ katamā vipatti|

vissaj‍janā – sīlavipatti bhantai|

pucchā – sattan‍naṃ āvusau āpattikkhandhānaṃ katamau āpattikkhandhau|

vissaj‍janā – pārājikāpattikkhandhau bhantai|

pucchā – chan‍naṃ āvusau āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ katihi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhāti|

vissaj‍janā – chan‍naṃ bhantai āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ tīhi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhāti, siyā kāyatauca cittatauca samuṭṭhāti, na vācatau, siyā vācatauca cittatauca samuṭṭhāti, na kāyatau, siyā kāyatauca vācatauca cittatauca samuṭṭhāti|

pucchā – catun‍naṃ āvusau adhikaraṇānaṃ katamaṃ adhikaraṇaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – āpattādhikaraṇaṃ bhantai|

pucchā – sattan‍naṃ  āvusau samathānaṃ katihi samathaihi sammati|

vissaj‍janā – sattānaṃ bhantai samathānaṃ dvīhi samathaihi sammati, sammukhā vinayainaca paṭiñ‍ñātakaraṇainaca|

pucchā – kau tattha āvusau vinayau kau tattha abhivinayau|

vissaj‍janā – pañ‍ñatti bhantai vinayau vibhatti abhivinayau|

pucchā – kiṃ āvusau tattha pātimaukkhaṃ kiṃ adhipātimaukkhaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – pañ‍ñatti bhantai pātimaukkhaṃ vibhatti adhipātimaukkhaṃ|

pucchā – kā  āvusau vipatti|

vissaj‍janā – asaṃvarau bhantai vipatti|

pucchā – kā āvusau sampatti|

vissaj‍janā – saṃvarau bhantai sampatti|

pucchā – kā āvusau tattha paṭipatti|

vissaj‍janā – na aivarūpaṃ karissāmīti yāvajīvaṃ āpāṇakauṭikaṃ samādāya sikkhati sikkhāpadaisu ayaṃ bhantai tattha paṭipatti|

pucchā – kati  āvusau atthavasai paṭic‍ca bhagavatā tatiyaṃ pārājikaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – dasa bhantai atthavasai paṭic‍ca bhagavatā tatiyaṃ pārājikaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ saṅghasuṭṭhutāya saṅghaphāsutāya (pa) vinayānuggahāya|

pucchā – kai  āvusau sikkhanti|

vissaj‍janā – saikkhāca bhantai puthuj‍janakalyāṇakāca sikkhanti|

pucchā – kai āvusau sikkhitasikkhā|

vissaj‍janā – arahantau bhantai sikkhitasikkhā|

pucchā – kattha āvusau ṭhitaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – sikkhākāmaisu bhantai ṭhitaṃ|

pucchā – kai āvusau dhārainti|

vissaj‍janā – yaisaṃ bhantai vattati tai dhārainti|

pucchā – kassa  āvusau vacanaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – bhagavatau bhantai vacanaṃ arahatau sammāsambuddhassa|

pucchā – kaināvusau ābhataṃ|

vissaj‍janā – paramparāya bhantai ābhataṃ|

pucchā – atthinukhau āvusau tattha kiñ‍ci upanaitabbaṃ vā apanaitabbaṃ vā akkharapadapac‍cābhaṭṭhaṃ vā viraddhapadabyañ‍janaṃ vā|

vissaj‍janā – natthi bhantai|

saṃgāyanatthāya saṅghassa ñāpanaṃ

suṇātu  mai bhantai saṅghau, ahaṃ bhantai āyasmantaṃ vicittasārābhivaṃsaṃ tipiṭakadhara dhammabhaṇḍāgārikaṃ tatiyassa pārājikassa nidānampi pucchiṃ, puggalampi pucchiṃ, vatthumpi pucchiṃ, pañ‍ñattimpi pucchiṃ, anupañ‍ñattimpi pucchiṃ, āpattimpi pucchiṃ, anāpattimpi pucchiṃ, añ‍ñānipi aivarūpāni pucchitabbaṭṭhānāni anaikāni pucchiṃ, puṭṭhau puṭṭhauca sau āyasmā vicittasārābhivaṃsau vissaj‍jaisi, itihidaṃ bhantai tatiyapārājikasikkhāpadaṃ nimmalaṃ suparisuddhaṃ tassaiva bhagavatau vacanaṃ arahatau sammāsammuddhassa, tasmā yathā purai mahākassapādayau mahāthairavarā paurāṇasaṃgītikārā brahmacariyasaṅkhātassa buddhasāsanassa ciraṭṭhitiyā bahujanahitāya bahujanasukhāya laukānukampāya atthāya hitāya sukhāya daivamanussānaṃ dhammavinayaṃ saṅgāyiṃsucaiva anusaṅgāyiṃsuca, aivamaiva mayampi dāni sabbaiva chaṭṭhasaṃgītimahādhammasabhāpariyāpan‍nā idaṃ tatiyapārājikasikkhāpadaṃ aikatau gaṇasajjhāyaṃ katvā samaggā sammaudamānā hutvā saṃgāyaiyyāma|

catuttha pārājika sikkhāpucchā

pucchā – yaṃ  taina āvusau bhagavatā jānatā passatā…pai॰… sammāsambuddhaina catutthaṃ pārājikaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – vaisāliyaṃ bhantai|

pucchā – kaṃ āvusau ārabbha pañ‍ñattaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – vaggumudātīriyai bhantai bhikkhū ārabbha pañ‍ñattaṃ|

pucchā – kismiṃ āvusau vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – vaggumudātīriyā bhantai bhikkhū gihīnaṃ añ‍ñamañ‍ñassa uttarimanussadhammassa vaṇṇaṃ bhāsiṃsu, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

pucchā – atthi  āvusau tattha pañ‍ñatti anupañ‍ñatti anuppan‍napañ‍ñatti|

vissaj‍janā – aikā bhantai pañ‍ñatti aikā anupañ‍ñatti anuppan‍napañ‍ñatti tasmiṃ natthi|

pucchā – katamā āvusau tattha mūlapañ‍ñatti|

vissaj‍janā – yau pana bhikkhu anabhijānaṃ uttarimanussadhammaṃ uttarimanussadhammaṃ attupanāyikaṃ alamariyañāṇadassanaṃ samudācaraiyya, itijānāmi itipassāmīti, tatau aparaina samayaina samanuggāhīya mānauvā asamanuggāhīya mānauvā āpan‍nau visuddhāpaikkhau aivaṃ vadaiyya ajānamaivaṃ āvusau avacaṃ jānāmi apassaṃ passāmi tucchaṃ musāvilapinti ayampi pārājikau hauti asaṃvāsauti ayaṃ bhantai tattha mūlapañ‍ñatti|

pucchā – katamā  āvusau tattha anupañ‍ñatti|

vissaj‍janā – añ‍ñatra adhimānāti ayaṃ bhantai tattha anupañ‍ñatti|

haunti yai tai ānanda bhikkhū adiṭṭhai diṭṭhasañ‍ñinau apattai pattasañ‍ñinau anadhigatai adhigatasañ‍ñinau asacchikatai sacchikatasañ‍ñanau adhimānaina añ‍ñaṃ byākaraunti, tañ‍ca khau aitaṃ abbauhārikaṃ|

pucchā – katamā  āvusau tattha paripuṇṇapañ‍ñatti|

vissaj‍janā – yaupana bhikkhu anabhijānaṃ uttarimanussadhammaṃ attupanāyikaṃ alamariyañāṇadassanaṃ samudācaraiyya, iti jānāmi iti passāmīti, tatau aparaina samayaina samanuggāhīyamānauvā asamanuggāhīyamānauvā āpan‍nau visuddhāpaikkhau aivaṃ vadaiyya ajānamaivaṃ āvusau avacaṃjānāmi apassaṃ passāmi tucchaṃ musā vilapinti, añ‍ñatra adhimānā ayampi pārājikau hauti asaṃvāsauti ayaṃ bhantai tattha paripuṇṇa pañ‍ñatti|

pucchā – sabbatthapañ‍ñatti  nukhau āvusau padaisapañ‍ñatti nukhau|

vissaj‍janā – sabbatthapañ‍ñatti bhantai|

pucchā – sādhāraṇapañ‍ñatti nukhau āvusau asādhāraṇapañ‍ñattinukhau|

vissaj‍janā – sādhāraṇapañ‍ñatti bhantai|

pucchā – aikataupañ‍ñatti nukhau āvusau ubhatau pañ‍ñatti nukhau|

vissaj‍janā – ubhataupañ‍ñatti bhantai|

pucchā – tasmiṃ  āvusau catuttha pārājikai asantaṃ abhūtaṃ uttarimanussadhammaṃ ul‍lapantau kati āpattiyau āpaj‍jati, vibhajitvā vissaj‍jaihi|

vissaj‍janā – tasmiṃ bhantai catutthapārājikai asantaṃ abhūtaṃ uttarimanussadhammaṃ ul‍lapantau tissau āpattiyau āpaj‍jati, asantaṃ abhūtaṃ uttarimanussadhammaṃ ul‍lapati āpattipārājikassa, yau tai vihārai vasati sau bhikkhu arahāti bhaṇati paṭivijānantassa āpatti thul‍lac‍cayassa, appaṭivijānantassa āpatti duk‍kaṭassa, uttarimanussadhammaṃ bhantai ul‍lapantau imā tissau āpattiyau āpaj‍jati|

pucchā – kaisaṃ  āvusau anāpatti|

vissaj‍janā – chan‍naṃ bhantai puggalānaṃ anāpatti adhimānaina bhaṇantassa anul‍lapanādhippāyassa ummattakassa khittacittassa vaidanāṭṭassa ādikammikassa|

pucchā – pañ‍can‍naṃ  āvusau pātimaukkhuddaisānaṃ katthaugadhaṃ kattha pariyāpan‍naṃ|

vissaj‍janā – nidānaugadhaṃ bhantai nidānapariyāpan‍naṃ|

pucchā – katamaina āvusau uddaisaina uddaisaṃ āgacchati|

vissaj‍janā – dutiyaina bhantai uddaisaina uddaisaṃ āgacchati|

pucchā – catun‍naṃ āvusau vipattīnaṃ katamā vipatti|

vissaj‍janā – sīlavipatti bhantai|

pucchā – sattan‍naṃ  āvusau āpattikkhandhānaṃ katamau āpattikkhandhau|

vissaj‍janā – pārājikāpattikkhandhau bhantai|

pucchā – chan‍naṃ āvusau āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ katihi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhāti|

vissaj‍janā – chan‍naṃ bhantai āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ tīhi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhāti, siyā kāyatauca cittatauca samuṭṭhāti, na vācatau, siyā vācatauca cittatauca samuṭṭhāti, na kāyatau, siyā kāyatauca vācatauca cittatauca samuṭṭhāti|

pucchā – catun‍naṃ  āvusau adhikaraṇānaṃ katamaṃ adhikaraṇaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – āpattādhikaraṇaṃ bhantai|

pucchā – sattan‍naṃ āvusau samathānaṃ katihi samathaihi sammati|

vissaj‍janā – sattan‍naṃ bhantai samathānaṃ dvīhi samathaihi sammati, sammukhāvinayainaca paṭiñ‍ñātakaraṇainaca|

pucchā – kau tattha āvusau vinayau, kau tattha abhivinayau|

vissaj‍janā – pañ‍ñatti bhantai vinayau, vibhatti abhivinayau|

pucchā – kiṃ  āvusau tattha pātimaukkhaṃ, kiṃ adhipātimaukkhaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – pañ‍ñatti bhantai pātimaukkhaṃ, vibhatti adhipātimaukkhaṃ|

pucchā – kā āvusau vipatti|

vissaj‍janā – asaṃvarau bhantai vipatti|

pucchā – kā āvusau sampatti|

vissaj‍janā – saṃvarau bhantai sampatti|

pucchā – kā  āvusau tattha paṭipatti|

vissaj‍janā – na aivarūpaṃ karissāmīti yāvajīvaṃ āpāṇakauṭikaṃ samādāya sikkhati sikkhāpadaisu ayaṃ bhantai tattha paṭipatti|

pucchā – kati āvusau atthavasai paṭic‍ca bhagavatā catutthaṃ pārājikaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – dasa bhantai atthavasai paṭic‍ca bhagavatā catutthaṃ pārājikaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ, saṅghasuṭṭhutāya saṅghaphāsutāya…pai॰… vinayānuggahāya|

pucchā – kai  āvusau sikkhanti|

vissaj‍janā – saikkhāca bhantai puthuj‍janakalyāṇakāca sikkhanti|

pucchā – kai āvusau sikkhitasikkhā|

vissaj‍janā – arahantau bhantai sikkhitasikkhā|

pucchā – kattha āvusau ṭhitaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – sikkhākāmaisu bhantai ṭhitaṃ|

pucchā – kai āvusau dhārainti|

vissaj‍janā – yaisaṃ bhantai vattati tai dhārainti|

pucchā – kassa  āvusau vacanaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – bhagavatau bhantai vacanaṃ arahatau sammāsambuddhassa|

pucchā – kaināvusau ābhataṃ|

vissaj‍janā – paramparāya bhantai ābhataṃ|

pucchā – atthi nukhau āvusau tattha kiñ‍ci upanaitabbaṃ vā apanaitabbaṃ vā akkharapadapac‍cābhaṭṭhaṃ vā viraddhapadabyañ‍janaṃ vā|

vissaj‍janā – natthi bhantai|

saṅghādisaisa sikkhāpucchā

1. suk‍kavissaṭṭhisaṅghādisaisa sikkhāpucchā

pucchā – yaṃ  taina āvusau bhagavatā jānatā passatā arahatā sammāsambuddhaina upak‍kamitvā asuciṃ maucaintassa saṅghādisaisau kattha pañ‍ñattau|

vissaj‍janā – sāvatthiyaṃ bhantai pañ‍ñattau|

pucchā – kaṃ āvusau ārabbha pañ‍ñattau|

vissaj‍janā – āyasmantaṃ bhantai saiyyasakaṃ ārabbha pañ‍ñattau|

pucchā – kismiṃ āvusau vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattau|

vissaj‍janā – āyasmā bhantai saiyyasakau hatthaina upak‍kamitvā asuciṃ maucaisi, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattau|

pucchā – atthi  āvusau tattha pañ‍ñatti anupañ‍ñatti anuppan‍napañ‍ñatti|

vissaj‍janā – aikā bhantai pañ‍ñatti, aikā anupañ‍ñatti, anuppan‍napañ‍ñatti tasmiṃ natthi|

pucchā – katamā āvusau tattha mūlapañ‍ñatti|

vissaj‍janā – sañ‍caitanikā suk‍kavissaṭṭhi saṅghādisaisauti ayaṃ bhantai tattha mūlapañ‍ñatti|

pucchā – katamā panāvusau tattha anupañ‍ñatti|

vissaj‍janā – añ‍ñatra supinantāti ayaṃ bhantai tattha anupañ‍ñatti|

pucchā – katamā  panāvusau tattha paripuṇṇapañ‍ñatti|

vissaj‍janā – sañ‍caitanikā suk‍kavissaṭṭhi añ‍ñatrasupinantā saṅghādisauti ayaṃ bhantai tattha paripuṇṇapañ‍ñatti|

pucchā – sādhāraṇa pañ‍ñattinukhau āvusau asādhāraṇa pañ‍ñattinukhau|

vissaj‍janā – asādhāraṇapañ‍ñatti bhantai|

pucchā – tattha āvusau upak‍kamitvā asuciṃ maucaintau kati āpattiyau āpaj‍jati|

vissaj‍janā – upak‍kamitvā bhantai asuciṃ maucaintau tissau āpattiyau āpaj‍jati, caitaiti upak‍kamati muc‍cati āpatti

saṅghādisaisassa, caitaiti upak‍kamati na muc‍cati āpatti thul‍lac‍cayassa, payaugai duk‍kaṭaṃ|

pucchā – kaisaṃ āvusau anāpatti|

vissaj‍janā – chan‍naṃ bhantai anāpatti supinantaina maucaintassa na maucanādhippāyassa ummattakassa khittacittassa vaidanāṭṭassa ādikammikassa imaisaṃ khau bhantai chan‍naṃ anāpatti|

pucchā – pañ‍can‍naṃ  āvusau pātimaukkhuddaisānaṃ katamaina uddaisaina uddaisaṃ āgacchati|

vissaj‍janā – pañ‍can‍naṃ bhantai pātimaukkhuddaisānaṃ tatiyaina uddaisaina uddaisaṃ āgacchati|

pucchā – catun‍naṃ āvusau vipattīnaṃ katamā vipatti|

vissaj‍janā – catun‍naṃ bhantai vipattīnaṃ sīlavipatti|

pucchā – kā āvusau vipatti|

vissaj‍janā – asaṃvarau bhantai vipatti|

pucchā – kā  āvusau sampatti|

vissaj‍janā – saṃvarau bhantai sampatti|

pucchā – kā āvusau paṭipatti|

vissaj‍janā – na aiva rūpaṃ karissāmīti yāvajīvaṃ āpāṇakauṭikaṃ samādāya sikkhati sikkhāpadaisu ayaṃ bhantai paṭipatti|

pucchā – kati āvusau atthavasai paṭic‍ca bhagavatā upak‍kamitvā asuciṃ maucaintassa saṅghādisaisau pañ‍ñattau|

vissaj‍janā – dasa  bhantai atthavasai paṭic‍ca bhagavatā upak‍kamitvā asuciṃ maucaintassa saṅghādisaisau pañ‍ñattau saṅghasuṭṭhutāya…pai॰… vinayānuggahāya|

pucchā – kattha āvusau ṭhitaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – sikkhākāmaisu bhantai ṭhitaṃ|

pucchā – kai āvusau dhārainti|

vissaj‍janā – yaisaṃ bhantai vattati tai dhārainti|

pucchā – kassa  āvusau vacanaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – bhagavatau bhantai vacanaṃ arahatau sammāsambuddhassa|

pucchā – kaināvusau ābhataṃ|

vissaj‍janā – paramparāya bhantai ābhataṃ|

pucchā – atthi nukhau āvusau tattha sanidānai sapadabhājanīyai savinītavatthukai upanaitabbaṃ vā apanaitabbaṃ vā akkharapadapac‍cābhaṭṭhaṃ vā viraddhapadabyañ‍janaṃ vā|

saṃgāyanatthāya saṅghassa ñāpanaṃ

suṇātu  mai bhantai saṅghau āyasmantaṃ vicittasārābhivaṃsaṃ tipiṭakadhara dhammabhaṇḍāgārikaṃ paṭhamassa saṅghādisaisa sikkhāpadassa nidānampi pucchiṃ, puggalampi pucchiṃ, vatthumpi pucchiṃ, pañ‍ñattimpi pucchiṃ, anupañ‍ñattimpi pucchi, āpattimpi pucchiṃ, anāpattimpi pucchiṃ, añ‍ñānipi aivarūpāni anaikāni pucchitabbaṭṭhānāni pucchiṃ, puṭṭhau puṭṭhau ca sau āyasmā vicittasārābhivaṃsau vissaj‍jaisi, itihidaṃ bhantai paṭhama saṅghādisaisa sikkhāpadaṃ saparivāraṃ nimmalaṃ suparisuddhaṃ tassaiva bhagavatau vacanaṃ arahatau sammāsambuddhassa| tasmā yathāpurai mahākassapādayau mahāthairavarā paurāṇasaṃgītikārā brahmacariyassa ciraṭṭhitiyā bahujanahitāya bahujanasukhāya laukānukampāya atthāya hitāya sukhāya daivamanussānaṃ  dhammavinayaṃ saṃgāyiṃsucaiva anusaṃgāyiṃsuca, aivamaiva mayampi sabbaiva chaṭṭhasaṃgītimahādhammasabhā pariyāpan‍nā idaṃ paṭhamaṃ saṅghādisaisa sikkhāpadaṃ nidānatau paṭṭhāya aikac‍cānaṃ vacasā sajjhāyavasaina aikac‍cānaṃ manasā manasikaraṇavasainaca aikatau gaṇasajjhāyaṃ katvā saṃgāyaiyyāma|

pucchā – yaṃ  taina āvusau bhagavatā…pai॰… sammāsambuddhaina mātukāmaina saddhiṃ kāyasaṃsaggaṃ samāpaj‍jantassa ca, mātugāmaṃ duṭṭhul‍lāhi vācāhi aubhāsantassa ca, mātugāmassa santikai attakāma pāricariyāya vaṇṇaṃ bhāsantassa ca, sañ‍carittaṃ samāpaj‍jantassa ca saṅghādisaisau kattha pañ‍ñattau|

vissaj‍janā – sāvatthiyaṃ bhantai pañ‍ñattau|

pucchā – kaṃ  āvusau ārabbha pañ‍ñattau|

vissaj‍janā – āyasmantaṃ bhantai udāyitthairaṃ ārabbha pañ‍ñattau|

pucchā – kismiṃ āvusau vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattau|

vissaj‍janā – āyasmā bhantai udāyitthairau mātugāmaina saddhiṃ kāyasaṃsaggaṃ samāpaj‍ji, āyasmāyaiva bhantai udāyitthairau mātugāmaṃ duṭṭhul‍lāhi vācāhi aubhāsi, āyasmāyaiva bhantai udāyitthairau mātugāmassa santikai attakāma pāricariyāya vaṇṇaṃ abhāsi, āyasmāyaiva bhantai udāyitthairau sañ‍carittaṃ samāpaj‍jiṃ, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattau|

6. kuṭikāra saṅghādisaisa sikkhāpucchā

pucchā – yaṃ  taina āvusau…pai॰… sammāsambuddhaina sañ‍ñācikāya kuṭiṃ kārāpaintassa saṅghādisaisau kattha pañ‍ñattau|

vissaj‍janā – āḷaviyaṃ bhantai pañ‍ñatthau|

pucchā – kaṃ āvusau ārabbha pañ‍ñattau|

vissaj‍janā – āḷavikai bhantai bhikkhū ārabbha pañ‍ñattau|

pucchā – kismiṃ  āvusau vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattau|

vissaj‍janā – āḷavikā bhantai bhikkhū sañ‍ñācikāyau kuṭiyau kārāpaisuṃ, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattau|

7. vihārakāra saṅghādisaisa sikkhāpucchā

pucchā – yaṃ taina āvusau…pai॰… sammāsambuddhaina mahal‍lakaṃ vihāraṃ kārāpaintassa saṅghādisaisau kattha pañ‍ñattau|

vissaj‍janā – kausambiyaṃ bhantai|

pucchā – kaṃ  āvusau ārabbha pañ‍ñattau|

vissaj‍janā – āyasmantaṃ bhantai chan‍natthairaṃ ārabbha pañ‍ñattau|

pucchā – kismiṃ āvusau vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattau|

vissaj‍janā – āyasmā bhantai chan‍nau vihāravatthuṃ saudhaintau añ‍ñataraṃ caitiyarukkhaṃ chaidāpaisi gāmapūjitaṃ nigamapūjitaṃ nagara pūjitaṃ janapadapūjitaṃ raṭṭhapūjitaṃ, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattau|

8-9. duṭṭhadausa saṅghādisaisa sikkhāpucchā

pucchā – yaṃ taina āvusau bhagavatā…pai॰… sammāsambuddhaina bhikkhuṃ amūlakaina pārājikaina dhammaina anuddhaṃsaintassa ca, bhikkhuṃ añ‍ñabhāgiyassa adhikaraṇassa kiñ‍cidaisaṃ laisamattaṃ upādāya pārājikaina dhammaina anuddhaṃsaintassa ca saṅghādisaisau kattha pañ‍ñattau|

vissaj‍janā – rājagahai  bhantai pañ‍ñattau|

pucchā – kaṃ āvusau ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattau|

vissaj‍janā – maittiyabhūmajakā bhantai bhikkhū ārabbha pañ‍ñattau, maittiyabhūmajakā bhantai bhikkhū āyasmantaṃ dabbaṃ mal‍laputtaṃ amūlakaina pārājikaina dhammaina anuddhaṃsaisuṃ, taiyaiva bhantai maittiyabhūmajakā bhikkhū āyasmantaṃ dabbaṃ mal‍laputtaṃ añ‍ñabhāgiyassa adhikaraṇassa kiñ‍cidaisaṃ laisamattaṃ upādāya pārājikaina dhammaina anuddhaṃsaisuṃ, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ, pañ‍ñattau|

10. saṅghabhaidaka saṅghādisaisa sikkhāpucchā

pucchā – yaṃ  taina āvusau bhagavatā…pai॰… sammāsambuddhaina saṅghabhaidakassa bhikkhunau yāvatatiyaṃ samanubhāsanāya appaṭinissaj‍jantassa saṅghādisaisau kattha pañ‍ñattau|

vissaj‍janā – rājagahai bhantai pañ‍ñattau|

pucchā – kaṃ  āvusau ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñatthau|

vissaj‍janā – daivadattaṃ bhantai ārabbha pañ‍ñattau|

daivadattau bhantai samaggassa saṅghassa bhaidāya parak‍kami, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattau|

11. bhaidānuvattaka sikkhāpucchā

pucchā – yaṃ  taina āvusau bhagavatā…pai॰… sammāsambuddhaina bhaidānuvattakānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ yāvatatiyaṃ samanubhāsanāya appaṭinissaj‍jantānaṃ saṅghādisaisau kattha pañ‍ñattau|

vissaj‍janā – rājagahai bhantai pañ‍ñattau|

pucchā – kaṃ āvusau ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattau|

vissaj‍janā – sambahulai bhantai bhikkhū ārabbha pañ‍ñattau, sambahulā bhantai bhikkhū daivadattassa saṅghābhaidāya parak‍kamantassa anuvattakā ahaisuṃ vaggavādakā, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattau|

12. dubbaca, saṅghādisaisa sikkhāpucchā

pucchā – yaṃ  taina āvusau bhagavatā…pai॰… sammāsambuddhaina dubbacassa bhikkhunau yāvatatiyaṃ samanubhāsanāya appaṭinissaj‍jantassa saṅghādisaisau kattha pañ‍ñattau|

vissaj‍janā – kausambiyaṃ bhantai pañ‍ñattau|

pucchā – kaṃ  āvusau ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattau|

vissaj‍janā – āyasmantaṃ bhantai chan‍naṃ ārabbha pañ‍ñattau, āyasmā bhantai chan‍nau bhikkhūhi sahadhammikaṃ vuc‍camānau attānaṃ avacanīyaṃ akāsi, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattau|

13. kuladūsaka saṅghādisaisa sikkhāpucchā

pucchā – yaṃ  taina āvusau bhagavatā…pai॰… sammāsambuddhaina kuladūsakassa bhikkhunau yāvatatiyaṃ samanubhāsanāya appaṭinissaj‍jantassa saṅghādisaisau kattha pañ‍ñattau|

vissaj‍janā – sāvatthiyaṃ  bhantai pañ‍ñattau|

pucchā – kaṃ ārabbha āvusau kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattau|

vissaj‍janā – assaji punabbasukai bhantai bhikkhū ārabbha pañ‍ñattau, assajipunabbasukā bhantai bhikkhū saṅghaina pabbājanīyakammakatā chandagāmitā dausagāmitā mauhagāmitā bhayagāmitā pāpaisuṃ, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattau|

aniyata sikkhāpucchā

pucchā – yaṃ  taina āvusau bhagavatā…pai॰… sammāsambuddhaina paṭhamau ca aniyatau dutiyau ca aniyatau kattha pañ‍ñattau|

vissaj‍janā – sāvatthiyaṃ bhantai pañ‍ñattau|

pucchā – kaṃ āvusau ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattau|

vissaj‍janā – āyasmantaṃ bhantai udāyiṃ ārabbha pañ‍ñattau, āyasmā bhantai udāyī mātugāmaina saddhiṃ aikauaikāya rahau paṭicchan‍nai āsanai alaṃkammaniyai nisaj‍jaṃ kappaisi, tasmiñ‍ca vatthusmiṃ, āyasmāyaiva bhantai udāyī mātugāmaina saddhiṃ rahau nisaj‍jaṃ akāsi, tasmiñ‍ca bhantai vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattau|

pucchā – kassa  āvusau vacanaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – bhagavatau bhantai vacanaṃ arahatau sammāsambuddhassa|

pucchā – kaināvusau ābhataṃ|

vissaj‍janā – paramparāya bhantai ābhataṃ|

pucchā – atthi nukhau āvusau aittha kaucipi viraddhadausau|

vissaj‍janā – natthi bhantai|

saṃgāyanatthāya saṅghassañāpanaṃ

suṇātu  mai bhantai saṅghau, ahaṃ bhantai āyasmantaṃ vicittasārābhivaṃsaṃ tipiṭakadhara dhammabhaṇḍāgārikaṃ paṭhama duṭṭhadausa sikkhāpadatau paṭṭhāya pucchitabbāni anaikāni ṭhānāni pucchiṃ, sau ca āyasmā puṭṭhau puṭṭhau vissaj‍jaiti, itihidaṃ bhantai sabbaṃ sikkhāpadaṃ nimmalaṃ suparisuddhaṃ tassaiva bhagavatau vacanaṃ arahatau sammāsambuddhassa, tasmā yathāpurai mahākassapādayau mahāthairavarā paurāṇasaṃgītikārā brahmacariyassa ciraṭṭhitiyā bahujanahitāya bahujanasukhāya laukānukampāya atthāya hitāya sukhāya daivamanussānaṃ saṃgāyiṃsucaiva anusaṃgāyiṃsuca, aivamaiva mayampi dāni sabbaiva chaṭṭhasaṃgīti mahādhammasabhāpariyāpan‍nā imāni sikkhāpadāni aikatau gaṇasajjhāyaṃ katvā saṃgāyaiyyāma|

nissaggipācittiya

1. cīvaravagga 1. paṭhamakathina sikkhāpucchā

pucchā – yaṃ  taina āvusau bhagavatā…pai॰… sammāsambuddhaina atiraikacīvaraṃ dasāhaṃ atik‍kāmaintassa nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – vaisāliyaṃ bhantai pañ‍ñattaṃ|

pucchā – kaṃ āvusau ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – chabbaggiyai bhantai bhikkhū ārabbha pañ‍ñattaṃ, chabbaggiyā bhantai bhikkhū atiraikacīvaraṃ dhāraisuṃ, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

1. cīvaravagga 2. udausita sikkhāpucchā

pucchā – aikarattaṃ  āvusau ticivaraina vippavasantassa nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ bhagavatā kattha pañ‍ñattaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – sāvatthiyaṃ bhantai pañ‍ñattaṃ|

pucchā – kaṃ āvusau ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – sambahulai bhantai bhikkhū ārabbha pañ‍ñattaṃ, sambahulā bhantai bhikkhū bhikkhūnaṃ hatthai cīvaraṃ nikkhipitvā santaruttaraina janapadacārikaṃ pak‍kamiṃsu, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

1. cīvaravagga 3. tatiyakathina sikkhāpucchā

pucchā – akālacīvaraṃ  āvusau paṭiggahaitvā māsaṃ atik‍kāmaintassa nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ bhagavatā kattha pañ‍ñattaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – sāvatthiyaṃ bhantai pañ‍ñattaṃ|

pucchā – kaṃ āvusau ārabbbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – sambahulai bhantai bhikkhū ārabbha pañ‍ñattaṃ, sambahulā bhantai bhikkhū akālacīvaraṃ paṭiggahaitvā māsaṃ atik‍kāmaisuṃ, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

1. cīvaravagga 4. purāṇacīvara sikkhāpucchā

pucchā – añ‍ñātikāya  āvusau bhikkhuniyā purāṇacīvaraṃ dhauvāpaintassa nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ bhagavatā kattha pañ‍ñattaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – sāvatthiyaṃ bhantai pañ‍ñattaṃ|

pucchā – kaṃ āvusau ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – āyasmantaṃ bhantai udāyiṃ ārabbha pañ‍ñattaṃ, āyasmā bhantai udāyī añ‍ñātikāya bhikkhuniyā purāṇacīvaraṃ dhauvāpaisi, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

1. cīvaravagga 5. cīvarapaṭiggahaṇa sikkhāpucchā

pucchā – añ‍ñātikāya  āvusau bhikkhuniyā hatthatau cīvaraṃ paṭiggaṇhantassa nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ bhagavatā kattha pañ‍ñattaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – rājagahai bhantai pañ‍ñattaṃ|

pucchā – kaṃ āvusau ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – āyasmantaṃ bhantai udāyiṃ ārabbha pañ‍ñattaṃ, āyasmā bhantai udāyī añ‍ñātikāya bhikkhuniyā hatthatau cīvaraṃ paṭiggahaisi, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

1. cīvaravagga 6. añ‍ñātakaviñ‍ñatti sikkhāpucchā

pucchā – añ‍ñātakaṃ  āvusau gahapatiṃvā gahatāniṃvā cīvaraṃ viñ‍ñāpaintassa nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ bhagavatā kattha pañ‍ñattaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – sāvatthiyaṃ bhantai pañ‍ñattaṃ|

pucchā – kaṃ āvusau ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – āyasmantaṃ bhantai upanandaṃ sakyaputtaṃ ārabbha pañ‍ñattaṃ, āyasmā bhantai upanandau sakyaputtau añ‍ñātakaṃ saiṭṭhiputtaṃ cīvaraṃ viñ‍ñāpaisi, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

1. cīvaravagga 7. tatuttari sikkhāpucchā

pucchā – añ‍ñātakaṃ  āvusau gahapatiṃ vā gahapatāniṃ vā tatuttari cīvaraṃ viñ‍ñāpaintassa nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ bhagavatā kattha pañ‍ñattaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – sāvatthiyaṃ bhantai pañ‍ñattaṃ|

pucchā – kaṃ āvusau ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – chabbaggiyai bhantai bhikkhū ārabbha pañ‍ñattaṃ, chabbaggiyā bhantai bhikkhū na mattaṃ jānitvā bahumpi cīvaraṃ viñ‍ñāpaisuṃ, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

pucchā – pubbai  appavāritassa āvusau añ‍ñātakaṃ gahapatiṃ vā gahatāniṃ vā upasaṅkamitvā cīvarai vikappaṃ āpaj‍jantassa ca añ‍ñātakai gahapatikai upasaṅkamitvā cīvarai vikappaṃ āpaj‍jantassa ca nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ bhagavatā kattha pañ‍ñattaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – sāvatthiyaṃ bhantai pañ‍ñattaṃ|

pucchā – kaṃ āvusau ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – āyasmantaṃ bhantai upanandaṃ sakyaputtaṃ ārabbha pañ‍ñattaṃ, āyasmā bhantai upanandau sakyaputtau pubbai appavāritau añ‍ñātakaṃ gahapatikaṃ upasaṅkamitvā cīvarai vikappaṃ āpaj‍ji| sauyaiva bhantai āyasmā upanandau sakyaputtau pubbai appavāritau añ‍ñātakai gahapatikai upasaṅkamitvā cīvarai vikappaṃ āpaj‍ji, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

1. cīvaravagga 10. rāja sikkhāpucchā

pucchā – atiraikatikkhattuṃ āvusau caudanāya atiraika chakkhattuṃ ṭhānaina cīvaraṃ abhinipphādaintassa nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ bhagavatā kattha pañ‍ñattaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – sāvatthiyaṃ bhantai pañ‍ñattaṃ|

pucchā – kaṃ  āvusau ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – āyasmantaṃ bhantai upanandaṃ sakyaputtaṃ ārabbha pañ‍ñattaṃ, āyasmā bhantai upanandau sakyaputtau upāsakaina aj‍jaṇhau bhantai āgamaihīti vuc‍camānau nāgamaisi, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

2. kausiyavagga, 1. kausiya sikkhāpucchā

pucchā – kausiyamissakaṃ āvusau santhakaṃ kārāpaintassa nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ bhagavatā kattha pañ‍ñattaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – āḷaviyaṃ bhantai pañ‍ñattaṃ|

pucchā – kaṃ 0 āvusau ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – chabbaggiyai bhantai ārabbha pañ‍ñattaṃ, chabbaggiyā bhantai bhikkhū kausiyakārakai upasaṅkamitvā aivamahaṃsu bahū āvusau kausakārakai pacatha amhākampi dassatha mayampi icchāma kausiyamissakaṃ santakaṃ kātunti, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

2. kausiyavagga 2. suddhakāḷaka sikkhāpucchā

pucchā – suddhakāḷakānaṃ āvusau aiḷakalaumānaṃ santhataṃ kārāpaintassa nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ bhagavatā kattha pañ‍ñattaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – vaisāliyaṃ bhantai pañ‍ñatthaṃ|

pucchā – kaṃ  āvusau ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – chabbaggiyai bhantai bhikkhū ārabbha pañ‍ñattaṃ, chabbaggiyā bhantai bhikkhū suddhakāḷakānaṃ aiḷakalaumānaṃ santhataṃ kārāpaisuṃ, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

2. kausiyavagga 3. dvaibhāga sikkhāpucchā

pucchā – anādiyitvā āvusau tulaṃ audātānaṃ tulaṃ gaucariyānaṃ navaṃ santhataṃ kārāpaintassa nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ bhagavatā kattha pañ‍ñattaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – sāvatthiyaṃ bhantai pañ‍ñattaṃ|

pucchā – kaṃ  āvusau ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – chabbaggiyai bhantai bhikkhū ārabbha pañ‍ñattaṃ, chabbaggiyā bhantai bhikkhū thaukaṃyaiva audātaṃ antai ādiyitvā tathaiva suddhakāḷakānaṃ aiḷakalaumānaṃ santhataṃ kārāpaisuṃ, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

2. kausiyavagga 4. chabbassa sikkhāpucchā

pucchā – yaṃ taina āvusau bhagavatā…pai॰… sammāsambuddhaina anuvassaṃ kārāpaintassa nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – sāvatthiyaṃ bhantai pañ‍ñattaṃ|

pucchā – kaṃ  āvusau ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – sambahulai bhantai bhikkhū ārabbha pañ‍ñattaṃ, sambahulā bhantai bhikkhū anuvassaṃ santhataṃ kārāpaisuṃ, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

pucchā – kassa āvusau vacanaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – bhagavatau bhantai vacanaṃ arahatau sammāsambuddhassa|

pucchā – kaināvusau ābhataṃ|

vissaj‍janā – paramparāya bhantai ābhataṃ|

pucchā – atthi  nukhau āvusau aittha kauci viraddhadausau|

vissaj‍janā – natthi bhantai|

saṃgāyanatthāya saṅghassa ñāpanaṃ

suṇātu mai bhantai saṅghau, ahaṃ āyasmantaṃ vicittasārābhivaṃsaṃ tipiṭakadhara dhammabhaṇḍāgārikaṃ imaisaṃ nissaggiyapācittiyasikkhāpadānaṃ yāva chabbassasikkhāpadaṃ nidānādīni pucchitabbāni anaikāni ṭhānāni pucchiṃ, puṭṭhaupuṭṭhau ca sau āyasmā vicittasārābhivaṃsau vissaj‍jaisi, itihidaṃ bhantai imāni sikkhāpadāni nimmalāni suparisuddhānī tassaiva bhagavatau vacanāni arahatau sammāsambuddhassa, tasmā yathāpurai mahākassapādayau mahāthairavarā paurāṇasaṃgītikārā brahmacariyassa ciraṭṭhitiyā bahujanahitāya bahujanasukhāya laukānukampāya atthāya hitāya sukhāya daivamanussānaṃ dhammavinayaṃ saṃgāyiṃsu caiva anusaṃgāyiṃsu ca, aivamaiva mayampi dāni sabbaiva chaṭṭhasaṃgīti mahādhammasabhā pariyāpan‍nā chabbassa nissaggiya sikkhāpadatau paṭṭhāya imāni nissaggiyasikkhāpadāni aikatau gaṇasajjhāyaṃ katvā saṃhāyaiyyāma|

2. kausiyavagga 5. nisīdanasanthata sikkhāpucchā

pucchā – yaṃ  taina āvusau bhagavatā…pai॰… sammāsambuddhaina anādiyitvā purāṇasanthatassa sāmantā sugatavidattiṃ navaṃ nisīdanasanthataṃ kārāpaintassa nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – sāvatthiyaṃ bhantai pañ‍ñattaṃ|

pucchā – kaṃ  āvusau ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – sambahulai bhantai bhikkhū ārabbha pañ‍ñattaṃ, sambahulā bhantai bhikkhū santhatāni ujjhitvā ārañ‍ñikaṃ piṇḍapātikaṅgaṃ paṃsukūlakaṅgaṃ samādiyiṃsu, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

2. kausiyavagga 6. aiḷakalauma sikkhāpucchā

pucchā – aiḷakalaumāni āvusau paṭiggahaitvā tiyaujanaṃ atik‍kāmaintassa nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – sāvatthiyaṃ bhantai pañ‍ñattaṃ|

pucchā – kaṃ  āvusau ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – añ‍ñataraṃ bhantai bhikkhuṃ ārabbha pañ‍ñattaṃ, añ‍ñatarau bhantai bhikkhu aiḷakalaumāni paṭiggahaitvā tiyaujanaṃ atik‍kāmaisi, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

2. kausiyavagga 7. aiḷakalaumadhauvāpana sikkhāpucchā

pucchā – añ‍ñātikāya  āvusau bhikkhuniyā aiḷakalaumāni dhauvāpaintassa nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – sak‍kaisu bhantai pañ‍ñattaṃ|

pucchā – kaṃ āvusau ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – chabbaggiyai bhantai bhikkhū ārabbha pañ‍ñattaṃ, chabbaggiyā bhantai bhikkhū añ‍ñātikāhi bhikkhūnīhi aiḷakalaumāni dhauvāpaisuṃ, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

2. kausiyavagga 8. rūpiya sikkhāpucchā

pucchā – rūpiyaṃ  āvusau paṭiggaṇhantassa nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ bhagavatā kattha pañ‍ñattaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – rājagahai bhantai pañ‍ñattaṃ|

pucchā – kaṃ āvusau ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – āyasmantaṃ bhantai upanandaṃ sakyaputtaṃ ārabbha pañ‍ñattaṃ, āyasmā bhantai upanandau sakyaputtau rūpiyaṃ paṭiggahaisi, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

pucchā – kathaṃ  āvusau tattha paṭipaj‍jantau rūpiyaṃ paṭiggaṇhāti nāma|

vissaj‍janā – sayaṃ bhantai gaṇhāti, añ‍ñaṃ vā gaṇhāpaisi, idaṃ ayyassa hautūti upanikkhittaṃ sādiyati, aivaṃ khau bhantai paṭipaj‍jantau bhikkhu rūpiyaṃ paṭiggaṇhāti nāma|

pucchā – kathaṃ āvusau tattha paṭipaj‍jantau bhikkhu rūpiyaṃ na paṭiggaṇhāti nāma|

vissaj‍janā – yathā  bhantai rājasikkhāpadai yathā ca bhantai maiṇḍakasikkhāpadai āgacchati, tathā bhantai paṭipaj‍jantau bhikkhu rūpiyaṃ na paṭiggaṇhātināma|

2. kausiyavagga 9. rūpiyasaṃvauhāra sikkhāpucchā

pucchā – rūpiyasaṃvauhāraṃ  āvusau samāpaj‍jantassa nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ bhagavatā kattha pañ‍ñattaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – sāvatthiyaṃ bhantai pañ‍ñattaṃ|

pucchā – kaṃ āvusau ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – chabbaggiyai bhantai bhikkhū ārabbha pañ‍ñattaṃ, chabbaggiyā bhantai bhikkhū nānappakārakaṃ rūpiyasaṃvauhāraṃ samāpaj‍jiṃsu, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

2. kausiyavagga, 10. kayavik‍kaya sikkhāpucchā

pucchā – nānappakārakaṃ āvusau kayavik‍kayaṃ āpaj‍jantassa nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ bhagavatā kattha pañ‍ñattaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – sāvatthiyaṃ bhantai pañ‍ñattaṃ|

pucchā – kaṃ āvusau ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – āyasmantaṃ bhantai upanandaṃ sakyaputtaṃ ārabbha pañ‍ñattaṃ, āyasmā bhantai upanandau sakyaputtau paribbājakaina saddhi kayavik‍kayaṃ samāpaj‍ji, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

pucchā – kathaṃ āvusau tattha paṭipaj‍jantau bhikkhu kayavik‍kayaṃ samāpaj‍jatināma|

vissaj‍janā – iminā imaṃ daihi iminā imaṃ āhara iminā imaṃ parivattaihi iminā imaṃ caitāmaihīti ajjhācarati, aivaṃ khau bhantai paṭipaj‍jantau kayavik‍kayaṃ samāpaj‍jatināma|

pucchā – kathaṃ  āvusau tattha paṭipaj‍jantau bhikkhu na kayavik‍kayaṃ samāpaj‍jatināma|

vissaj‍janā – yau bhantai agghaṃ pucchati, yau ca bhantai kappiyakārakassa ācikkhati, yau ca bhantai amhākañ‍ca idaṃ atthi amhākañ‍ca iminā ca iminā ca atthauti bhaṇati, aivaṃ khau bhantai paṭipaj‍jantau bhikkhu na kayavik‍kayaṃ samāpaj‍jatināma|

3. pattavagga, 1. patta sikkhāpucchā

pucchā – atiraikapattaṃ āvusau dasāhaṃ atik‍kāmaintassa nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – sāvatthiyaṃ bhantai pañ‍ñattaṃ|

pucchā – kaṃ  āvusau ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – chabbaggiyai bhantai bhikkhū ārabbha pañ‍ñattaṃ, chabbaggiyā bhantai bhikkhū atiraikapattaṃ dhāraisuṃ, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

514 3. pattavagga, 2. ūnapañ‍cabandhana sikkhāpucchā

pucchā – ūnapañ‍cabandhanaina pattaina añ‍ñaṃ navaṃ pattaṃ caitāpaintassa nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – sak‍kaisu bhantai pañ‍ñattaṃ|

pucchā – kaṃ  āvusau ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – chabbaggiyai bhantai bhikkhū ārabbha pañ‍ñattaṃ, chabbaggiyā bhantai bhikkhū appamattakainapi bhin‍naina appamattakainapi khaṇḍaina vilikhitamattainapi bahū pattai viyyāpaisuṃ, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

na bhikkhavai pattau viñ‍ñāpaitabbau, yau viñ‍ñāpaiyya āpatti duk‍kaṭassa|

3. pattavagga 3. bhaisaj‍ja sikkhāpucchā

pucchā – bhaisaj‍jāni  āvusau paṭiggahaitvā sattāhaṃ atik‍kāmaintassa nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – sāvatthiyaṃ bhantai pañ‍ñattaṃ|

pucchā – kaṃ āvusau ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – sambahulai bhantai bhikkhū ārabbha ārabbha pañ‍ñattaṃ, sambahulā bhantai bhikkhū bhaisaj‍jāni paṭiggahaitvā sattāhaṃ atik‍kāmaisuṃ, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

3. pattavagga 4. vassikasāṭika sikkhāpucchā

pucchā – atiraikamāsai  saisai gimānai āvusau vassikasāṭikacīvaraṃ pariyaisantassa nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – sāvatthiyaṃ bhantai pañ‍ñattaṃ|

pucchā – kaṃ  āvusau ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – chabbaggiyai bhantai bhikkhū ārabbha pañ‍ñattaṃ, chabbaggiyā bhantai bhikkhū atiraikamāsai saisai gimhānai vassikasāṭikacīvaraṃ pariyaisiṃsu, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

3. pattavagga 5. cīvaraacchindana sikkhāpucchā

pucchā – bhikkhussa āvusau sāmaṃ cīvaraṃ datvā kupitaina anattamanaina acchindantassa nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – sāvatthiyaṃ bhantai pañ‍ñattaṃ|

pucchā – kaṃ  āvusau ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – āyasmantaṃ bhantai upanandaṃ sakyaputtaṃ ārabbha pañ‍ñattaṃ, āyasmā bhantai upanandau sakyaputtau bhikkhussa sāmaṃ cīvaraṃ datvā kupitau anattamanau acchindi, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

3. pattavaggau, 6. suttaviñ‍ñatti sikkhāpucchā

pucchā – sāmaṃ āvusau suttaṃ viñ‍ñāpaitvā tantavāyaihi cīvaraṃ vāyāpaintassa nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – rājagahai bhantai pañ‍ñattaṃ|

pucchā – kaṃ āvusau ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – chabbaggiyai bhantai bhikkhū ārabbha pañ‍ñattaṃ, chabbaggiyā bhantai bhikkhū sāmaṃ suttaṃ viñ‍ñāpaitvā tantavāyaihi cīvaraṃ vāyāpaisuṃ, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

3. pattavagga, 7. mahāpaisakāra sikkhāpucchā

pucchā – pubbai  appavāritassa āvusau añ‍ñātakassa gahapatikassa tantavāyai upasaṅkamitvā cīvarai vikappaṃ āpaj‍jantassa nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – sāvatthi yaṃ bhantai pañ‍ñattaṃ|

pucchā – kaṃ  āvusau ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – āyasmantaṃ bhantai upanandaṃ sakyaputtaṃ ārabbha pañ‍ñattaṃ, āyasmā bhantai upanandau sakyaputtau pubbai appavāritau añ‍ñātakassa gahapatikassa tantavāyai upasaṅkamitvā cīvarai vikappaṃ āpaj‍ji, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

3. pattavaggau, 8. ac‍caikacīvara sikkhāpucchā

pucchā – ac‍caika cīvaraṃ āvusau paṭiggahaitvā cīvarakālasamayaṃ atik‍kāmaintassa nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – sāvatthiyaṃ bhantai pañ‍ñattaṃ|

pucchā – kaṃ  āvusau ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – sambahulai bhantai bhikkhū ārabbha pañ‍ñattaṃ, sambahulā bhantai bhikkhū ac‍caikacīvaraṃ paṭiggahaitvā cīvarakālasamayaṃ atik‍kāmaisuṃ, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

3. pattavaggau, 9. sāsaṅka sikkhāpucchā

pucchā – tiṇṇaṃ āvusau cīvarānaṃ añ‍ñataraṃ cīvaraṃ antaragharai nikkhipitvā atiraikachārattaṃ vippavasantassa nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – sāvatthiyaṃ bhantai pañ‍ñattaṃ|

pucchā – kaṃ  āvusau ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – sambahulai bhantai bhikkhū ārabbha pañ‍ñattaṃ, sambahulā bhantai bhikkhū tiṇṇaṃ cīvarānaṃ añ‍ñataraṃ cīvaraṃ antaragharai nikkhipitvā atiraikachārattaṃ vippavasiṃsu, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

3. pattavaggau, 10. pariṇata sikkhāpucchā

pucchā – yaṃ taina āvusau bhagavatā passatā arahatā sammāsambuddhaina jānaṃ saṅghikaṃ lābhaṃ atthanau pariṇāmaintassa nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – sāvatthiyaṃ bhantai pañ‍ñattaṃ|

pucchā – kaṃ  āvusau ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – chabbaggiyai bhantai bhikkhū ārabbha pañ‍ñattaṃ, chabbaggiyā bhantai bhikkhū jānaṃ saṅghikaṃ lābhaṃ pariṇataṃ attanau pariṇāmaisuṃ, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

pucchā – jānaṃ  āvusau saṅghikaṃ lābhaṃ pariṇataṃ attanau pariṇāmaintau, kati āpattiyau āpaj‍jati|

vissaj‍janā – jānaṃ bhantai saṅghikaṃ lābhaṃ pariṇataṃ attanau pariṇāmaintau dvai āpattiyau āpaj‍jati, pariṇāmaiti payaugai duk‍kaṭaṃ, pariṇāmitai nissaggiyaṃ pācittiyaṃ, jānaṃ bhantai saṅghikaṃ lābhaṃ pariṇataṃ attanau pariṇāmaintau imā dvai āpattiyau āpaj‍jati|

pucchā – kassa  āvusau vacanaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – bhagavatau bhantai vacanaṃ arahatau sammāsambuddhassa|

pucchā – kaināvusau ābhataṃ|

vissaj‍janā – paramparāya bhantai ābhataṃ|

pucchā – atthi nukhau āvusau tattha kaucipi viraddhadausau|

vissaj‍janā – natthi bhantai|

saṃgāyanatthāya ñāpanaṃ

suṇātu  mai bhantai saṅghau, ahaṃ bhantai imaisaṃ sauḷasan‍naṃ nissaggiyapācittiyasikkhāpadānaṃ nidānādīni anaikāni pucchitabbāni āyasmantaṃ vicittasārābhivaṃsaṃ tipiṭakadhara dhammabhaṇḍāgārikaṃ pucchiṃ, sau ca puṭṭhau puṭṭhau vissaj‍jaiti, itihidaṃ bhantai sikkhāpadasañ‍jātaṃ nimmalaṃ su parisuddhaṃ tassaiva bhagavatau vacanaṃ arahatau sammāsambuddhassa, tasmā yathā purai mahākassapādayau mahāthaira varā paurāṇasaṃgītikārā brahmacariyasaṅkhātassa buddhasāsanassa ciraṭṭhitiyā bahujanahitāya bahujanasukhāya laukānukampāya atthāya hitāya sukhāya daivamanussānaṃ dhammavinayaṃ saṃgāyiṃsucaiva anusaṃgāyiṃsuca, aivamaiva mayampi dāni chaṭṭhasaṃgīti mahādhammasabhāpariyāpan‍nā imāni sikkhāpadāni yathānuppattaṭṭhānatau paṭṭhāya aikatau gaṇasajjhāyaṃ katvā saṃgāyaiyyāma|

pācittiyapāḷi

1. musāvādavagga, 1. musāvāda sikkhāpucchā

pucchā – yaṃ taina āvusau bhagavatā jānatā passatā arahatā sammāsambuddhaina sampajānamusāvādai pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – sāvatthiyaṃ bhantai pañ‍ñattaṃ, hatthakaṃ sakyaputtaṃ ārabbha pañ‍ñattaṃ, hatthakau bhantai sakyaputtau titthiyaihi saddhiṃ sal‍lapantau avajānitvā paṭijānāti paṭijānitvā avajānāti, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

1. musāvādavaggau, 2-3. aumasavāda, paisuñ‍ña sikkhāpucchā

pucchā – aumasavādai pācittiyañ‍ca āvusau bhikkhupaisuñ‍ñai pācittiyañ‍ca bhagavatā katthapañ‍ñattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – sāvatthiyaṃ  bhantai pañ‍ñattaṃ, chabbaggiyai bhikkhū ārabbha pañ‍ñattaṃ, chabbaggiyā bhantai bhikkhū bhikkhūhi saddhiṃ bhaṇḍantā aumasiṃsu tasmiṃca vatthusmiṃ, chabbaggiyā bhantai bhikkhū bhikkhūnaṃ bhaṇḍanajātānaṃ kalahajātānaṃ vivādāpan‍nānaṃ paisuñ‍ñaṃ upasaṃhariṃsu, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

1. musāvādavagga, 4. padasaudhamma sikkhāpucchā

pucchā – anupasampan‍naṃ āvusau padasau vācaintassa pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – sāvatthiyaṃ bhantai pañ‍ñattaṃ, chabbaggiyai bhikkhū ārabbha pañ‍ñattaṃ, chabbaggiyā bhantai bhikkhū upāsakai padasau dhammaṃ vācaisuṃ, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

1. musāvādavaggau 5. sahasaiyya sikkhāpucchā

pucchā – anupasampan‍naina āvusau uttaridirattatirattaṃ sahasaiyyaṃ kappaintassa pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattaṃ, kaṃārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – āḷaviyaṃ bhantai pañ‍ñattaṃ, sambahulai bhikkhū ārabbha pañ‍ñattaṃ, sambahulā bhantai bhikkhū anupasampan‍naina sahasaiyyaṃ kappaisuṃ, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

pucchā – kaisaṃ  āvusau aittha anupasampan‍naina sahasaiyyaṃ kappaintānampi anāpatti hauti|

vissaj‍janā – aikādasan‍naṃ bhantai puggalānaṃ anupasampan‍naina sahasaiyyaṃ kappaintānaṃ anāpatti, dvaitissau rattiyau vasatī, ūnakadvai tissau rattiyau vasati, dvairattiyau vasitvā tatiyāyarattiyā purāruṇā nikkhamitvā puna vasati, sabbacchan‍nai sabbaaparicchan‍nai, sabbaparicchan‍nai sabbaacchan‍nai, yaibhuyyaina acchan‍nai yaibhuyyaina aparicchan‍nai, anupasampan‍nai nipan‍nai bhikkhu nisīdati, bhikkhunipan‍nai anupasampan‍nau nisīdati, ubhauvā nisīdanti, ummattakassa, ādikammikassa, imaisaṃkhau bhantai aikādasan‍naṃ puggalānaṃ anupasampan‍naina sahasaiyyaṃ kappaintānaṃ anāpatti|

1. musāvādavaggau, 6. dutiya sahasaiyya sikkhāpucchā

pucchā – mātugāmaina  āvusau sahasaiyyaṃ kappaintassa pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – sāvatthiyaṃ bhantai āyasmantaṃ anuruddhaṃ ārabbha pañ‍ñattaṃ, āyasmā bhantai anuruddhau mātugāmaina sahasaiyyaṃ kappaisi, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

1. musāvādavaggau, 7. dhammadaisanā sikkhāpucchā

pucchā – mātugāmassa  āvusau uttari chappañ‍cavācāhi dhammaṃ daisaintassa pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – sāvatthiyaṃ bhantai āyasmantaṃ udāyiṃ ārabbha pañ‍ñattaṃ, āyasmā bhantai udāyī mātugāmassa dhammaṃdaisaiti, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

1. musāvādavaggau, 8. bhūtāraucana sikkhāpucchā

pucchā – anupasampan‍nassa  āvusau uttarimanussadhammaṃ bhūtaṃ āraucaintassa pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – vaisāliyaṃ bhantai vaggumudātīriyai bhikkhū ārabbha pañ‍ñattaṃ, vaggumudātīriyā bhantai bhikkhū gihīnaṃ añ‍ñamañ‍ñassa uttarimanussadhammassa vaṇṇaṃ bhāsiṃsu, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

1. musāvādavaggau, 9. duṭṭhul‍lāraucana sikkhāpucchā

pucchā – bhikkhussa  āvusau duṭṭhul‍lāpattiṃ anupasampan‍nassa āraucaintassa pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – sāvatthiyaṃ bhantai chabbaggiyai bhikkhū ārabbha pañ‍ñattaṃ, chabbaggiyā bhantai bhikkhū bhikkhussa duṭṭhul‍lāpattiṃ anupasampan‍nassa āraucaisuṃ, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

1. musāvādavaggau, 10. pathavīkhaṇana sikkhāpucchā

pucchā – pathaviṃkhaṇantassa  āvusau pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – āḷaviyaṃ bhantai āḷavikai bhikkhū ārabbha pañ‍ñattaṃ, āḷavikā bhantai bhikkhū pathaviṃkhaṇiṃsu, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

2. bhūtagāmavaggau, 1. bhūtagāma sikkhāpucchā

pucchā – bhūtagāmapātabyatāya  pācittiyaṃ āvusau bhagavatā kattha pañ‍ñattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – āḷaviyaṃ bhantai āḷavikai bhikkhū ārabbha pañ‍ñattaṃ, āḷavikā bhantai bhikkhū rukkhaṃ chindiṃ su, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

2. bhūtagāmavagga, 2. añ‍ñavādaka sikkhāpucchā

pucchā – añ‍ñavādakai vihaisakai pācittiyaṃ āvusau bhagavatā kattha pañ‍ñattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – kausambiyaṃ  bhantai āyasmantaṃ chan‍naṃ ārabbha pañ‍ñattaṃ, āyasmā bhantai chan‍nau saṅghamajjhai āpattiyā anuyuñ‍jīyamānau añ‍ñainañ‍ñaṃ paṭicari, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

2. bhūtagāmavagga, 3. ujjhāpanaka sikkhāpucchā

pucchā – ujjhāpanakai khiyyanakai pācittiyaṃ āvusau bhagavatā kattha pañ‍ñattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – rājagahai bhantai maittiyabhūmajakai bhikkhū ārabbha pañ‍ñattaṃ, maittiyabhūmajakā bhantai bhikkhū āyasmantaṃ dabbaṃ mal‍laputtaṃ bhikkhū ujjhāpaisuṃ, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

2. bhūtagāmavagga, 4. paṭhama saināsana sikkhāpucchā

pucchā – saṅghikaṃ  āvusau mañ‍caṃvā pīṭhaṃvā bhisiṃvā kaucchaṃvā ajjhaukāsai santharitvā anuddharitvā anāpucchā pak‍kamantassa pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – sāvatthiyaṃ bhantai sambahulai bhikkhū ārabbha pañ‍ñattaṃ, sambahulā bhantai bhikkhū saṅghikaṃ saināsanaṃ ajjhaukāsai santharitvā anuddharitvā anāpucchā pak‍kamiṃsu, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

2. bhūtagāmavagga, 5. dutiya saināsana sikkhāpucchā

pucchā – saṅghikai  āvusau vihārai saiyyaṃ santharitvā anuddharitvā anāpucchā pak‍kamantassa pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – sāvatthiyaṃ bhantai sattarasavaggiyai bhikkhū ārabbha pañ‍ñattaṃ, sattarasavaggiyā bhantai bhikkhū saṅghikai vihārai saiyyaṃ santharitvā anuddharitvā anāpucchā pak‍kamiṃsu, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

pucchā – kassa  āvusau vacanaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – bhagavatā bhantai vacanaṃ arahatau sammāsambuddhassa|

pucchā – kaināvusau ābhataṃ|

vissaj‍janā – paramparāya bhantai ābhataṃ|

pucchā – atthi  nukhau āvusau aittha kaucipi viraddhadausau|

vissaj‍janā – natthi bhantai|

saṅghassa ñāpanaṃ

suṇātu mai bhantai saṅghau, ahaṃ bhantai āyasmantaṃ vicittasārābhivaṃsaṃ imaisaṃ pan‍narasan‍naṃ sikkhāpadānaṃ nidānādīni pucchitabbāni anaikāni ṭhānāni pucchiṃ, sau ca puṭṭhau puṭṭhau vissaj‍jaiti, iti hidaṃ bhantai nimmalaṃ suparisuddhaṃ tassaiva bhagavatau vacanaṃ arahatau sammāsambuddhassa, tasmā yathāpurai mahākassapādayau mahāthairavarā paurāṇasaṃgītikārā brahmacariyassa ciraṭṭhitiyā bahujanahitāya bahujanasukhāya launukampāya atthāya hitāya sukhāya daivamanussānaṃ dhammavinayaṃ saṃgāyiṃsucaiva anusaṃgāyiṃsuca, aivamaiva mayampi dāni chaṭṭhasaṃgītimahādhammasabhā pariyāpan‍nā yathānuppaṭṭhānatau paṭṭhāya imāni sikkhāpadāni aikatau gaṇasajjhāyaṃ katvā saṃgāyaiyyāma|

2. bhūtagāmavagga, 6. anupakhaj‍ja sikkhāpucchā

pucchā – yaṃ  taina āvusau…pai॰… sammāsambuddhaina saṅghikai vihārai jānaṃ pubbupagataṃ bhikkhuṃ anupakhaj‍ja saiyyaṃ kappaintassa pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – sāvatthiyaṃ bhantai chabbaggiyai bhikkhū ārabbha pañ‍ñattaṃ, chabbaggiyā bhantai bhikkhū thairai bhikkhū anupakhaj‍ja saiyyaṃ kappaisuṃ, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

2. bhūtagāmavagga, 7. nikaḍḍhana sikkhāpucchā

pucchā – bhikkhuṃ  āvusau kupitaina anattamanaina saṅghikā vihārā nik‍kaḍḍhaintassa pācittiyaṃ bhagavatā kattha pañ‍ñattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – sāvatthiyaṃ bhantai chabbaggiyai bhikkhū ārabbha pañ‍ñattaṃ, chabbaggiyā bhantai bhikkhū kupitā anattamanā bhikkhū saṅghikāvihārā nik‍kaḍḍhiṃsu, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

2. bhūtagāmavagga, 8. vaihāsakuṭi sikkhāpucchā

pucchā – uparivaihāsakuṭiyā āvusau āhac‍capādakaṃ mañ‍caṃvā pīṭhaṃvā abhinisīdantassa pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – sāvatthiyaṃ  bhantai añ‍ñataraṃ bhikkhuṃ ārabbha pañ‍ñattaṃ, añ‍ñatarau bhantai bhikkhu saṅghikai vihārai uparivaihāsa kuṭiyā āhac‍capādakaṃ mañ‍caṃ sahasā abhinisīdi, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

2. bhūtagāmavagga, 9. mahal‍lakavihāra sikkhāpucchā

pucchā – dvatti pariyāyai āvusau adhiṭṭhahitvā tatuttari adhiṭṭhahantassa pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – kausambiyaṃ bhantai āyasmantaṃ chan‍natthairaṃ ārabbha pañ‍ñattaṃ, āyasmā bhantai chandau katapariyausitaṃ vihāraṃ punappunaṃ chādāpaisi punappunaṃ laipāpaisi, atibhāritau vihārau paripati, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

2. bhūtagāmavagga, 10. sappāṇaka sikkhāpucchā

pucchā – jānaṃ  āvusau sappāṇakaṃ udakaṃ tiṇaṃvā mattikaṃvā siñ‍cantassa pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – āḷaviyaṃ bhantai āḷavikai bhikkhū ārabbha pañ‍ñattaṃ, āḷavikā bhantai bhikkhū jānaṃ sappāṇakaṃ udakaṃ tiṇampi mattikampi siñ‍ciṃsu, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

3. auvādavagga, 1. auvāda sikkhāpucchā

pucchā – asammataina  āvusau bhikkhuniyau auvadantassa pācittiyaṃ bhagavatā kattha pañ‍ñattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – sāvatthiyaṃ bhantai chabbaggiyai bhikkhū ārabbha pañ‍ñattaṃ, chabbaggiyā bhantai bhikkhū asammatā bhikkhuniyau auvadiṃsu, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

3. auvādavagga, 2. atthaṅgata sikkhāpucchā

pucchā – atthaṅgatai āvusau sūriyai bhikkhuniyau auvadantassa pācittiyaṃ bhagavatā kattha pañ‍ñattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – sāvatthiyaṃ bhantai āyasmantaṃ cūḷapanthakaṃ ārabbha pañ‍ñattaṃ, āyasmā bhantai cūḷapanthakau atthaṅgatai sūriyai bhikkhuniyau auvadi, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

3. auvādavagga, 3-4. bhikkhunupassaya, āmisa sikkhāpucchā

pucchā – bhikkhunupassayaṃ  āvusau upasaṅkamitvā bhikkhuniyau auvadantassaca āmisahaitu thairā bhikkhū bhikkhuniyau auvadantīti bhaṇantassaca pācittiyaṃ bhagavatā kattha pañ‍ñattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – bhikkhunupassayaṃ upasaṅkamitvā bhikkhuniyau auvadantassa pācittiyaṃ bhantai sak‍kaisu chabbaggiyai bhikkhū ārabbha pañ‍ñattaṃ, chabbaggiyā bhantai bhikkhū bhikkhunupassayaṃ upasaṅkamitvā bhikkhuniyau auvadiṃsu, āmisahaitu thairā bhikkhū bhikkhuniyau auvadantīti bhaṇantassa pācittiyaṃ sāvatthiyaṃ chabbaggiyai bhikkhū ārabbha pañ‍ñattaṃ, chabbaggiyā bhantai bhikkhū āmisahaitu thairā bhikkhū  bhikkhuniyau auvadantīti bhaṇiṃsu, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

3. auvādavagga, 5. cīvaradāna sikkhāpucchā

pucchā – añ‍ñātikāya  āvusau bhikkhuniyā cīvaraṃ daintassa pācittiyaṃ bhagavatā kattha pañ‍ñattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – sāvatthiyaṃ bhantai añ‍ñataraṃ bhikkhuṃ ārabbha pañ‍ñattaṃ, añ‍ñatarau bhantai bhikkhu añ‍ñātikāya bhikkhuniyā cīvaraṃ adāsi, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

3. auvādavagga, 6. cīvarasibbana sikkhāpucchā

pucchā – añ‍ñātikāya āvusau bhikkhuniyā cīvaraṃ sibbaintassa pācittiyaṃ bhagavatā kattha pañ‍ñattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – sāvatthiyaṃ bhantai āyasmantaṃ udāyiṃ ārabbha pañ‍ñattaṃ, āyasmā bhantai udāyī añ‍ñātikāya bhikkhuniyā cīvaraṃ sibbaisi, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

3. auvādavagga, 7. saṃvidhāna sikkhāpucchā

pucchā – bhikkhunayā  āvusau saddhiṃ saṃvidhāya aikaddhānamaggaṃ paṭipaj‍jantassa pācittiyaṃ bhagavatā kattha pañ‍ñattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – sāvatthiyaṃ bhantai chabbaggiyai bhikkhū ārabbha pañ‍ñattaṃ, chabbaggiyā bhantai bhikkhū bhikkhunīhi saddhiṃ saṃvidhāya aikaddhānamaggaṃ paṭipaj‍jiṃsu, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

3. auvādavagga, 8. nāvābhiruhana sikkhāpucchā

pucchā – bhikkhuniyā  āvusau saddhiṃ saṃvidhāya aikaṃ nāvaṃ abhiruhantassa pācittiyaṃ bhagavatā kattha pañ‍ñattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – sāvatthiyaṃ bhantai chabbaggiyai bhikkhū ārabbha pañ‍ñattaṃ, chabbaggiyā bhantai bhikkhū bhikkhūnīhi saddhiṃ saṃvidhāya aikaṃ nāvaṃ abhiruhiṃsu, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

3. auvādavagga, 9. paripācita sikkhāpucchā

pucchā – jānaṃ āvusau bhikkhuniparipācitaṃ piṇḍapātaṃ bhuñ‍jantassa pācittiyaṃ bhagavatā kattha pañ‍ñattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – rājagahai bhantai daivadattaṃ ārabbha pañ‍ñattaṃ, daivadattau bhantai jānaṃ bhikkhuniparipācitaṃ piṇḍapātaṃ bhuñ‍ji, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

3. auvādavagga, 10. rahaunisaj‍ja sikkhāpucchā

pucchā – bhikkhuniyā  saddhiṃ āvusau aikau aikāya rahau nisaj‍jaṃ kappaintassa pācittiyaṃ bhagavatā kattha pañ‍ñattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – sāvatthiyaṃ bhantai āyasmantaṃ udāyiṃ ārabbha pañ‍ñattaṃ, āyasmā bhantai udāyī bhikkhuniyā saddhiṃ aikau aikāya rahau nisaj‍jaṃ kappaisi, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

4. bhaujanavagga, 1. āvasathapiṇḍa sikkhāpucchā

pucchā – tatuttari  āvusau āvasathapiṇḍaṃ bhuñ‍jantassa pācittiyaṃ bhagavatā kattha pañ‍ñattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – sāvatthiyaṃ bhantai chabbaggiyai bhikkhū ārabbha pañ‍ñattaṃ, chabbaggiyā bhantai bhikkhū anuvasitvā anuvasitvā āvasathapiṇḍaṃ paribhuñ‍jiṃsu, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

4. bhaujanavagga, 2. gaṇabhaujana sikkhāpucchā

pucchā – gaṇabhaujanai pācittiyaṃ āvusau bhagavatā kattha pañ‍ñattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – rājagahai bhantai daivadattaṃ ārabbha pañ‍ñattaṃ, daivadattau bhantai saparisau kulaisu viñ‍ñāpaitvā bhuñ‍ji, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

4. bhaujanavagga 3. paramparabhaujana sikkhāpucchā

pucchā – parampara  bhaujanai pācittiyaṃ āvusau bhagavatā kattha pañ‍ñattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – vaisāliyaṃ bhantai sambahulai bhikkhū ārabbha pañ‍ñattaṃ, sambahulā bhantai bhikkhū añ‍ñatra nimantitā añ‍ñatra bhuñ‍jiṃsu, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

4. bhaujanavagga, 4. kāṇamātu sikkhāpucchā

pucchā – dvattipattapūraṃ  pūvaṃ paṭiggahaitvā tatuttari paṭiggaṇhantassa pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – sāvatthiyaṃ bhantai sambahulai bhikkhū ārabbha pañ‍ñattaṃ, sambahulā bhantai bhikkhū na mattaṃ jānitvā pūvaṃ paṭiggahaisuṃ, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

pucchā – kassa āvusau vacanaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – bhagavatau bhantai vacanaṃ arahatau sammāsambuddhassa|

pucchā – kaināvusau ābhataṃ|

vissaj‍janā – paramparāya bhantai ābhataṃ|

pucchā – atthi nukhau āvusau aittha kaucipi viraddhadausau|

vissaj‍janā – natthi bhantai|

4. bhaujanavagga, 5. paṭhamapavāraṇā sikkhāpucchā

pucchā – yaṃ  taina sāvusau…pai॰… sammāsambuddhaina bhuttāvinā parivātaina anatirittaṃ khādanīyaṃvā bhaujanīyaṃvā bhuñ‍jantassa pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – sāvatthiyaṃ bhantai sambahulai bhikkhū ārabbha pañ‍ñattaṃ, sambahulā bhantai bhikkhū bhuttāvī pavāritā añ‍ñatra bhuñ‍jiṃsu, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

4. bhaujanavagga, 6. dutiya pavāraṇā sikkhāpucchā

pucchā – bhikkhuṃ  āvusau bhuttāviṃ pavāritaṃ anatirittaina khādanīyaina bhaujanīyaina abhihaṭṭhuṃ pavāraintassa pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – sāvatthiyaṃ bhantai añ‍ñataraṃ bhikkhuṃ ārabbha pañ‍ñattaṃ, añ‍ñatarau bhantai bhikkhu bhikkhuṃ bhuttāviṃ pavāritaṃ anatirittaina bhaujanīyaina abhihaṭṭhuṃ pavāraisi, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

4. bhaujanavagga, 7. vikālabhaujana sikkhāpucchā

pucchā – vikālai āvusau khādanīyaṃ vā bhaujanīyaṃ vā bhuñ‍jantassa pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – rājagahai bhantai sattarasavaggiyai bhikkhū ārabbha pañ‍ñattaṃ, sattarasavaggiyā bhantai bhikkhū vikālai bhaujanaṃ bhuñ‍jiṃsu, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

4. bhaujanavagga, 8. san‍nidhikāraka sikkhāpucchā

pucchā – san‍nidhikārakaṃ āvusau khādanīyaṃ vā bhaujanīyaṃ vā bhuñ‍jantassa pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – sāvatthiyaṃ bhantai āyasmantaṃ bailaṭṭhasīsaṃ ārabbha pañ‍ñattaṃ, āyasmā bhantai bailaṭṭhasīsau san‍nidhikārakaṃ bhaujanaṃ bhuñ‍ji, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

4. bhaujanavagga, 9. paṇītabhaujana sikkhāpucchā

pucchā – paṇīta bhaujanāni āvusau attanau atthāya viñ‍ñāpaitvā bhuñ‍jantassa pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – sāvatthiyaṃ  bhantai chabbaggiyai bhikkhū ārabbha pañ‍ñattaṃ, chabbaggiyā bhantai bhikkhū paṇītabhaujanāni attanau atthāya viñ‍ñāpaitvā bhuñ‍jiṃsu, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

4. bhaujanavagga, 10. dantapauna sikkhāpucchā

pucchā – adin‍naṃ āvusau mukhadvāraṃ āhāraṃ āharantassa pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – vaisāliyaṃ bhantai añ‍ñataraṃ bhikkhuṃ ārabbha pañ‍ñattaṃ, añ‍ñatarau bhantai bhikkhu adin‍naṃ mukhadvāraṃ āhāraṃ āhari, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

5. acailakavagga, 1. acailaka sikkhāpucchā

pucchā – acailakassavā  āvusau paribbājakassavā paribbājikāyavā sahatthā khādanīyaṃvā bhaujanīyaṃvā daintassa pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – vaisāliyaṃ bhantai āyasmantaṃ ānandaṃ ārabbha pañ‍ñattaṃ, āyasmā bhantai ānandau añ‍ñatarissā paribbājikāya aikaṃ mañ‍ñamānau dvai pūvai adāsi, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

5. acailakavagga, 2. uyyaujana sikkhāpucchā

pucchā – bhikkhuṃ  aihāvusau gāmaṃvā nigamaṃvā piṇḍāya pavisissāmāti tassa dāpaitvāvā adāpaitvāvā uyyaujaintassa āvusau pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – sāvatthiyaṃ bhantai āyasmantaṃ upanandaṃ sakyaputtaṃ ārabbha pañ‍ñattaṃ, āyasmā bhantai upanandau sakyaputtau bhikkhu aihāvusau gāmaṃ piṇḍāya pavisissāmāti tassa adāpaitvā uyyaujaisi, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

5. acailakavagga, 3. sabhaujana sikkhāpucchā

pucchā – sabhaujanai kulai anupakhaj‍ja nisaj‍jaṃ kappaintassa āvusau pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – sāvatthiyaṃ  bhantai āyasmantaṃ upanandaṃ sakyaputtaṃ ārabbha pañ‍ñattaṃ, āyasmā bhantai upanandau sakyaputtau sabhaujanai kulai anupakhaj‍ja nisaj‍jaṃ kappaisi, tasmiṃ bhantaivatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

5. acailakavagga 4-5. rahaupaṭicchan‍na, rahaunisaj‍ja sikkhāpucchā

pucchā – mātugāmaina saddhiṃ rahau paṭicchan‍nai āsanai nisaj‍jaṃ kappaintassaca āvusau mātugāmaina saddhiṃ aikau aikāya rahau nisaj‍jaṃ kappaintassaca pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – sāvatthiyaṃ bhantai āyasmantaṃ upanandaṃ sakyaputtaṃ ārabbha pañ‍ñattaṃ, āyasmā bhantai upanandau sakyaputtau mātugāmaina saddhiṃ rahau paṭicchan‍nai āsanai nisaj‍jaṃ kappaisi, mātugāmaina saddhiṃ aikau aikāya rahau nisaj‍jaṃ kappaisi, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

5. acailakavagga, 6. cāritta sikkhāpucchā

pucchā – nimantitaina  āvusau sabhattaina santaṃ bhikkhuṃ anāpucchā puraibhattaṃvā pacchābhattaṃvā kulaisu cārittaṃ āpaj‍jantassa pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – rājagahai bhantai āyasmantaṃ upanandaṃ sakyaputtaṃ ārabbha pañ‍ñattaṃ, āyasmā bhantai upanandau sakyaputtau nimantitau sabhattau samānau puraibhattaṃ pacchābhattaṃ kulaisu cārittaṃ āpaj‍ji, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

5. acailakavagga, 7. mahānāma sikkhāpucchā

pucchā – tatuttari āvusau bhaisaj‍jaṃ viñ‍ñāpaintassa pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – sak‍kaisu  bhantai chabbaggiyai bhikkhū ārabbha pañ‍ñattaṃ, chabbaggiyā bhantai bhikkhū mahānāmaina sak‍kaina aj‍jaṇhau bhantai āgamaitāti vuc‍camānā nāgamaisuṃ, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

5. acailakavagga 8-9-10. uyyuttasainā casau sikkhāpucchā

pucchā – uyyuttaṃ āvusau sainaṃ dassanāya gacchantassa ca atiraikatirattaṃ saināya vasantassa ca uyyaudhikaṃ gacchantassa ca pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – sāvatthiyaṃ bhantai chabbaggiyai bhikkhū ārabbha pañ‍ñattaṃ, chabbaggiyā bhantai bhikkhū uyyuttaṃ sainaṃ dassanāya agamaṃsu, taiyaiva bhantai chabbaggiyā bhikkhū atiraikatirattaṃ saināya vasiṃsu, taiyaiva bhantai chabbaggiyā bhikkhū uyyaudhikaṃ agamaṃsu, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

6. surāpānavagga, 1. surāpāna sikkhāpucchā

pucchā – surāmairayapānai  āvusau pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – kausambiyaṃ bhantai āyasmantaṃ sāgataṃ ārabbha pañ‍ñattaṃ, āyasmā bhantai sāgatau maj‍jaṃ pivi, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

6. surāpānavagga, 2. aṅgulipataudaka sikkhāpucchā

pucchā – aṅgulipataudakai  āvusau pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – sāvatthiyaṃ bhantai chabbaggiyai bhikkhū ārabbha pañ‍ñattaṃ, chabbaggiyā bhantai bhikkhū bhikkhuṃ aṅgulipataudakaina hāsaisuṃ, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

6. surāpānavagga, 3. hasadhamma sikkhāpucchā

pucchā – udakai hasadhammai āvusau pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – sāvatthiyaṃ bhantai sattarasavaggiyai bhikkhū ārabbha pañ‍ñattaṃ, sattarasavaggiyā bhantai bhikkhū aciravatiyā nadiyā udakai kīḷiṃsu, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

6. surāpānavagga, 4. anādariya sikkhāpucchā

pucchā – anādariya  āvusau pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – kausambiyaṃ bhantai āyasmantaṃ chan‍naṃ ārabbha pañ‍ñattaṃ, āyasmā bhantai chan‍nau āpattiyā anuyuñ‍jiyamānau anādariyaṃ akāsi, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

6. surāpānavagga, 5. bhiṃsāpana sikkhāpucchā

pucchā – bhikkhuṃ  bhiṃsāpaintassa āvusau pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – sāvatthiyaṃ bhantai chabbaggiyai bhikkhū ārabbha pañ‍ñattaṃ, chabbaggiyā bhantai bhikkhū bhikkhuṃ bhiṃsāpaisuṃ, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

6. surāpānavagga, 6. jautika sikkhāpucchā

pucchā – jautiṃ āvusau samādahitvā visibbaintassa pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – bhaggaisu bhantai sambahulai bhikkhū ārabbha pañ‍ñattaṃ, sambahulā bhantai bhikkhū jautiṃ samādapaitvā visibbaisuṃ, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

6. surāpānavagga, 7. nahāna sikkhāpucchā

pucchā – aurainaddhamāsaṃ  āvusau nahāyantassa pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – rājagahai bhantai sambahulai bhikkhū ārabbha pañ‍ñattaṃ, sambahulā bhantai bhikkhū rājānampi passitvā na mattaṃ jānitvā nahāyiṃsu, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

pucchā – kassa  āvusau vacanaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – bhagavatau bhantai vacanaṃ arahatau sammāsambuddhassa|

pucchā – kaināvusau ābhataṃ|

vissaj‍janā – paramparāya bhantai ābhataṃ|

pucchā – atthi nukhau āvusau aittha kaucipi viruddhadausau|

vissaj‍janā – natthi bhantai|

suṇātu mai bhantai saṅghau…pai॰… saṃgāyaiyyāma|

pucchā – yaṃ  taina āvusau…pai॰… sammāsambuddhaina anādiyitvā tiṇṇaṃ dubbaṇṇakaraṇānaṃ añ‍ñataraṃ dubbaṇṇakaraṇaṃ navaṃ cīvaraṃ paribhuñ‍jantassa pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – sāvatthiyaṃ bhantai sambahulai bhikkhū ārabbha pañ‍ñattaṃ, sambahulā bhantai bhikkhū attanau cīvaraṃ na sañ‍jāniṃsu, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

6. surāpānavagga, 10. cīvaraapanidhāna sikkhāpucchā

pucchā – bhikkhussa āvusau pattaṃ vā cīvaraṃ vā nisīdanaṃ vā sūcigharaṃ vā kāyabandhanaṃ vā apanidhaintassa pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – sāvatthiyaṃ  bhantai chabbaggiyai bhikkhū ārabbha pañ‍ñattaṃ, chabbaggiyā bhantai bhikkhū bhikkhūnaṃ pattampi cīvarampi apanidhaisuṃ, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

7. sappāṇakavagga, 1. sañ‍cic‍ca sikkhāpucchā

pucchā – sañ‍cic‍ca āvusau pāṇaṃ jīvitā vauraupaintassa pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – sāvatthiyaṃ bhantai āyasmantaṃ udāyiṃ ārabbha pañ‍ñattaṃ āyasmā bhantai udāyī sañ‍cic‍ca pāṇaṃ jīvitā vauraupaisi, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

7. sappāṇakavagga, 2. sappāṇaka sikkhāpucchā

pucchā – jānaṃ  āvusau sappāṇakaṃ udakaṃ paribhuñ‍jantassa pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – sāvatthiyaṃ bhantai chabbaggiyai bhikkhū ārabbha pañ‍ñattaṃ, chabbaggiyā bhantai bhikkhū jānaṃ sappāṇakaṃ udakaṃ paribhuñ‍jiṃsu, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

7. sappāṇakavagga, 6. thaiyyasattha sikkhāpucchā

pucchā – jānaṃ āvusau thaiyyasatthaina saddhiṃ saṃvidhāya aikaddhānamaggaṃ paṭipaj‍jantassa pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – sāvatthiyaṃ bhantai añ‍ñataraṃ bhikkhuṃ ārabbha pañ‍ñattaṃ, añ‍ñatarau bhantai bhikkhu jānaṃ thaiyyasatthaina saddhiṃ saṃvidhāya aikaddhānamaggaṃ paṭipaj‍ji, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

7. sappāṇakavagga, 7. saṃvidhāna sikkhāpucchā

pucchā – mātugāmaina  saddhiṃ āvusau saṃvidhāya aikaddhānamaggaṃ paṭipaj‍jantassa pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – sāvatthiyaṃ bhantai añ‍ñataraṃ bhikkhuṃ ārabbha pañ‍ñattaṃ, añ‍ñatarau bhantai bhikkhu mātugāmaina saddhiṃ saṃvidhāya aikaddhānamaggaṃ paṭipaj‍ji, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

8. sahadhammikavagga, 1. sahadhammika sikkhāpucchā

pucchā – bhikkhūhi āvusau sahadhammikaṃ vuc‍camānaina na tāvāhaṃ āvusau aitasmiṃ sikkhāpadai sikkhissāmi, yāva na añ‍ñaṃ bhikkhuṃ byattaṃ vinayadharaṃ paripucchissāmīti bhaṇantassa pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – kausambiyaṃ  bhantai āyasmantaṃ chan‍naṃ ārabbha pañ‍ñattaṃ, āyasmā bhantai chan‍nau bhikkhūhi sahadhammikaṃ vuc‍camānau na tāvāhaṃ āvusau aitasmiṃ sikkhāpadai sikkhissāmi, yāva na añ‍ñaṃ bhikkhuṃ byattaṃ vinayadharaṃ paripucchissāmīti bhaṇi, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

8. sahadhammikavagga, 2. vilaikhana sikkhāpucchā

pucchā – vinayaṃ vivaṇṇaintassa āvusau pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – sāvatthiyaṃ bhantai chabbaggiyai bhikkhū ārabbha pañ‍ñattaṃ, chabbaggiyā bhantai bhikkhū vinayaṃ vivaṇṇaisuṃ, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

8. sahadhammikavagga, 3. mauhana sikkhāpucchā

pucchā – mauhanakai  āvusau pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – sāvatthiyaṃ bhantai chabbaggiyai bhikkhū ārabbha pañ‍ñattaṃ, chabbaggiyā bhantai bhikkhū mauhaisuṃ, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

pucchā – bhikkhussa  āvusau kupitaina anattamanaina pahāraṃ daintassa ca talasattikaṃ uggirantassa ca pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – sāvatthiyaṃ bhantai chabbaggiyai bhikkhū ārabbha pañ‍ñattaṃ, chabbaggiyā bhantai bhikkhū kupitā anattamanā sattarasavaggiyānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ pahāraṃ adaṃsu, talasattikañ‍ca uggiriṃsu, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

8. sahadhammikavagga, 7. sañ‍cic‍ca sikkhāpucchā

pucchā – bhikkhussa āvusau sañ‍cic‍ca kuk‍kuc‍caṃ upadahantassa pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – sāvatthiyaṃ bhantai chabbaggiyai bhikkhū ārabbha pañ‍ñattaṃ, chabbaggiyā bhantai bhikkhū bhikkhūnaṃ sañ‍cic‍ca kuk‍kuc‍caṃ upadahiṃsu, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

8. sahadhammikavagga, 8. upassuti sikkhāpucchā

pucchā – bhikkhūnaṃ  āvusau bhaṇḍanajātānaṃ kalahajātānaṃ vivādā pan‍nānaṃ upassutiṃ tiṭṭhantassa pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – sāvatthiyaṃ bhantai chabbaggiyai bhikkhū ārabbha pañ‍ñattaṃ, chabbaggiyā bhantai bhikkhū bhikkhūnaṃ bhaṇḍanajātānaṃ kalaha jātānaṃ vivādāpan‍nānaṃ upassutiṃ tiṭṭhahiṃsu, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

8. sahadhammikavagga, 9. kammapaṭibāhana sikkhāpucchā
