pucchā – dhammikānaṃ āvusau kammānaṃ chandaṃ datvā pacchā khīyanadhammaṃ āpaj‍jantassa pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – sāvatthiyaṃ  bhantai chabbaggiyai bhikkhū ārabbha pañ‍ñattaṃ, chabbaggiyā bhantai bhikkhū dhammikānaṃ kammānaṃ chandaṃ datvā pacchā khīyanadhammaṃ āpaj‍jiṃsu, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

8. sahadhammikavagga 10. chandaṃ adatvāgamana sikkhāpucchā

pucchā – saṅghai āvusau vinicchakathāya vattamānāya chandaṃ adatvā uṭṭhāyāsanā pak‍kamantassa pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – sāvatthiyaṃ bhantai añ‍ñataraṃ bhikkhuṃ ārabbha pañ‍ñattaṃ, añ‍ñatarau bhantai chabbaggiyau bhikkhu saṅghai vinicchayakathāya vattamānāya chandaṃ adatvā uṭṭhāyāsanā pak‍kāmi, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

8. sahadhammikavagga, 12. pariṇāmana sikkhāpucchā

pucchā – jānaṃ  āvusau saṅghikaṃ lābhaṃ pariṇataṃ puggalassa pariṇāmaintassa pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – sāvatthiyaṃ bhantai chabbaggiyai bhikkhū ārabbha pañ‍ñattaṃ, chabbaggiyā bhantai bhikkhū jānaṃ saṅghikaṃ lābhaṃ pariṇataṃ puggalassa pariṇāmaisuṃ, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

9. ratanavagga, 1. antaipura sikkhāpucchā

pucchā – pubbai appaṭisaṃviditaina āvusau rañ‍ñau antaipuraṃ pavisantassa pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – sāvatthiyaṃ  bhantai āyasmantaṃ ānandaṃ ārabbha pañ‍ñattaṃ, āyasmā bhantai ānandau pubbai appaṭisaṃviditau rañ‍ñau antaipuraṃ pāvisi, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

9. ratanavagga, 2. ratana sikkhāpucchā

pucchā – ratanaṃ āvusau uggaṇhantassa pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – sāvatthiyaṃ bhantai añ‍ñataraṃ bhikkhuṃ ārabbha pañ‍ñattaṃ, añ‍ñatarau bhantai bhikkhu ratanaṃ uggahaisi, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

9. ratanavagga, 3. vikālagāmappavisana sikkhāpucchā

pucchā – santaṃ  āvusau bhikkhuṃ anāpucchā vikālai gāmaṃ pavisantassa pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – sāvatthiyaṃ bhantai chabbaggiyai bhikkhū ārabbha pañ‍ñattaṃ, chabbaggiyā bhantai bhikkhū santaṃ bhikkhuṃ anāpucchā vikālai gāmaṃ pavisiṃsu, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

pāṭidaisanīya

3. tatiyapāṭidaisanī sikkhāpucchā

pucchā – saikkhasammataisu  āvusau kulaisu khādanīyaṃ vā bhaujanīyaṃ vā sahatthā paṭiggahaitvā bhuñ‍jantassa pāṭidaisanīyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – sāvatthiyaṃ bhantai sambahulai bhikkhū ārabbha pañ‍ñattaṃ, sambahulā bhantai bhikkhū namattaṃ jānitvā paṭiggahaisuṃ, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

saikhiyakaṇḍa

pucchā – anādariyaṃ  āvusau paṭic‍ca puratau vā pacchatau vā aulambaintaina nivāsaintassaca pārupantassaca duk‍kaṭaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – sāvatthiyaṃ bhantai chabbaggiyai bhikkhū ārabbha pañ‍ñattaṃ, chabbaggiyā bhantai bhikkhū puratau ca pacchatau ca aulambantā nivāsaisuṃcaiva pārupiṃsuca, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

pucchā – anādariyaṃ āvusau paṭic‍ca kāyaṃ vivaritvā antaragharai gacchantassa ca nisīdantassa ca duk‍kaṭaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – sāvatthiyaṃ bhantai chabbaggīyai bhikkhū ārabbha pañ‍ñattaṃ, chabbaggiyā bhantai bhikkhū kāyaṃ vivaritvā antaragharai gacchiṃsu caiva nisīdiṃsuca, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

pucchā – anādariyaṃ  āvusau paṭic‍ca hatthaṃ vā pādaṃ vā kīḷāpaintaina antaragharai gacchantassa ca nisīdantassa ca, tahaṃ tahaṃ aulaukaintaina antaragharai gacchantassa ca nisīdantassa ca, ukkhittakāya antaragharai gacchantassa ca nisīdantassa ca duk‍kaṭaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – sāvatthiyaṃ bhantai chabbaggiyai bhikkhū ārabbha pañ‍ñattaṃ, chabbaggiyā bhantai bhikkhū hatthampi pādampi kīḷāpaintā tahaṃ tahaṃpi aulaukaintā antaragharai gacchiṃsucaiva nisīdiṃsu ca, taiyaiva bhantai chabbaggiyā bhikkhū ukkhittakāya antaragharai gacchiṃsucaiva nisīdiṃsuca, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

pucchā – anādariyaṃ  āvusau paṭic‍ca uj‍jagghikāya vā uc‍cāsaddaṃ mahāsaddaṃ karauntaina vā antaragharai gacchantassaca nisīdantassaca duk‍kaṭaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – sāvatthiyaṃ bhantai chabbaggiyai bhikkhū ārabbha pañ‍ñattaṃ, chabbaggiyā bhantai bhikkhū mahāhasitaṃ hasantā ca uc‍cāsaddaṃ mahāsaddaṃ karauntā ca antaragharai gacchiṃsucaiva nisīdiṃsuca, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

pucchā – anādariyaṃ āvusau paṭic‍ca kāyappacālakaṃ vā bāhuppa cālakaṃ vā sīsappacālakaṃ vā khambhakataina vā auguṇṭhitaina vā antaragharai gacchantassa ca nisīdantassa ca uk‍kuṭikāya kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – sāvatthiyaṃ bhantai chabbaggiyai bhikkhū ārabbha pañ‍ñattaṃ, chabbaggiyā bhantai bhikkhū kāyappacālakaṃ bāhuppacālakaṃ sīsappacālakaṃ karauntā ca khambhīkatā ca auguṇṭhitā ca antaragharai gacchiṃsucaiva nisīdiṃsuca, uk‍kuṭikāyaca antaragharai gacchiṃsu, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

pucchā – anādariyaṃ  āvusau paṭic‍ca pal‍latthikāya antaragharai nisin‍nassa duk‍kaṭaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – sāvatthiyaṃ bhantai chabbaggiyai bhikkhū ārabbha pañ‍ñattaṃ, chabbaggiyā bhantai bhikkhū pal‍latthikāya antaragharai nisīdiṃsu, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

pucchā – anādariyaṃ  āvusau paṭic‍ca asak‍kac‍caṃ piṇḍapātaṃ paṭiggaṇhantassa ca tahaṃ tahaṃ aulaukaintaina piṇḍapātaṃ paṭiggaṇhantassaca sūpañ‍ñaiva bahuṃ paṭiggaṇhantassaca thūpīkataṃ piṇḍapātaṃ paṭiggaṇhantassaca duk‍kaṭaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – sāvatthiyaṃ bhantai chabbaggiyai bhikkhū ārabbha pañ‍ñattaṃ, chabbaggiyā bhantai bhikkhū asak‍kac‍caṃ piṇḍapātaṃ paṭiggahaisuṃ, tahaṃ tahaṃ aulaukaintā piṇḍapātaṃ paṭiggahaisuṃ, sūpañ‍ñaiva bahuṃ paṭiggahaisuṃ, thūpīkataṃ piṇḍapātaṃ paṭiggahaisuṃ, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

pucchā – anādariyaṃ  āvusau paṭic‍ca āsak‍kac‍caṃ piṇḍapātaṃ bhuñ‍jantassaca tahaṃ tahaṃ aulaukaintana piṇḍapātaṃ bhuñ‍jantassa ca tahaṃ tahaṃ aumasitvā piṇḍapātaṃ bhuñ‍jantassa ca sūpañ‍ñaiva bahuṃ bhuñ‍jantassa ca thūpakatau aumadditvā piṇḍapātaṃ bhuñ‍jantassa ca sūpaṃ vā byañ‍janaṃ vā audanaina paṭicchādaintassa ca duk‍kaṭaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – sāvatthiyaṃ bhantai chabbaggiyai bhikkhū ārabbha pañ‍ñattaṃ, chabbaggiyā bhantai bhikkhū asak‍kac‍caṃ piṇḍapātaṃ bhuñ‍jiṃsu, tahaṃ tahaṃ aulaukaintā piṇḍapātaṃ bhuñ‍jiṃsu, taiyaiva bhantai chabbaggiyā bhikkhū tahaṃ tahaṃ aumasitvā piṇḍapātaṃ bhuñ‍jiṃsu, taiyaiva bhantai chabbaggiyā bhikkhū bahuṃ sūpaṃ bhuñ‍jiṃsu, taiyaiva bhantai chabbaggiyā bhikkhū thūpakatau aumadditvā piṇḍapātaṃ bhuñ‍jiṃsu, taiyaiva bhantai chabbaggiyā bhikkhū sūpampi byañ‍janampi audanaina paṭicchādaisuṃ, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

pucchā – anādariyaṃ  āvusau paṭic‍ca sūpaṃvā audanaṃvā agilānaina attanau atthāya viñ‍ñāpaitvā bhuñ‍jantassa duk‍kaṭaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – sāvatthiyaṃ bhantai chabbaggiyai bhikkhū ārabbha pañ‍ñattaṃ, chabbaggiyā bhantai bhikkhū sūpampi audanampi attanau atthāya viñ‍ñāpaitvā bhuñ‍jiṃsu, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

pucchā – anādariyaṃ āvusau paṭic‍ca ujjhānasañ‍ñinā paraisaṃ pattaṃ aulaukaintassa ca atimahantaṃ kabaḷaṃ karauntassa ca dīghaṃ ālaupaṃ karauntassa ca duk‍kaṭaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – sāvatthiyaṃ  bhantai chabbaggiyai bhikkhū ārabbha pañ‍ñattaṃ, chabbaggiyā bhantai bhikkhū ujjhānasañ‍ñinau paraisaṃ pattaṃ aulaukaisuṃ, taiyaiva bhantai chabbaggiyā bhikkhū atimahantaṃ kabaḷaṃ kariṃsu, taiyaiva bhantai chabbaggiyā bhikkhū dīghaṃ ālaupaṃ kariṃsu, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

pucchā – anādariyaṃ āvusau paṭic‍ca anāhaṭai kabaḷai mukhadvāraṃ vivarantassa ca bhuñ‍jamānai sabbaṃ hatthaṃ mukhai pakkhipantassa ca sakabaḷaina mukhaina byāharantassa ca piṇḍukkhaipakaṃ piṇḍapātaṃ bhuñ‍jantassa ca kabaḷāvacchaidakaṃ piṇḍapātaṃ bhuñ‍jantassa ca avagaṇḍakārakaṃ piṇḍapātaṃ bhuñ‍jantassa ca hatthaniddhunakaṃ sitthāvakārakaṃ jivhānicchārakaṃ capucapukārakaṃ piṇḍapātaṃ bhuñ‍jantassa ca duk‍kaṭaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – sāvatthiyaṃ bhantai chabbaggiyai bhikkhū ārabbha pañ‍ñattaṃ, chabbaggiyā bhantai bhikkhū anāhaṭai kabaḷai mukhadvāraṃ vivariṃsu, bhuñ‍jamānā sabbaṃ hatthaṃ mukhai pakkhipiṃsu, sakabaḷaina mukhaina byāhariṃsu, piṇḍukkhaipakaṃ bhuñ‍jiṃsu, kabaḷāvacchaidakaṃ bhuñ‍jiṃsu, avagaṇḍakārakaṃ bhuñ‍jiṃsu, hatthaniddhunakaṃ sitthāvakārakaṃ jivhānicchārakaṃ  capucapukārakaṃ bhuñ‍jiṃsu, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

pucchā – anādariyaṃ āvusau paṭic‍ca surusurukārakaṃ bhuñ‍jantassaca hatthanil‍laihakaṃvā pattanil‍laihakaṃ vā auṭṭhanil‍laihakaṃ vā bhuñ‍jantassa ca sāmisaina hatthaina pānīyathālakaṃ paṭiggaṇhantassa ca sasitthakaṃ pattadhauvanaṃ antaragharai caḍḍaintassa ca duk‍kaṭaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – suru surukārakaṃ bhantai bhuñ‍jantassa duk‍kaṭaṃ kausambiyaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ, sambahulai bhantai bhikkhū ārabbha pañ‍ñattaṃ, sambahulā bhantai bhikkhū surusurukārakaṃ khīraṃ piviṃsu, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ| hatthanil‍laihakaṃ  bhuñ‍jantassa ca pattanil‍laihakaṃ bhuñ‍jantassa ca auṭṭhanil‍laihakaṃ bhuñ‍jantassa duk‍kaṭaṃ sāvatthiyaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ, chabbaggiyai bhantai bhikkhū ārabbha pañ‍ñattaṃ, chabbaggiyā bhantai bhikkhū hatthanil‍laihakaṃ bhuñ‍jiṃsu, pattanil‍laihakaṃ bhuñ‍jiṃsu, auṭṭhanil‍laihakaṃ bhuñ‍jiṃsu, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

sāmisaina hatthaina pānīyathālakaṃ paṭiggaṇhantassa ca sasitthakaṃ pattadhauvanaṃ antaragharai chaḍḍaintassa ca duk‍kaṭaṃ bhaggaisu pañ‍ñattaṃ, sambahulai bhikkhū ārabbha pañ‍ñattaṃ, sambahulā bhikkhū sāmisaina hatthaina pānīyathālakaṃ paṭiggahaisuṃ, taiyaiva bhantai sambahulā bhikkhū sasitthakaṃ pattadhauvanaṃ antaragharai chaḍḍaisuṃ, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

pucchā – anādariyaṃ  āvusau paṭic‍ca chattapāṇissa dhammaṃ daisaintassa ca daṇḍapāṇissa vā satthapāṇissa vā āvudhapāṇissa vā dhammaṃ daisaintassa ca duk‍kaṭaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – sāvatthiyaṃ bhantai chabbaggiyai bhikkhū ārabbha pañ‍ñattaṃ, chabbaggiyā bhantai bhikkhū chattapāṇissa daṇḍapāṇissa satthapāṇissa āvudhapāṇissa dhammaṃ daisaisuṃ, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

pucchā – anādariyaṃ āvusau paṭic‍ca pādukāruḷhassa ca upahanāruḷhassa ca yānagatassa ca sayanagatassa ca dhammaṃ daisaintassa duk‍kaṭaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – sāvatthiyaṃ bhantai chabbaggiyai bhikkhū ārabbha pañ‍ñattaṃ, chabbaggiyā bhantai bhikkhū pādukāruḷhassa upahanāruḷhassa yānagatassa sayanagatassa dhammaṃ daisaisuṃ, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

pucchā – anādariyaṃ  āvusau paṭic‍ca pal‍latthikāya nisin‍nassa ca vaiṭhitasīsassa ca auguṇṭhitasīsassa ca chamāyaṃ nisīditvā āsanai nisin‍nassa ca nīcai āsanai nisīditvā uc‍cai āsanai nisin‍nassa ca ṭhitaina nisin‍nassa ca pacchatau gacchantaina puratau gacchantassa ca uppathaina gacchantaina pathaina gacchantassa ca dhammaṃ daisaintassa duk‍kaṭaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – sāvatthiyaṃ bhantai chabbaggiyaiva bhikkhū ārabbha pañ‍ñattaṃ, chabbaggiyā bhantai bhikkhū pal‍latthikāya nisin‍nassa ca vaiṭhitasīsassa ca auguṇṭhitasīsassa ca chamāyaṃ nisīditvā āsanai nisin‍nassa ca nīcai āsanai nisīditvā uc‍cai āsanai nisin‍nassa ca ṭhitānisin‍nassa ca pacchatau gacchantā puratau gacchantassa ca uppathaina gacchantā pathaina gacchantassa ca dhammaṃ daisaisuṃ, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

pucchā – anādariyaṃ  āvusau paṭic‍ca ṭhitaina uc‍cāraṃ vā passāvaṃ vā karauntassa ca haritai vā udakai vā uc‍cāraṃ vā passāvaṃ vā khaiḷaṃ vā karauntassa ca duk‍kaṭaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – sāvatthiyaṃ bhantai chabbaggiyaiva bhikkhū ārabbha pañ‍ñattaṃ, chabbaggiyā bhantai bhikkhū ṭhitā uc‍cārampi passāvampi akaṃsu, taiyaiva bhantai chabbaggiyā bhikkhū haritaipi udakaipi uc‍cārampi passāvampi khaiḷampi akaṃsu, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

bhikkhunīvibhaṅga

5. pañ‍cama pārājika sikkhāpucchā

pucchā – bhikkhunīnaṃ  āvusau pañ‍cama pārājikaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – sāvatthiyaṃ bhantai sundarīnandaṃ bhikkhuniṃ ārabbha pañ‍ñattaṃ, sundarīnandā bhantai bhikkhunī avassutā avassutassa purisapuggalassa kāyasaṃsaggaṃ sādiyi, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

bhikkhunīvibhaṅga, saṅghādisaisa sikkhāpucchā

pucchā – ussayavādikāya āvusau bhikkhuniyā aḍḍaṃ karauntiyā saṅghādisaisau kattha pañ‍ñattau, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattau|

vissaj‍janā – sāvatthiyaṃ bhantai tul‍lanandaṃ bhikkhuniṃ ārabbha pañ‍ñattau, thul‍lanandā bhantai bhikkhunī ussayavādikā vihari, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattau|

pucchā – cauriṃ  āvusau vuṭṭhāpaintiyā saṅghādisaisau kattha pañ‍ñattau, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattau|

vissaj‍janā – sāvatthiyaṃ bhantai thul‍lanandaṃ bhikkhuniṃ ārabbha pañ‍ñattau, thul‍lanandā bhantai bhikkhunī cauriṃ vuṭṭhāpaisi, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattau|

pucchā – aikāya  āvusau gāmantaraṃ gacchantiyā saṅghādisaisau kattha pañ‍ñattau, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattau|

vissaj‍janā – sāvatthiyaṃ bhantai añ‍ñataraṃ bhikkhuniṃ ārabbha pañ‍ñattau, añ‍ñatarā bhantai bhikkhunī aikā gāmantaraṃ gacchi, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattau|

pucchā – avassutāya āvusau bhikkhuniyā avassutassa purisapuggalassa hatthatau khādanīyaṃvā bhaujanīyaṃvā sahatthā paṭiggahaitvā bhuñ‍jantassa saṅghādisaisau kattha pañ‍ñattau, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattau|

vissaj‍janā – sāvatthiyaṃ bhantai sundarīnandaṃ bhikkhuniṃ ārabbha pañ‍ñattau, sundarīnandā bhantai bhikkhunī avassutā avassutassa purisapuggalassa  hatthatau āmisaṃ paṭiggahaisi tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattau|

pucchā – kupitāya āvusau anattamanāya bhikkhuniyā yāvatatiyaṃ samanubhāsantiyā na paṭinisaj‍jantiyā saṅghādisaisau kattha pañ‍ñattau, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattau|

vissaj‍janā – sāvatthiyaṃ bhantai caṇḍakāḷiṃ bhikkhuniṃ ārabbha pañ‍ñattau, caṇḍakāḷī bhantai bhikkhunī kupitā anattamanā aivaṃ abhaṇi, buddhaṃ pac‍cācikkhāmi, dhammaṃ pac‍cācikkhāmi, saṅghaṃ pac‍cācikkhāmi, sikkhaṃ pac‍cācikkhāmi, kin‍numāva samaṇiyau yā samaṇiyau sakyadhītarau, santañ‍ñāpi samaṇiyau laj‍jiniyau kuk‍kuc‍cikā sikkhākāmā, tāsāhaṃ santikai brahmacariyaṃ carissāmīti, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattau|

pucchā – kassa  āvusau vacanaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – bhagavatau bhantai vacanaṃ arahatau sammāsambuddhassa|

pucchā – kaināvusau ābhataṃ|

vissaj‍janā – paramparāya bhantai ābhataṃ|

pucchā – atthi nukhau āvusau aittha kaucipi viraddhadausau|

vissaj‍janā – natthi bhantai|

bhikkhunī vibhaṅgapācittiya

1. lasuṇavagga, 1. paṭhama sikkhāpucchā

pucchā – yaṃ  taina āvusau bhagavatā…pai॰… sammāsambuddhaina lakkhaṇaṃ khādantiyā pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – sāvatthiyaṃ bhantai thul‍lanandaṃ bhikkhuniṃ ārabbha pañ‍ñattaṃ, thul‍lanandā bhantai bhikkhunī na mattaṃ jānitvā bahuṃ lasuṇaṃ harāpaisi, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

1. lasuṇavagga, 6. chaṭṭha sikkhāpucchā

pucchā – bhikkhussa āvusau bhuñ‍jamānassa an‍naina vā pānaina vā vidhūpanaina vā upatiṭṭhantiyā bhikkhuniyā pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – sāvatthiyaṃ  bhantai añ‍ñataraṃ bhikkhuniṃ ārabbha pañ‍ñattaṃ, añ‍ñatarā bhantai bhikkhunī bhikkhussa bhuñ‍jamānassa pānīyaina vidhūpanaina ca upatiṭṭhati, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

1. lasuṇavagga, 8. aṭṭhama sikkhāpucchā

pucchā – uc‍cāraṃvā āvusau passāvaṃvā saṅkāraṃvā vighāsaṃvā thiraukuṭṭai chaḍḍaintiyā pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – sāvatthiyaṃ bhantai añ‍ñataraṃ bhikkhuniṃ ārabbha pañ‍ñattaṃ, añ‍ñatarā bhantai bhikkhunī uc‍cāraṃ tiraukuṭṭai chaḍḍaisi, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

1. lasuṇavagga, 10. dasama sikkhāpucchā

pucchā – nac‍caṃ  vā āvusau gītaṃ vā vāditaṃ vā dassanāya gacchantiyā pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – sāvatthiyaṃ bhantai chabbaggiyā bhikkhuniyau ārabbha pañ‍ñattaṃ, chabbaggiyā bhantai bhikkhuniyau nac‍campi gītampi vāditampi dassanāya agamaṃsu, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

pucchā – bhikkhupana  āvusau nac‍caṃ vā gītaṃ vā vāditaṃ vā dassanāya gacchantau kaṃ nāma āpattiṃ āpaj‍jati|

vissaj‍janā – duk‍kaṭaṃ bhantai āpattiṃ āpaj‍jati|

1. paṭhama sikkhāpucchā

pucchā – rattandhakārai āvusau apadīpai purisaina saddhiṃ aikainaikāya santiṭṭhantiyā pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – sāvatthiyaṃ bhantai añ‍ñataraṃ bhikkhuniṃ ārabbha pañ‍ñattaṃ, añ‍ñatarā bhantai bhikkhunī rattandhakārai apadīpai purisaina saddhiṃ aikainaikā santiṭṭhi, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

2. andhakāravagga, 2-3. dutiya, tatiya sikkhāpucchā

pucchā – paṭicchan‍nai  vā āvusau aukāsai ajjhaukāsai vā purisaina saddhiṃ aikainaikāya santiṭṭhantiyā pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – sāvatthiyaṃ bhantai añ‍ñataraṃ bhikkhuniṃ ārabbha pañ‍ñattaṃ, añ‍ñatarā bhantai bhikkhunī paṭicchan‍nai aukāsai ajjhaukāsai ca purisaina saddhiṃ aikainaikā santiṭṭhi, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

2. andhakāravagga, 8. aṭṭhama sikkhāpucchā

pucchā – duggahitaina āvusau dūpadhāritaina paraṃ ujjhāpaintiyā pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – sāvatthiyaṃ bhantai añ‍ñataraṃ bhikkhuniṃ ārabbha pañ‍ñattaṃ, añ‍ñatarā bhantai bhikkhunī duggahitaina dūpadhāritaina paraṃ ujjhāpaisi, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

2. andhakāravagga, 9. navama sikkhāpucchā

pucchā – attānaṃ  vā āvusau paraṃ vā nirayaina vā brahmacariyaina vā abhisapantiyā pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – sāvatthiyaṃ bhantai caṇḍakāḷiṃ bhikkhuniṃ ārabbha pañ‍ñattaṃ, caṇḍakāḷī bhantai bhikkhunī attānampi parampi nirayainapi brahmacariyainapi abhisapi, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

2. andhakāravagga, 10. dasama sikkhāpucchā

pucchā – attānaṃ  āvusau vadhitvā vadhitvā raudantiyā pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – sāvatthiyaṃ bhantai caṇḍakāḷiṃ bhikkhuniṃ ārabbha pañ‍ñattaṃ, caṇḍakāḷī bhantai bhikkhunī bhikkhunīhi saddhiṃ bhaṇḍitvā attānaṃ vadhitvā vadhitvā raudi, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

3. naggavagga, 3. tatiya sikkhāpucchā

pucchā – bhikkhuniyā  āvusau cīvaraṃ visibbaitvā vā visibbāpaitvā vā naiva sibbaintiyā na sibbāpanāya ussuk‍kaṃ karauntiyā pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – sāvatthiyaṃ bhantai thul‍lanandaṃ bhikkhuniṃ ārabbha pañ‍ñattaṃ, thul‍lanandā bhantai bhikkhunī bhikkhuniyā cīvaraṃ visibbāpaitvā naiva sibbaisi na sibbāpanāya ussuk‍kaṃ akāsi, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

3. naggavagga, 8. aṭṭhama sikkhāpucchā

pucchā – agārikassa  vā āvusau paribbājakassa vā paribbājikāya vā samaṇacīvaraṃ daintiyā pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – sāvatthiyaṃ bhantai thul‍lanandaṃ bhikkhuniṃ ārabbha pañ‍ñattaṃ, thul‍lanandā bhantai bhikkhunī agārikassa samaṇacīvaraṃ adāsi, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

4. tuvaṭṭavagga, 1-2. paṭhama, dutiya sikkhāpucchā

pucchā – dvin‍naṃ āvusau bhikkhunīnaṃ aikamañ‍cai vā aikattharaṇapāvuraṇai vā tuvaṭṭaintīnaṃ pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – sāvatthiyaṃ bhantai sambahulā bhikkhuniyau ārabbha pañ‍ñattaṃ, sambahulā bhantai bhikkhuniyau dvai aikamañ‍cai tuvaṭṭaisuṃ, aikattharaṇapāvuraṇai tuvaṭṭaisuṃ, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

4. tuvaṭṭavagga, 3. tatiya sikkhāpucchā

pucchā – bhikkhuniyā  āvusau sañ‍cic‍ca aphāsuṃ karauntiyā pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – sāvatthiyaṃ bhantai thul‍lanandaṃ bhikkhuniṃ ārabbha pañ‍ñattaṃ, thul‍lanandā bhantai bhikkhunī bhikkhuniyā sañ‍cic‍ca aphāsuṃ akāsi, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

4. tuvaṭṭavagga, 4. catuttha sikkhāpucchā

pucchā – dukkhitaṃ  āvusau sahajīviniṃ naiva upaṭṭhaintiyā na upaṭṭhāpanāya ussuk‍kaṃ karauntiyā pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – sāvatthiyaṃ bhantai thul‍lanandaṃyaiva bhikkhuniṃ ārabbha pañ‍ñattaṃ, thul‍lanandā bhantai bhikkhunī dukkhitaṃ sahajīviniṃ naivaupaṭṭhaisi na upaṭṭhāpanāya ussuk‍kaṃ akāsi, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

4. tuvaṭṭavagga, 5. pañ‍cama sikkhāpucchā

pucchā – bhikkhuniyā  āvusau upassayaṃ datvā kupitāya anattamanāya nik‍kaḍḍhantiyā pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – sāvatthiyaṃ bhantai thul‍lanandaṃyaiva bhikkhuniṃ ārabbha pañ‍ñattaṃ, thul‍lanandā bhantai bhikkhunī bhikkhuniyā upassayaṃ datvā kupitā anattamanā nik‍kaḍḍhi, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

4. tuvaṭṭavagga, 6. chaṭṭha sikkhāpucchā

pucchā – saṃsaṭṭhāya  āvusau bhikkhuniyā yāvatatiyaṃ samanubhāsanāya na paṭinissaj‍jantiyā pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – sāvatthiyaṃ bhantai caṇḍakāḷiṃ bhikkhuniṃ ārabbha pañ‍ñattaṃ, caṇḍakāḷī bhantai bhikkhunī saṃsaṭṭhā vihari, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

4. tuvaṭṭavagga, 7-8. sattama aṭṭhama sikkhāpucchā

pucchā – antauraṭṭhai  vā āvusau tirauraṭṭhai vā sāsaṅkasammatai sappaṭibhayai asatthikāya cārikaṃ carantiyā pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – sāvatthiyaṃ bhantai sambahulā bhikkhuniyau ārabbha pañ‍ñattaṃ, sambahulā bhantai bhikkhuniyau antauraṭṭhaipi tirauraṭṭhaipi sāsaṅkasammatai sappaṭibhayai asatthikāyau cārikaṃ cariṃsu, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

4. tuvaṭṭavagga, 9. navama sikkhāpucchā

pucchā – antauvassaṃ āvusau cārikaṃ carantiyā pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – rājagahai bhantai sambahulā bhikkhuniyau ārabbha pañ‍ñattaṃ, sambahulā bhantai bhikkhuniyau antauvassaṃ cārikaṃ pak‍kamiṃsu, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

4. tuvaṭṭavagga, 10. dasama sikkhāpucchā

pucchā – vassaṃ  vuṭṭhāya āvusau bhikkhuniyā cārikaṃ na pak‍kamantiyā pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – rājagahai bhantai sambahulā bhikkhuniyau ārabbhaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ, sambahulā bhantai bhikkhuniyau vassaṃvuṭṭhā cārikaṃ na pak‍kamiṃsu, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

pucchā – kassa  āvusau vacanaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – bhagavatau bhantai vacanaṃ arahatau sammāsambuddhassa|

pucchā – kaināvusau ābhataṃ|

vissaj‍janā – paramparāya bhantai vacanaṃ|

suṇātu mai bhantai saṅghau…pai॰… saṃgāyaiyyāma|

5. cittāgāravagga, 1. paṭhama sikkhāpucchā

pucchā – yaṃ taina āvusau bhagavatā…pai॰… sammāsambuddhaina rājāgāraṃ vā cittāgāraṃ vā ārāmaṃ vā uyyānaṃ vā paukkharaṇiṃ vā dassanāya gacchantiyā pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – sāvatthiyaṃ  bhantai chabbaggiyā bhikkhuniyau ārabbha pañ‍ñattaṃ, chabbaggiyā bhantai bhikkhuniyau rājāgārampi cittāgārampi dassanāya gacchiṃsu, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

5. cittāgāravagga, 4. catuttha sikkhāpucchā

pucchā – gihivaiyyāvac‍caṃ āvusau karauntiyā pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – sāvatthiyaṃ bhantai sambahulā bhikkhuniyau ārabbha pañ‍ñattaṃ, sambahulā bhantai bhikkhuniyaugihi vaiyyāvac‍caṃ akaṃsu, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

5. cittāgāravagga, 6. chaṭṭha sikkhāpucchā

pucchā – agārikassa  vā āvusau paribbājakassa vā paribbājikāya vā sahatthā khādanīyaṃ bhaujanīyaṃ daintiyā pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – sāvatthiyaṃ bhantai thul‍lanandaṃ bhikkhuniṃ ārabbha pañ‍ñattaṃ, thul‍lanandā bhantai bhikkhunī agārikassa sahatthā khādanīyampi bhaujanīyampi adāsi, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

5. cittāgāravagga, 9-10. navama, dasama sikkhāpucchā

pucchā – tiracchānavij‍jaṃ āvusau pariyāpuṇantiyā ca vācaintiyā ca pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – sāvatthiyaṃ bhantai chabbaggiyā bhikkhuniyau ārabbha pañ‍ñattaṃ, chabbaggiyā bhantai bhikkhuniyau tiracchānavij‍jaṃ pariyāpuṇiṃsucaiva vācaisuñ‍ca, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

pucchā – bhikkhu  pana āvusau tādisaṃ tiracchānavij‍jaṃ pariyāpuṇantau ca vācaintau ca kiṃ nāma āpattiṃ āpaj‍jati|

vissaj‍janā – duk‍kaṭaṃ bhantai āpattiṃ āpaj‍jati|

6. ārāmavagga, 1. paṭhama sikkhāpucchā

pucchā – jānaṃ āvusau sabhikkhukaṃ ārāmaṃ anāpucchā pavisantiyā pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – sāvatthiyaṃ bhantai sambahulā bhikkhuniyau ārabbha pañ‍ñattaṃ, sambahulā bhantai bhikkhuniyau ārāmaṃ anāpucchā pavisiṃsu, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

6. ārāmavagga, 2. dutiya sikkhāpucchā

pucchā – bhikkhuṃ  āvusau ak‍kausantiyā pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – vaisāliyaṃ bhantai chabbaggiyā bhikkhuniyau ārabbha pañ‍ñattaṃ, chabbaggiyā bhantai bhikkhuniyau āyasmantaṃ upāliṃ ak‍kausiṃsu, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

6. ārāmavagga, 3. tatiya sikkhāpucchā

pucchā – caṇḍikatāya  āvusau gaṇaṃ paribhāsantiyā pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – sāvatthiyaṃ bhantai thul‍lanandaṃ bhikkhuniṃ ārabbha pañ‍ñattaṃ, thul‍lanandā bhantai bhikkhunī caṇḍikatāya gaṇaṃ paribhāsi, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

6. ārāmavagga, 5. pañ‍cama sikkhāpucchā

pucchā – kulaṃ  āvusau maccharāyantiyā pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – sāvatthiyaṃ bhantai añ‍ñataraṃ bhikkhuniṃ ārabbha pañ‍ñattaṃ, añ‍ñatarā bhantai bhikkhunī kulaṃ maccharāyi, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

6. ārāmavagga, 6. chaṭṭha sikkhāpucchā

pucchā – abhikkhukai  āvusau āvāsai vassaṃ vasantiyā pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – sāvatthiyaṃ bhantai sambahulā bhikkhuniyau ārabbha pañ‍ñattaṃ, sambahulā bhantai bhikkhuniyau abhikkhukai āvāsai vassaṃ vasiṃsu, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

6. ārāmavagga, 7. sattama sikkhāpucchā

pucchā – vassaṃ  vuṭṭhāya āvusau bhikkhuniyā ubhatausaṅghai tīhi ṭhānaihi na pavāraintiyā pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – sāvatthiyaṃ bhantai sambahulā bhikkhuniyau ārabbha pañ‍ñattaṃ, sambahulā bhantai bhikkhuniyau vassaṃ vuṭṭhā bhikkhusaṅghaṃ na pavāraisuṃ, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

6. ārāmavagga, 8. aṭṭhama sikkhāpucchā

pucchā – auvādāya vā āvusau saṃvāsāya vā nagacchantiyā pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – sak‍kaisu bhantai chabbaggiyā bhikkhuniyau ārabbha pañ‍ñattaṃ, chabbaggiyā bhantai bhikkhuniyau auvādaṃ nagacchiṃsu, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

6. ārāmavagga, 9. navama sikkhāpucchā

pucchā – upausathampi  āvusau na pucchantiyā auvādampi na yācantiyā pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – sāvatthiyaṃ bhantai sambahulā bhikkhuniyau ārabbha pañ‍ñattaṃ, sambahulā bhantai bhikkhuniyau upausathaṃ na pucchiṃsu, auvādampi na yāciṃsu, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

7. gabbhinīvagga, 1-2. paṭhama, dutiya sikkhāpucchā

pucchā – gabbhiniṃ vā āvusau pāyantiṃ vā vuṭṭhāpaintiyā pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – sāvatthiyaṃ  bhantai sambahulā bhikkhuniyau ārabbha pañ‍ñattaṃ, sambahulā bhantai bhikkhuniyau gabbhiniṃ vuṭṭhāpaisuṃ, pāyantiṃ vuṭṭhāpaisuṃ, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

7. gabbhinīvagga, 3. tatiya sikkhāpucchā

pucchā – dvai vassāni āvusau chasu dhammaisu asikkhitasikkhaṃ sikkhamānaṃ vuṭṭhāpaintiyā pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – sāvatthiyaṃ bhantai sambahulā bhikkhuniyau ārabbha pañ‍ñattaṃ, sambahulā bhantai bhikkhuniyau dvai vassāni chasu dhammaisu asikkhitasikkhaṃ sikkhamānaṃ vuṭṭhāpaisuṃ, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

8. kumārībhūtavagga, 9. navama sikkhāpucchā

pucchā – purisasaṃsaṭṭhaṃ  āvusau kumārakasaṃsaṭṭhaṃ caṇḍiṃ saukāvāsaṃ sikkhamānaṃ vuṭṭhāpaintiyā pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – sāvatthiyaṃ bhantai thul‍lanandaṃ bhikkhuniṃ ārabbha pañ‍ñattaṃ, thul‍lanandā bhantai bhikkhunī purisasaṃsaṭṭhaṃ kumārakasaṃsaṭṭhaṃ caṇḍiṃ saukāvāsaṃ caṇḍakāḷiṃ saikkhamānaṃ vuṭṭhāpaisi, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

8. kumārībhūtavagga, 10. dasama sikkhāpucchā

pucchā – mātāpitūhi  vā āvusau sāmikaina vā ananuñ‍ñātaṃ sikkhamānaṃ vuṭṭhāpaintiyā pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – sāvatthiyaṃ bhantai thul‍lanandaṃ bhikkhuniṃ ārabbha pañ‍ñattaṃ, thul‍lanandā bhantai bhikkhunī mātāpitūhipi sāmikainapi ananuñ‍ñātaṃ sikkhamānaṃ vuṭṭhāpaisi, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

9. chattupāhanavagga, 1-2. paṭhama, dutiya sikkhāpucchā

pucchā – chattupāhanaṃ āvusau dhāraintiyā ca yānaina yāyantiyā ca pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – sāvatthiyaṃ bhantai chabbaggiyā bhikkhuniyau ārabbha pañ‍ñattaṃ, chabbaggiyā bhantai bhikkhuniyau chattupāhanaṃ dhāraisuṃ, yānaina yāyiṃsu, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

9. chattupāhanavagga, 4. catuttha sikkhāpucchā

pucchā – itthālaṅkāraṃ  āvusau dhāraintiyā pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – sāvatthiyaṃ bhantai chabbaggiyā bhikkhuniyau ārabbha pañ‍ñattaṃ, chabbaggiyā bhantai bhikkhuniyau itthālaṅkāraṃ dhāraisuṃ, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

pucchā – bhikkhu pana āvusau purisālaṅkāraṃ dhāraintau kiṃ nāma āpattiṃ āpaj‍jaiyya|

vissaj‍janā – duk‍kaṭaṃ bhantai āpattiṃ āpaj‍jaiyya|

9. chattupāhanavagga, 5. pañ‍cama sikkhāpucchā

pucchā – gandhavaṇṇakaina  āvusau nahāyantiyā pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – sāvatthiyaṃ bhantai chabbaggiyā bhikkhuniyau ārabbha pañ‍ñattaṃ, chabbaggiyā bhantai bhikkhuniyau gandhavaṇṇakaina nahāyiṃsu, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

9. chattupāhanavagga, 11. aikādasama sikkhāpucchā

pucchā – bhikkhussa āvusau puratau anāpucchā āsanai nisīdantiyā pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – sāvatthiyaṃ bhantai sambahulā bhikkhuniyau ārabbha pañ‍ñattaṃ, sambahulā bhantai bhikkhuniyau bhikkhussa puratau anāpucchā āsanai nisīdiṃsu, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

9. chattupāhanavagga, 12. dvādasama sikkhāpucchā

pucchā – anaukāsakataṃ  āvusau bhikkhuṃ pañhaṃ pucchantiyā pācittiyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – sāvatthiyaṃ bhantai sambahulā bhikkhuniyau ārabbha pañ‍ñattaṃ, sambahulā bhantai bhikkhuniyau anaukāsakataṃ bhikkhuṃ pañhaṃ pucchiṃsu, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

pucchā – sappiṃ vā āvusau tailaṃ vā madhuṃ vā phāṇitaṃ vā macchaṃ vā maṃsaṃ vā khīraṃ vā dadhiṃ vā viñ‍ñāpaitvā bhuñ‍jantiyā pāṭidaisanīyaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – sāvatthiyaṃ bhantai chabbaggiyā bhikkhuniyau ārabbha pañ‍ñattaṃ, chabbaggiyā bhantai bhikkhuniyau sappimpi tailampi madhumpi phāṇitampi macchampi maṃsampi khīrampi dadhimpi viñ‍ñāpaitvā bhuñ‍jiṃsu, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

pucchā – catun‍naṃ  āvusau vipattīnaṃ katamā vipatti|

vissaj‍janā – ācāravipatti bhantai|

pucchā – chan‍naṃ āvusau āpattisamuṭṭhānānaṃ katihi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhāti|

vissaj‍janā – catūhi bhantai samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhāti, siyā kāyatau samuṭṭhāti, navācatau nacittatau, siyā kāyatau ca vācatau ca samuṭṭhāti, nacittatau, siyā kāyatau ca cittatau ca samuṭṭhāti, navācatau, siyā kāyatau ca vācatau ca cittatau ca samuṭṭhāti, imaihi bhantai catūhi samuṭṭhānaihi samuṭṭhāti|

pucchā – kā  āvusau vipatti|

vissaj‍janā – asaṃvarau bhantai vipatti|

pucchā – kā āvusau sampatti|

vissaj‍janā – saṃvarau bhantai sampatti|

pucchā – kā  āvusau paṭipatti|

vissaj‍janā – na aivarūpaṃ karissāmīti yāvajīvaṃ āpāṇakauṭikaṃ samādāya sikkhati sikkhāpadaisu, ayaṃ bhantai paṭipatti|

pucchā – kassa āvusau vacanaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – bhagavatau bhantai vacanaṃ arahatau sammāsambuddhassa|

pucchā – kaināvusau ābhataṃ|

vissaj‍janā – paramparāya bhantai ābhataṃ|

suṇātu mai bhantai saṅghau…pai॰… saṃgāyaiyyāma|

mahāvaggapāḷi

saṃgāyanassa pucchā vissaj‍janā

pucchā – ubhatau  vibhaṅgānantaraṃ āvusau paurāṇakaihi saṃgītikāramahāthairaihi kaṃ nāma pāvacanaṃ saṃgītaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – ubhatau vibhaṅgānantaraṃ bhantai paurāṇakaihi saṃgītikāramahāthairaihi khandhakā saṃgītā|

pucchā – tai pana āvusau khandhakā vaggabhaidaina katividhā|

vissaj‍janā – tai  pana bhantai khandhakā duvidhā vaggabhaidaina mahāvaggau cūḷavaggauti|

pucchā – mahāvaggai āvusau kati khandhakā pariyāpan‍nā|

vissaj‍janā – mahāvaggai bhantai dasakhandhakā pariyāpan‍nā, saiyyathidaṃ, mahākhandhakau upausathakkhandhakau vassūpanāyikakkhandhakau pavāraṇākkhandhakau cammakkhandhakau bhaisaj‍jakkhandhakau kathinakkhandhakau cīvarakkhandhakau campaiyyakkhandhakau kausambakakkhandhakauti|

pucchā – mahākhandhakai āvusau bahū kathāyau, katamā tāsaṃ ādikathā|

vissaj‍janā – mahākhandhakai bhantai baudhikathā ādi|

mahākhandhaka

mahābaudhipucchā

pucchā – tasmātiha  āvusau tatau paṭṭhāya taṃ pucchissāmi, bhagavā āvusau paṭhamābhisambuddhakālai kattha kīvacīraṃ kainākāraina vihāsi|

vissaj‍janā – bhagavā bhantai paṭhamābhisambuddhau baudhirukkhamūlai sattāhaṃ aikapal‍laṅkaina vihāsi vimuttisukhaṃ paṭisaṃvaidī|

pucchā – tadā āvusau bhagavā kīdisaṃ dhammaṃ manasikatvā kīdisaṃ udānaṃ udānaisi|

vissaj‍janā – tadā bhantai bhagavā rattiyā paṭhamaṃ yāmaṃ rattiyā majjhimaṃ yāmaṃ rattiyā pacchimaṃ yāmaṃ paṭic‍casamuppādaṃ anulaumaṃ paṭilaumaṃ manasikatvā tīṇi udānāni udānaisi|

ajapāla

pucchā – tassa  āvusau sattāhassa ac‍cayaina bhagavā kattha vihāsi|

vissaj‍janā – tassa bhantai sattāhassa ac‍cayaina bhagavā ajapāla nigraudhamūlai vihāsi, sattāhaṃ aikapal‍laṅkaina vihāsi vimuttisukhaṃ paṭisaṃvaidī|

mucalindā

pucchā – tassapi āvusau sattāhassa ac‍cayaina bhagavā kattha vihāsi|

vissaj‍janā – tassa  bhantai sattāhassa ac‍cayaina bhagavā mucalindamūlai sattāhaṃ aikapal‍laṅkaina nisīdi|

pucchā – tassapi āvusau sattāhassa ac‍cayaina bhagavā kattha vihāsi|

vissaj‍janā – tassapi bhantai sattāhassa ac‍cayaina bhagavā rājāyatanamūlai sattāhaṃ aikapal‍laṅkaina nisīdi vimuttisukhaṃ paṭisaṃvaidī|

pucchā – tassapi  āvusau sattāhassa ac‍cayaina bhagavā kattha vihāsi|

vissaj‍janā – tassa bhantai sattāhassa ac‍cayaina bhagavā punadaiva ajapālanigraudhamūlai vihāsi|

pucchā – tasmiṃ pana āvusau ajapālanigraudhai viharantassa bhagavatau kathaṃ caitasau parivitak‍kau udapādi|

vissaj‍janā – tasmiṃ pana bhantai ajapālanigraudhai viharantassa bhagavatau rahaugatassa paṭisal‍līnassa dhammagambhīratāpaṭisaṃyuttau caitasau parivitak‍kau udapādi|

adhigatau khau myāyaṃ dhammau gambhīrau duddasau aranubaudhau santau paṇītau atak‍kāvacarā nipuṇau paṇḍitavaidanīyau|

ālayarāmākhau  panāyaṃ pajā ālayaratā ālayasammuditā,

sahampati brahmā

pucchā – kathaṃ āvusau tadā dhammadaisanāya brahmayācanā ca bhagavatau paṭiñ‍ñāca ahausi|

vissaj‍janā – daisaitu bhantai bhagavā dhammaṃ, daisaitu sugatau dhammaṃ, santi sattā apparajakkhajātikā assavanatā dhammassa parihāyanti, bhavissanti dhammassa añ‍ñātārauti iti bhantai brahmunau dhammadaisanāya yācanā ahausi,

‘‘apārutā taisaṃ amatassa dvārā,

yai sautavantau pamuñ‍cantu saddhaṃ|

vihiṃsasañ‍ñī paguṇaṃ nabhāsiṃ,

dhammaṃ paṇītaṃ manujaisu brahmai’’ti||

aivaṃ khau bhantai bhagavatau dhammadaisanāya paṭiñ‍ñā ca ahausi|

nassati  vata laukau…

daisaitu bhantai bhagavā dhammaṃ…

apārutā taisaṃ amatassa dvārā,

yai sautavantau pamuñ‍cantu saddhaṃ||

pucchā – aivaṃ  khau āvusau bhagavā dhammadaisanāya paṭiñ‍ñaṃ katvā paṭhamaṃ dhammaṃ daisaintau kattha kīdisaṃ dhammaṃ kassa daisaisi|

vissaj‍janā – bārāṇasiyaṃ bhantai isipatanai migadāyai pañ‍cavaggiya bhikkhupamukhānaṃ brahmagaṇānaṃ dhammacak‍kapavattanasuttaṃ daisaisi|

pucchā – tasmiṃ  khau pana āvusau dhammacak‍kapavattanasuttai daisiyamānai manussalaukai kassa dhammābhisamayau ahausi|

vissaj‍janā – āyasmatau bhantai kauṇḍañ‍ñassa dhammābhisamayau ahausi|

pucchā – kathaṃ nu khau āvusau imasmiṃ buddhasāsanai paṭhamaṃ pabbaj‍jā ca upasampadā ca ahausi|

vissaj‍janā – imasmiṃ bhantai buddhasāsanai paṭhamaṃ aihibhikkhupasampadā ahausi|

labhaiyyāhaṃ  bhantai bhagavatau santikai pabbajaṃ, labhaiyyaṃ upasampadaṃ|

aihi bhikkhu svākhātau dhammau, cara brahmacariyaṃ sammā dukkhassa antakiriyāya|

pucchā – itaraisaṃ pana āvusau catun‍naṃ pañ‍cavaggiyānaṃ kadā dhammābhisamayau ahausi|

vissaj‍janā – pāṭipadai bhantai divasai āyasmatau vappassa, dutiyai bhantai divasai āyasmatau bhaddiyassa, tatiyai bhantai divasai āyasmatau mahānāmassa, catutthai bhantai divasai āyasmatau assajissa dhammābhisamayau ahausi|

pucchā – sabbaisampi  āvusau taisaṃ pañ‍cavaggiyānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ kadā arahattapatti hauti|

vissaj‍janā – sabbaisampi bhantai taisaṃ pañ‍cavaggiyānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ pakkhassa pañ‍camiyaṃ anattalakkhaṇasuttai daisiyamānai arahattapatti hauti|

pucchā – kadā āvusau bhagavatā bhikkhū tattha tattha cārikaṃ caritvā dhammadaisanatthāya paisitā|

vissaj‍janā – yasappamukhānaṃ bhantai catupañ‍ñāsa sahāyakānaṃ pabbajitakālai bhagavatā bhikkhū tattha tattha cārikaṃ caritvā dhammadaisanatthāya paisitā|

saraṇagamana

pucchā – kadā  āvusau bhagavatā tīhi saraṇagamanaihi pabbaj‍jūpa sampadā anuñ‍ñātau ahausi|

vissaj‍janā – yasappamukhānaṃ bhantai catupañ‍ñāsāya gihi sahāyakānaṃ pabbajitvā tattha tattha cārikaṃ caritvā dhammadaisanatthāya paisitakālai bhagavatā tīhi saraṇagamanaihi pabbaj‍jā ca upasampadā ca anuñ‍ñātā|

anujānāmi  bhikkhavai tumhaiva dāni tāsu tāsu disāsu taisu taisu janapadaisu pabbājaitha upasampādaitha|

pucchā – bhagavatā āvusau bhikkhūnaṃ ārāmau paṭiggaṇhituṃ kattha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ anuñ‍ñātau|

vissaj‍janā – bhagavatā bhantai bhikkhūnaṃ ārāmaṃ paṭiggaṇhituṃ rājagahai anuñ‍ñātau, rājā bhantai māgadhau sainiyau bimbisārau buddhappamukhassa bhikkhusaṅghassa vaiḷuvanaṃ uyyānaṃ adāsi, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ anuñ‍ñātau|

aitāhaṃ  bhantai vaiḷuvanaṃ uyyānaṃ buddhappamukhassa bhikkhusaṅghassa dammi –

anujānāmi bhikkhavai ārāmaṃ|

pucchā – kathañ‍ca  āvusau dvin‍naṃ aggasāvakānaṃ pabbaj‍jūpasampadā ahausi|

vissaj‍janā – dvin‍naṃ bhantai aggasāvakānaṃ aihi bhikkhupasampadā ahausi|

vippasan‍nāni  khau tai āvusau indriyāni|

kaṃsi tvaṃ āvusau uddissa pabbajitau|

kau vā tai satthā|

kassa vā tvaṃ dhammaṃ raucaisi|

atthāvusau mahāsamaṇau sakyaputtau sakyakulā pabbajitau|

kiṃ vādī panāyasmatau satthā kimakkhāyī|

yai  dhammā haituppabhavā, taisaṃ haituṃ tathāgatau|

āha taisañ‍ca yau niraudhau, aivaṃ vādī mahāsamaṇau||

alaṃ āvusau mā agamittha|

aitai bhikkhavai dvai sahāyakā āgacchanti kaulitau upatissau ca aitaṃ mai sāvakayugaṃ bhavissati aggaṃ bhaddayugaṃ|

labhaiyyāma  mayaṃ bhantai bhagavatau santikai pabbaj‍jaṃ labhaiyyāma upasampadaṃ|

aitha bhikkhavau svākhātau dhammau, caratha brahmacariyaṃ sammā dukkhassa antakiriyāya|

pucchā – upajjhāyau āvusau gaṇhituṃ bhagavatā kattha anuñ‍ñātau, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ anuñ‍ñātau|

vissaj‍janā – rājagahai bhantai sambahulai bhikkhū ārabbha anuñ‍ñātau, sambahulā bhantai bhikkhū anupajjhāyakā anācariyakā anauvadiyamānā ananusāsiyamānā dun‍nivatthā duppārutā anaukappasampan‍nā piṇḍāya cariṃsu, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ anuñ‍ñātau|

pucchā – upajjhāyamhi āvusau na sammāvattantassa duk‍kaṭaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – rājagahai  bhantai sambahulai saddhivihārikai ārabbha pañ‍ñattaṃ, sambahulā bhantai saddhivihārikā upajjhāyamhi na sammāvattiṃsu, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

pucchā – kadā āvusau bhagavatā tīhi saraṇagamanaihi upasampadaṃ paṭikkhipitvā ñāticatutthaina kammaina upasampadā anuñ‍ñātā|

vissaj‍janā – rādhabrāhmaṇassa bhantai pabbajitakālai bhagavatā tīhi saraṇagamanaihi upasampadaṃ paṭikkhipitvā ñātticatutthaina kammaina upasampadā anuñ‍ñātā|

kaunu  khau bhikkhavai tassa brāhmaṇassa adhikāraṃ sarati|

ahaṃ khau bhantai tassa brāhmaṇassa adhikāraṃ sarāmi|

sādhu sādhu sāriputta, katañ‍ñunau hi sāriputta sappurisā katavaidinau|

kathāhaṃ bhantai taṃ brāhmaṇaṃ upasampādaimi|

yāsā  bhikkhavai mayā tīhi saraṇagamanaihi upasampadāanuñ‍ñātā, taṃ aj‍jataggai paṭikkhipāmi|

anujānāmi bhikkhavai ñātticatutthaina kammaina upasampādaituṃ|

pucchā – ayācitakaṃ āvusau upasampadāpaikkhaṃ upasampādaintassa duk‍kaṭaṃ bhagavatā kattha pañ‍ñattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – rājagahai bhantai añ‍ñataraṃ bhikkhuṃ ārabbha pañ‍ñattaṃ, añ‍ñatarau bhantai bhikkhu upasampan‍nasamanantaraṃ anācāraṃ acari, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

pucchā – catun‍naṃ  āvusau nissayānaṃ ācikkhaṇā bhagavatā kattha anuñ‍ñātā, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ anuñ‍ñātā|

vissaj‍janā – rājagahai bhantai añ‍ñataraṃ bhikkhuṃ ārabbha anuñ‍ñātā, añ‍ñatarau bhantai bhikkhu udarassa kāraṇā pabbaji, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ anuñ‍ñātā|

aihi  dāni āvusau piṇḍāya carissāma|

kathañhi nāma bhikkhu aivaṃ svākkhātai dhammavinayai udarassa kāraṇā pabbajissati|

pucchā – ūnadasavassaina  āvusau upasampādaintassa duk‍kaṭaṃ bhagavatā kattha pañ‍ñattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – rājagahai bhantai āyasmantaṃ upasainaṃ vaṅgantaputtaṃ ārabbha pañ‍ñattaṃ, āyasmā bhantai upasainau vaṅgantaputtau aikavassau saddhivihārikaṃ upasampādaisi, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

pucchā – bālaina  āvusau abyattaina upasampādaintassa duk‍kaṭaṃ bhagavatā kattha pañ‍ñattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – rājagahai bhantai sambahulai bhikkhū ārabbha pañ‍ñattaṃ, sambahulā bhantai bhikkhū ‘‘dasavassamhā dasavassamhā’’ti bālā abyattā upasampādaisuṃ, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

pucchā – ācariyau  āvusau gaṇhituṃ bhagavatā kattha anuñ‍ñātau, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ anuñ‍ñātau|

vissaj‍janā – rājagahai bhantai sambahulai bhikkhū ārabbha anuñ‍ñātau, sambahulā bhantai bhikkhū upajjhāyaisu pak‍kantaisupi vibbhantaisupi kālaṅkataisupi pakkhasaṅkantaisupi anācariyakā anauvadiyamānā ananusāsiyamānā dun‍nivatthā duppārutā anākappasampan‍nā piṇḍāya cariṃsu, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ anuñ‍ñātau|

pucchā – pañ‍cahi  āvusau ābādhaihi phuṭṭhaṃ pabbājaintassa duk‍kaṭaṃ bhagavatā kattha pañ‍ñattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – rājagahai bhantai sambahulai bhikkhū ārabbha pañ‍ñattaṃ, sambahulā bhantai bhikkhū pañ‍cahi ābādhaihi phuṭṭhaṃ pabbājaisuṃ, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

sādhu  bhantai ayyā pañ‍cahi ābādhaihi phuṭṭhaṃ na pabbājaiyyuṃ…

pucchā – rājabhaṭaṃ āvusau pabbājaintassa duk‍kaṭaṃ bhagavatā kattha pañ‍ñattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – rājagahai bhantai sambahulai bhikkhū ārabbha pañ‍ñattaṃ, sambahulā bhantai bhikkhū rājabhaṭai pabbājaisuṃ, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

pucchā – dhajabandhaṃ  āvusau cauraṃ pabbājaintassa duk‍kaṭaṃ bhagavatā kattha pañ‍ñattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – rājagahaiyaiva bhantai aṅgulimālaṃ cauraṃ ārabbha pañ‍ñattaṃ, aṅgulimālau bhantai caurau bhikkhūsu pabbajitau hauti, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

pucchā – kārabhaidakaṃ āvusau cauraṃ pabbājaintassa duk‍kaṭaṃ bhagavatā kattha pañ‍ñattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – rājagahaiyaiva  bhantai sambahulai bhikkhū ārabbha pañ‍ñattaṃ, sambahulā bhantai bhikkhū kārabhaidakacauraṃ pabbājaisuṃ, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

pucchā – iṇāyikaṃ āvusau pabbājaintassa duk‍kaṭaṃ bhagavatā kattha pañ‍ñattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – rājagahaiyaiva bhantai sambahulai bhikkhū ārabbha pañ‍ñattaṃ, sambahulā bhantai bhikkhū iṇāyikaṃ pabbājaisuṃ, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

pucchā – dāsaṃ  āvusau pabbajaintassa duk‍kaṭaṃ bhagavatā kattha pañ‍ñattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – rājagahaiyaiva bhantai sambahulai bhikkhū ārabbha pañ‍ñattaṃ, sambahulā bhantai bhikkhū dāsaṃ pabbājaisuṃ, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

pucchā – bhaṇḍukammāya āvusau apalaukanaṃ bhagavatā kattha anuñ‍ñātaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ anuñ‍ñātaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – rājagahaiyaiva bhantai añ‍ñataraṃ kammārabhaṇḍuputtaṃ ārabbha anuñ‍ñātaṃ añ‍ñatarau bhantai kammārabhaṇḍuputtau mātāpitūhi saddhiṃ bhaṇḍitvā bhikkhūsu pabbajitau hauti, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ anuñ‍ñātaṃ|

pucchā – ūnavīsativassassa  āvusau puggalassa upasampadā bhagavatā kattha paṭikkhittā, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ paṭikkhittā|

vissaj‍janā – rājagahai  bhantai sambahulai bhikkhū ārabbha paṭikkhittā, sambahulā bhantai bhikkhū ūnavīsativassaṃ puggalaṃ upasampādaisuṃ, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ paṭikkhittā|

na  bhikkhavai jānaṃ ūnavīsativassau puggalau upasampādaitabbau, yauupasampādaiyya, yathādhammau kāraitabbau|

pucchā – mātāpitūhi  āvusau ananuñ‍ñātaṃ puttaṃ pabbājaintassa duk‍kaṭaṃ bhagavatā kattha pañ‍ñattaṃ, kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – sak‍kaisu bhantai pañ‍ñattaṃ, rājā bhantai suddhaudanau bhagavantaṃ upasaṅkamitvā varaṃ yāci, sādhu bhantai ayyāananuñ‍ñātaṃ mātāpitūhi puttaṃ pabbājaiyyunti, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

na  bhikkhavai ananuñ‍ñātau mātāpitūhi puttau pabbājaitabbau, yau pabbājaiyya, āpattiduk‍kaṭassa|

pucchā – thaiyyasaṃvāsakassa āvusau upasampadā bhagavatā kattha paṭikkhittā, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ paṭikkhittā|

vissaj‍janā – sāvatthiyaṃ bhantai añ‍ñataraṃ purāṇakulaputtaṃ ārabbha paṭikkhittā, añ‍ñatarau bhantai purāṇakulaputtau khīṇa kaulañ‍ñau sāmaṃ pattacīvaraṃ paṭiyādaitvā kaisamassuṃ auhāraitvā  kāsāyāni vatthāni acchādaitvā ārāmaṃ gantvā bhikkhūhi saddhiṃ saṃvasi, bhantai vatthusmiṃ paṭikkhittā|

pucchā – tiracchānagatassa  āvusau upasampadā bhagavatā kattha paṭikkhittā, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ paṭikkhittā|

vissaj‍janā – sāvatthiyaṃ bhantai añ‍ñataraṃ nāgaṃ ārabbha paṭikkhittā, añ‍ñatarau bhantai nāgau māṇavakavaṇṇaina bhikkhū upasaṅkamitvā pabbaj‍jaṃ yāci, taṃ bhikkhū pabbājaisuṃ upasampādaisuṃ, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ paṭikkhittā|

pucchā – mātughātakassa  ca āvusau pitughātakassa ca upasampadā bhagavatā kattha paṭikkhittā, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ paṭikkhittā|

vissaj‍janā – sāvatthiyaṃyaiva  bhantai añ‍ñataraṃ māṇavakaṃ ārabbha paṭikkhittā, añ‍ñatarau ca bhantai māṇavakau añ‍ñatarau ca māṇavakau mātaraṃ jīvitā vauraupaisi pitaraṃ jīvitā vauraupaisi, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ paṭikkhittā|

mātughātakau  bhikkhavai anupasampan‍nau naupasampādaitabbau, upasampan‍nau nāsaitabbau, pitughātakau bhikkhavai anupasampan‍nau naupasampādaitabbau, upasampan‍nau nāsaitabbau|

pucchā – apattacīvarakaṃ vā āvusau yācitapattacīvarakaṃ vā upasampādaintassa duk‍kaṭaṃ bhagavatā kattha pañ‍ñattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – sāvatthiyaṃ bhantai sambahulai bhikkhū ārabbha pañ‍ñattaṃ, sambahulā bhantai bhikkhū apattacīvarakaṃ upasampādaisuṃ, yācitakaina pattacīvaraina upasampādaisuṃ, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

pucchā – upasampadaṃ  pucchissaṃ, sanidānaṃ sauddaisaṃ| samuk‍kaṭṭhapadānaṃ āvusau kati āpattiyau|

vissaj‍janā – upasampadaṃ vissaj‍jissaṃ, sanidānaṃ saniddaisaṃ| samuk‍kaṭṭhapadānaṃ bhantai dvai āpattiyau|

2. upausathakkhandhaka

pucchā – cātuddasai  ca āvusau pan‍narasai ca pakkhassa ca aṭṭhamiyā san‍nipatitvā dhammaṃ bhāsituṃ bhagavatā kattha anuñ‍ñātaṃ, kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ anuñ‍ñātaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – rājagahai bhantai sambahulai bhikkhū ārabbha anuñ‍ñātā, sambahulā bhantai bhikkhū cātuddaisa pan‍narasai aṭṭhamiyā ca pakkhassa san‍nipatitvā tuṇhī nisīdiṃsu, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ anuñ‍ñātā|

anujānāmi  bhikkhavai cātuddasai pan‍narasai aṭṭhamiyā ca pakkhassa san‍nipatituṃ|

nanu nāma san‍nipatitaihi dhammau bhāsitabbau|

pucchā – kadā āvusau bhikkhūnaṃ pātimaukkhuddaisau bhagavatā anuñ‍ñātau|

vissaj‍janā – yadā bhantai bhagavatau rahaugatassa paṭisal‍līnassa aivaṃ caitasau parivitak‍kau udapādi ‘‘yaṃnūnāhaṃ yāni mayā bhikkhūnaṃ pañ‍ñattāni sikkhāpadāni, tāni naisaṃ pātimaukkhuddaisaṃ anujānaiyyaṃ, sau naisaṃ bhavissati upausathakamma’’nti  tadā bhantai bhagavatā bhikkhūnaṃ pātimaukkhuddaisau anuñ‍ñātau|

pucchā – sīmaṃ āvusau sammanituṃ bhagavatā kattha anuñ‍ñātaṃ, kismiñ‍ca vatthusmiṃ anuñ‍ñātaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – rājagahai bhantai anuñ‍ñātaṃ, rājagahai bhantai bhagavati viharati bhikkhūnaṃ aitadahausi ‘‘bhagavatā pañ‍ñattaṃ aittāvatā sāmaggī, yāvatā aikāvāsauti kittāvatānukhau aikāvāsau hautī’’ti, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ anuñ‍ñātaṃ|

pucchā – kati  āvusau upausathā divasavasaina ca kārakapuggalavasaina ca kātabbākāravasaina ca|

vissaj‍janā – divasavasaina bhantai tayau upausathā, puggalavasaina ca bhantai tayau upausathā, kātabbākāravasaina ca bhantai tayau upausathā|

pucchā – kati  āvusau upausathakammāni taisu ca kīdisaṃ upausathakammaṃ bhagavatā anuñ‍ñātaṃ, kīdisaṃ ananuñ‍ñātaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – cattārimāni bhantai upausathakammāni, adhammaina vaggaṃ upausathakammaṃ, adhammaina samaggaṃ upausathakammaṃ, dhammaina vaggaṃ upausathakammaṃ, dhammaina samaggaṃ upausathakammaṃ| tatra bhantai yadidaṃ adhammaina vaggaṃ upausathakammaṃ, adhammaina samaggaṃ upausathakammaṃ dhammaina vaggaṃ upausathakammaṃ, aivarūpaṃ bhantai upausathakammaṃ ananuñ‍ñātaṃ, tatra bhantai yadidaṃ dhammaina samaggaṃ upausathakammaṃ, aivarūpaṃ bhantai upausathakammaṃ anuñ‍ñātaṃ|

pucchā – upausathaṃ  pucchissaṃ, sanidānaṃ saniddaisaṃ| samuk‍kaṭṭhapadānaṃ āvusau kati āpattiyau|

vissaj‍janā – upausathaṃ vissaj‍jissaṃ, sanidānaṃ saniddaisaṃ| samuk‍kaṭṭhapadānaṃ bhantai tissau āpattiyau|

3. vassupanāyikakkhandhaka

pucchā – vassūpanāyikaṃ āvusau bhagavatā kattha pañ‍ñattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – rājagahai bhantai sambahulā bhikkhū ārabbha pañ‍ñattaṃ, sambahulā bhantai bhikkhū haimantampi gimhampi vassampi cārikaṃ cariṃsu, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

pucchā – katipanāvusau vassūpanāyikā bhagavatā anuñ‍ñātā|

vissaj‍janā – dvaimā bhantai vassūpanāyikā bhagavatā anuñ‍ñātā purimikā pacchimikā, aparaj‍jugatāya bhantai āsaḷhiyā purimikā upagantabbā, māsagatāya bhantai āsaḷhiyā pacchimikā upagantabbā, imā khau bhantai dvai vassūpanāyikā bhagavatā anuñ‍ñātā|

pucchā – vassaṃ  āvusau upagantvā purimaṃ vā taimāsaṃ pacchimaṃ vā taimāsaṃ avasitvā cārikaṃ pak‍kamantassa duk‍kaṭaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – rājagahai bhantai chabbaggiyai bhikkhū ārabbha pañ‍ñattaṃ, chabbaggiyā bhantai bhikkhū vassaṃ upagantvā antarā vassaṃ cārikaṃ pak‍kamiṃsu, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

pucchā – sattāha karaṇīyaina āvusau pahitai gantuṃ bhagavatā kattha anuñ‍ñātaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ anuñ‍ñātaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – sāvatthiyaṃ bhantai sambahulai bhikkhū ārabbha anuñ‍ñātaṃ, sambahulā bhantai bhikkhū udainaina upāsakaina pahitai na gacchiṃsu, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ anuñ‍ñātaṃ|

pucchā – sattāhakaraṇīyaina  āvusau sattan‍naṃ apahitaipi gantuṃ bhagavatā kattha anuñ‍ñātaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ anuñ‍ñātaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – sāvatthiyaṃ bhantai añ‍ñatarassa bhikkhunau mātugilāna vatthusmiṃ anuñ‍ñātaṃ|

anujānāmi  bhikkhavai sattan‍naṃ sattāhakaraṇīyaina apahitaipi gantuṃ, pagaiva pahitai|

pucchā – kismiṃci āvusau antarāyai sati antauvassaṃ pak‍kamituṃ bhagavatā kattha anuñ‍ñātaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ anuñ‍ñātaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – sāvatthiyaṃ bhantai sambahulai bhikkhū ārabbha anuñ‍ñātaṃ, sambahulā bhantai bhikkhū kausalaisu janapadaisu vassaṃ upagacchiṃsu, tai vassūpagatā vāḷaihi ubbāḷhā ahaisuṃ, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ anuñ‍ñātaṃ|

pucchā – rukkha  susirai vā āvusau rukkhaviṭabhiyā vā ajjhaukāsai vā vassaṃ upagacchantassa duk‍kaṭaṃ bhagavatā kattha pañ‍ñattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – sāvatthiyaṃ bhantai sambahulai bhikkhū ārabbha pañ‍ñattaṃ, sambahulā bhantai bhikkhū rukkhasusiraipi rukkhaviṭabhiyāpi ajjhaukāsaipi vassaṃ upagacchiṃsu, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

pucchā – asaināsanikaina  āvusau vassaṃ upagacchantassa duk‍kaṭaṃ bhagavatā kattha pañ‍ñattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – tasmiṃyaiva bhantai sāvatthiyaṃ sambahulai bhikkhū ārabbha pañ‍ñattaṃ, sambahulā bhantai bhikkhū asaināsanikā vassaṃ upagacchiṃsu, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

pucchā – chavakuṭikāya vā āvusau chattai vā cāṭiyā vā vassaṃ upagacchantassa duk‍kaṭaṃ bhagavatā kattha pañ‍ñattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – sāvatthiyaṃyaiva bhantai sambahulai bhikkhū ārabbha pañ‍ñattaṃ, sambahulā bhantai bhikkhū chavakuṭikāyapi chattaipi cāṭiyāpi vassaṃ upagacchiṃsu, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

pucchā – paṭissavaṃ  āvusau visaṃ vādaintassa duk‍kaṭaṃ bhagavatā kattha pañ‍ñattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – sāvatthiyaṃ bhantai āyasmantaṃ upanandaṃ sakyaputtaṃ ārabbha pañ‍ñattaṃ, āyasmā bhantai upanandau sakyaputtau rañ‍ñau pasainadissa kausalassa vassaṃ vāsaṃ paṭissuṇitvā visaṃvādaisi, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

pucchā – vassūpanāyikaṃ  pucchissaṃ, sanidānaṃ saniddaisaṃ| samuk‍kaṭṭhapadānaṃ āvusau kati āpattiyau|

vissaj‍janā – vassūpanāyikaṃ vissaj‍jissaṃ, sanidānaṃ saniddaisaṃ| samuk‍kaṭṭhapadānaṃ bhantai aikā āpatti|

4. pavāraṇākkhandhaka

pucchā – vassaṃ  vuṭṭhānaṃ āvusau bhikkhūnaṃ tīhi ṭhānaihi pavārituṃ bhagavatā kattha pañ‍ñattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – sāvatthiyaṃ bhantai sambahulai bhikkhū ārabbha anuñ‍ñātaṃ, sambahulā bhantai bhikkhū sandiṭṭhā sambhattā antauvassaṃ naiva ālapiṃsu na sal‍lapiṃsu, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ anuñ‍ñātaṃ|

anujānāmi  bhikkhavai vassaṃ vuṭṭhānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ tīhi ṭhānaihi pavāraituṃ, diṭṭhaiva vā sutaina vā parisaṅkāya vā|

pucchā – kati āvusau pavāraṇā divasavasaina ca puggalavasaina ca kātabbākāravasaina ca|

vissaj‍janā – divasavasaina bhantai tissau pavāraṇā, tathā puggalavasaina kātabbākāravasainaca|

pucchā – katīnaṃ  āvusau saṅghai pavāraituṃ bhagavatā anuñ‍ñātaṃ, katīnaṃ panāvusau añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ pavāraituṃ bhagavatā anuñ‍ñātaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – pañ‍can‍naṃ bhantai bhikkhūnaṃ saṅghai pavāraituṃ bhagavatā anuñ‍ñātaṃ, catun‍naṃ vā bhantai tiṇṇaṃ vā dvin‍naṃ vā añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ pavāraituṃ anuñ‍ñātaṃ|

pucchā – aikaina panāvusau vassaṃvuṭṭhaina bhikkhunā kathaṃ paṭipaj‍jitabbanti bhagavatā anuñ‍ñātaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – aikaina pana bhantai bhikkhunā ‘‘aj‍ja mai pavāraṇā’’ti adhiṭṭhātabbanti bhagavatā anuñ‍ñātaṃ|

pucchā – pavāraṇaṃ  pucchissaṃ, sanidānaṃ saniddaisaṃ| samuk‍kaṭṭhapadānaṃ āvusau kati āpattiyau|

vissaj‍janā – pavāraṇaṃ vissaj‍jissaṃ, sanidānaṃ saniddaisaṃ| samuk‍kaṭṭhapadānaṃ bhantai tissau āpattiyau|

5. cammakkhandhaka

sauṇamathaira vatthu

pucchā – upāhanaṃ  āvusau bhagavatā kattha anuñ‍ñātaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ anuñ‍ñātaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – rājagahai bhantai āyasmantaṃ sauṇaṃ ārabbha anuñ‍ñātaṃ, āyasmatau bhantai sauṇassa ac‍cāraddhavīriyassa caṅkamatau pādā bhij‍jiṃsu, caṅkamau lauhitaina phuṭau ahausi, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ anuñ‍ñātaṃ|

pucchā – sabbanīlikā  vā āvusau sabbapītikā vā sabbalauhitikā vā sabbamañ‍jiṭṭhikā vā sabbakaṇhā vā sabbamahāraṅgarattā vā sabbamahānāmarattā  vā upāhanāyau dhāraintassa duk‍kaṭaṃ bhagavatā kattha kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – rājagahai bhantai chabbaggiyai bhikkhū ārabbha pañ‍ñattaṃ, chabbaggiyā bhantai bhikkhū sabbanīlikāyau upāhanāyau dhāraisuṃ, sabbapītikāyau sabbalauhitikāyau sabbamañ‍jiṭṭhikāyau sabbakaṇhāyau sabbamahāraṅgarattā sabbamahānāmarattāyaupi upāhanāyau dhāraisuṃ, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

pucchā – khal‍lakabandhā  vā āvusau puṭabandhā vā pāliguṇṭhimā vā tūlapuṇṇikā vā tittirapattikā vā maiṇḍavisāṇavaddhikā vā ajavisāṇavaddhikā vā vicchikāḷikā vā maurapiñcha parisibbikā vā citrā vā upāhanāyau dhāraintassa duk‍kaṭaṃ bhagavatā kattha pañ‍ñattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – rājagahai bhantai chabbaggiyai bhikkhū ārabbha pañ‍ñattaṃ, chabbaggiyā bhantai bhikkhū khal‍lakabandhā upāhanāyaupi dhāraisuṃ, puṭabandhā upāhanāyaupi dhāraisuṃ, pāliguṇṭhimā upāhanāyaupi dhāraisuṃ, tūlapuṇṇikā upāhanāyaupi dhāraisuṃ, tittirapattikā upāhanāyaupi dhāraisuṃ, maiṇḍavisāṇavaddhikā upāhanāyaupi dhāraisuṃ, ajavisāṇavaddhikā upāhanāyaupi dhāraisuṃ, vicchikāḷikā upāhanāyaupi dhāraisuṃ, maurapiñcha parisibbitā upāhanāyau dhāraisuṃ, citrā upāhanāyaupi dhāraisuṃ, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

pucchā – kaṭṭhapādukāyau  āvusau dhāraintassa duk‍kaṭaṃ bhagavatā kattha kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – rājagahai bhantai chabbaggīyaiva bhikkhū ārabbha pañ‍ñattaṃ, chabbaggiyā bhantai bhikkhū rattiyā pac‍cūsasamayaṃ pac‍cuṭṭhāya kaṭṭhapādukāyau abhiruhitvā ajjhaukāsai caṅkamanti uc‍cāsaddā mahāsaddā khaṭakhaṭasaddā anaikavihitaṃ tiracchānakathaṃ kathaintā, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

pucchā – yānaina  āvusau yāyantassa duk‍kaṭaṃ bhagavatā kattha pañ‍ñattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – sāvatthiyaṃ bhantai chabbaggiyai bhikkhū ārabbha pañ‍ñattaṃ, chabbaggiyā bhantai bhikkhū yānaina yāyiṃsu, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

pucchā – gilānassa  āvusau yānaṃ bhagavatā kattha anuñ‍ñātaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ anuñ‍ñātaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – sāvatthiyaṃ bhantai añ‍ñataraṃ gilānaṃ bhikkhuṃ ārabbha anuñ‍ñātaṃ, añ‍ñatarau bhantai bhikkhu kausalaisu janapadai sāvatthiṃ gacchantau bhagavantaṃ dassanāya antarāmaggai gilānau kuk‍kuc‍cāyantau yānaṃ nābhiruhi, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ anuñ‍ñātaṃ|

pucchā – uc‍cāsayanamahāsayanāni  āvusau dhāraintassa duk‍kaṭaṃ bhagavatā kattha pañ‍ñattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – sāvatthiyaṃ bhantai chabbaggiyai bhikkhū ārabbha pañ‍ñattaṃ, chabbaggiyā bhantai bhikkhū uc‍cāsayanamahāsayanāni dhāraisuṃ, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

pucchā – gaucammāni āvusau dhāraintassa duk‍kaṭaṃ bhagavatā kattha pañ‍ñattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – sāvatthiyaṃ bhantai añ‍ñataraṃ pāpabhikkhuṃ ārabbha pañ‍ñattaṃ, añ‍ñatarau bhantai bhikkhu pāṇātipātai samādapaisi, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

pucchā – pac‍cantimaisu  āvusau janapadaisu vinayadharapañ‍camaina gaṇaina upasampadā ca guṇaṅguṇūpahanañ‍ca dhuvanahānañ‍ca cammāni attharaṇāni ca nissīmagatānaṃ cīvaradānañ‍cāti imāni pañ‍ca bhagavatā kattha anuñ‍ñātāni, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ anuñ‍ñātāni|

vissaj‍janā – sāvatthiyaṃ bhantai āyasmantaṃ sauṇaṃ kuṭikaṇṇaṃ ārabbha anuñ‍ñātā, āyasmā bhantai sauṇau kuṭikaṇṇau aitāni pañ‍cavatthūni āyasmatau mahākac‍cānassa vacanaina bhagavantaṃ yāci, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ anuñ‍ñātā|

pucchā – cammasañ‍ñuttaṃ  pucchissaṃ, sanidānaṃ saniddaisaṃ| samuk‍kaṭṭhapadānaṃ āvusau kati āpattiyau|

vissaj‍janā – cammasañ‍ñuttaṃ vissaj‍jissaṃ, sanidānaṃ saniddaisaṃ| samuk‍kaṭṭhapadānaṃ bhantai tissau āpattiyau|

6. bhaisaj‍jakkhandhaka

pucchā – pañ‍ca  āvusau bhaisaj‍jāni paṭiggahaitvā kālaipi vikālaipi paribhuñ‍jituṃ bhagavatā kattha anuñ‍ñātaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ anuñ‍ñātaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – sāvatthiyaṃ bhantai sambahulai bhikkhū ārabbha anuñ‍ñātaṃ, sambahulānaṃ bhantai bhikkhūnaṃ sāradikaina ābādhaina puṭṭhānaṃ yāgupi na sammā pariṇāmaṃ gacchi, bhattampi bhuttaṃ na sammā pariṇāmaṃ gacchi, tai taina kisā ahaisuṃ lūkhā dubbaṇṇā uppaṇḍuppaṇḍukajātā dhamanisanthatagattā, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ anuñ‍ñātaṃ|

pucchā – mūlādibhaisaj‍jāni  āvusau bhagavatā kattha anuñ‍ñātāni, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ anuñ‍ñātāni|

vissaj‍janā – sāvatthiyaṃ bhantai sambahulai gilānai bhikkhū ārabbha anuñ‍ñātāni, sambahulānaṃ gilānānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ mūlaihi bhaisaj‍jaihi atthau ahausi, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ anuñ‍ñātāni|

pucchā – antauvuṭṭhaṃvā āvusau antaupak‍kaṃ vā sāmaṃpak‍kaṃ vā paribhuñ‍jantassa duk‍kaṭaṃ bhagavatā kattha pañ‍ñattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – rājagahai bhantai āyasmantaṃ ānandaṃ ārabbha pañ‍ñattaṃ, āyasmā bhantai ānandau sāmaṃ tilampi taṇḍulampi muggampi viñ‍ñāpaitvā antau vāsaitvā antau sāmaṃ paci, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

suppiyā vatthu

pucchā – manussamaṃsaṃ  āvusau paribhuñ‍jantassa thul‍lac‍cayañ‍ca appaṭivaikkhitvā maṃsaṃ paribhuñ‍jantassa duk‍kaṭañ‍ca bhagavatā kattha pañ‍ñattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – bārāṇasiyaṃ bhantai añ‍ñataraṃ bhikkhuṃ ārabbha pañ‍ñattaṃ, añ‍ñatarau bhantai bhikkhu appaṭivaikkhitvā manussamaṃsaṃ bhuñ‍ji, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

na  bhikkhavai manussamaṃsaṃ paribhuñ‍jitabbaṃ, yau paribhuñ‍jaiyya, āpatti thul‍lac‍cayassa, na ca bhikkhavai appaṭivaikkhitvā maṃsaṃ paribhuñ‍jitabbaṃ, yau paribhuñ‍jaiyya āpatti duk‍kaṭassa|

pucchā – hatthimaṃsaṃ vā āvusau assamaṃsaṃ vā paribhuñ‍jantassa duk‍kaṭaṃ bhagavatā kattha pañ‍ñattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – bārāṇasiyaṃ bhantai sambahulai bhikkhū ārabbha pañ‍ñattaṃ, sambahulā bhantai bhikkhū hatthimaṃsampi assamaṃsampi paribhuñ‍jiṃsu, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

pucchā – sunakkhamaṃsaṃ  vā āvusau ahimaṃsaṃ vā bhuñ‍jantassa duk‍kaṭaṃ bhagavatā kattha pañ‍ñattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – bārāṇasiyaṃ bhantai sambahulai bhikkhū ārabbha pañ‍ñattaṃ, sambahulā bhantai bhikkhū sunakkhamaṃsampi ahimaṃsampi paribhuñ‍jiṃsu, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

pucchā – sīhamaṃsaṃ  vā āvusau byagghamaṃsaṃ vā dīpimaṃsaṃ vā acchamaṃsaṃ vā taracchamaṃsaṃ vā bhuñ‍jantassa duk‍kaṭaṃ bhagavatā kattha pañ‍ñattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – bārāṇasiyaṃyaiva bhantai sambahulai bhikkhū ārabbha pañ‍ñattaṃ, sambahulā bhantai bhikkhū sīhamaṃsampi byagghamaṃsampi dīpimaṃsampi acchamaṃsampi taracchamaṃsampi paribhuñ‍jiṃsu, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

pucchā – gilānassa  āvusau guḷaṃ vā agilānassa guḷaudakaṃ vā bhagavatā kattha anuñ‍ñātaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ anuñ‍ñātaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – rājagahai bhantai sambahulai bhikkhū ārabbha anuñ‍ñātaṃ, sambahulā bhantai bhikkhū gilānassaiva bhagavatā guḷau anuñ‍ñātau nau agilānassāti kuk‍kuc‍cāyantā guḷaṃ na bhuñ‍jiṃsu, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ anuñ‍ñātaṃ|

pucchā – jānaṃ  āvusau uddissa kataṃ maṃsaṃ paribhuñ‍jantassa duk‍kaṭaṃ pañ‍ñapaitvā tikauṭiparisuddhaṃ maṃsaṃ bhagavatā kattha anuñ‍ñātaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – vaisāliyaṃ bhantai sīhassa saiṭāpatinau vatthusmiṃ anuñ‍ñātaṃ|

maiṇḍaka vatthu

pucchā – pāthaiyyaṃ  āvusau pariyaisituñ‍ca kappiyakārakānaṃ hatthai upanikkhittahirañ‍ñatau nibbattaṃ kappiyapac‍cayaṃ sādituñ‍ca bhagavatā kattha anuñ‍ñātaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ anuñ‍ñātaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – antarā  ca bhantai bhaddiyaṃ antarā ca aṅguttarāpaṃ maiṇḍakaṃ gahapatiṃ ārabbha anuñ‍ñātaṃ, maiṇḍakau bhantai gahapati bhagavantaṃ yāci ‘‘santi bhantai maggā kantārā appaudakā appabhakkhā na sukarā apāthaiyyaina gantuṃ, sādhu bhantai bhagavā bhikkhūnaṃ pāthaiyyaṃ anujānātū’’ti, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ anuñ‍ñātaṃ|

kaiṇiya vatthu

pucchā – aṭṭha  āvusau pānāni bhagavatā kattha anuñ‍ñātāni, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ anuñ‍ñātāni|

vissaj‍janā – āpaṇai bhantai sambahulai bhikkhū ārabbha anuñ‍ñātāni, sambahulā bhantai bhikkhū kuk‍kuc‍cāyantā pānāni na paṭiggaṇhiṃsu, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ anuñ‍ñātāni|

raujamal‍lā vatthu

pucchā – sabbañ‍ca  āvusau ḍākaṃ sabbañ‍ca piṭṭhakhādanīyaṃ bhagavatā kattha anuñ‍ñātaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ anuñ‍ñātaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – kusinārāyaṃ bhantai sambahulai bhikkhū ārabbha anuñ‍ñātaṃ, sambahulā bhantai bhikkhū kuk‍kuc‍cāyantā ḍākañ‍ca piṭṭhakhādanīyañ‍ca na paṭiggahaisuṃ, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ anuñ‍ñātaṃ|

pucchā – akappiyaṃ  āvusau samādapaintassa duk‍kaṭaṃ bhagavatā kattha pañ‍ñattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – ātumāyaṃ bhantai añ‍ñataraṃ vuḍḍhapabbajitaṃ ārabbha pañ‍ñattaṃ, añ‍ñatarau bhantai vuḍḍhapabbajitau akappiyai samādapaiti, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

pucchā – saṅghikāni  vā āvusau puggalikāni vā bījāni puggalikāya vā saṅghikāya vā bhūmiyā raupitāni bhāgaṃ datvā paribhuñ‍jituṃ bhagavatā kattha anuñ‍ñātaṃ, kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ anuñ‍ñātaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – sāvatthiyaṃ bhantai anuñ‍ñātaṃ, sāvatthiyaṃ bhantai bhagavati viharati saṅghikānipi bījāni puggalikāya bhūmiyā raupiyiṃsu, puggalikānipi bījāni saṅghikāya bhūmiyā raupiyiṃsu, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ anuñ‍ñātaṃ|

pucchā – vinayamahāpadaisasaṅkhātāni  āvusau cattāri kappiyākappiyaanulaumāni bhagavatā kattha pañ‍ñattāni, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattāni|

vissaj‍janā – sāvatthiyaṃ bhantai sambahulai bhikkhū ārabbha pañ‍ñattāni, sambahulānaṃ bhantai bhikkhūnaṃ kismiñ‍ci kismiñ‍ciṭhānai kuk‍kuc‍caṃ uppaj‍ji, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattāni|

pucchā – yāvakālikādīhi  saṃsaṭṭhānaṃ āvusau yāmakālikādīnaṃ paribhaugakālamariyādaṃ bhagavatā kattha pañ‍ñattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – sāvatthiyaṃ bhantai sambahulai bhikkhū ārabbha pañ‍ñattaṃ, sambahulānaṃ bhantai bhikkhūnaṃ aitadahausi ‘‘kappati nukhau yāvakālikaina yāmakālikaṃ sattāhakālikaṃ yāvajīvikaṃ, na nukhau kappati| kappatinukhau yāmakālikaina sattāhakālikaṃ yāvajīvikaṃ, na nukhau kappati| kappati nukhau sattāhakālikaina yāvajīvikaṃ, na nukhau kappatī’’ti| tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

pucchā – bhaisaj‍jakaṃ  pucchissaṃ, sanidānaṃ saniddaisaṃ| samuk‍kaṭṭhapadānaṃ āvusau kati āpattiyau|

vissaj‍janā – bhaisaj‍jakaṃ vissaj‍jissaṃ sanidānaṃ saniddaisaṃ| samuk‍kaṭṭhapadānaṃ bhantai tissau āpattiyau|

7. kathinakkhandhaka

pucchā – vassaṃvuṭṭhānaṃ  āvusau bhikkhūnaṃ kathinaṃ attharituṃ bhagavatā kattha anuñ‍ñātaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ anuñ‍ñātaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – sāvatthiyaṃ bhantai tiṃsamattai pāvaiyyakai bhikkhū ārabbha anuñ‍ñātaṃ, tiṃsamattā bhantai pāvaiyyakā bhikkhū sāvatthiṃ āgacchantā bhagavantaṃ dassanāya upakaṭṭhāya vassūpanāyikāya nāsakkhiṃsu sāvatthiyaṃ vassūpanāyikaṃ sambhāvaituṃ, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ anuñ‍ñātaṃ|

pucchā – kathinakaṃ  pucchissaṃ, sanidānaṃ saniddaisaṃ| samuk‍kaṭṭhapadānaṃ āvusau kati āpattiyau|

vissaj‍janā – kathinakaṃ vissaj‍jissaṃ, sanidānaṃ saniddaisaṃ| samuk‍kaṭṭhapadānaṃ bhantai na katamā āpatti|

8. cīvarakkhandhaka

jīvakavatthu

pucchā – gahapaticīvaraṃ  āvusau sādiyituṃ bhagavatā kattha anuñ‍ñātaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ anuñ‍ñātaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – rājagahai bhantai jīvakaṃ kaumārabhac‍caṃ ārabbha anuñ‍ñātaṃ, jīvakau bhantai kaumārabhac‍cau bhikkhūnaṃ gahapaticīvaraṃ anujānituṃ bhagavantaṃ yāci, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ anuñ‍ñātaṃ|

pucchā – cha  āvusau rajanāni bhagavatā kattha anuñ‍ñātāni, kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ anuñ‍ñātāni|

vissaj‍janā – rājagahai bhantai sambahulai bhikkhū ārabbha anuñ‍ñātāni, sambahulā bhantai bhikkhū chakaṇainapi paṇḍumattikāyapi cīvaraṃ rajiṃsu, cīvaraṃ dubbaṇṇaṃ hauti, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ anuñ‍ñātāni|

pucchā – acchin‍nakāni  āvusau cīvarāni dhāraintassa duk‍kaṭaṃ bhagavatā kattha pañ‍ñattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – rājagahai bhantai sambahulai bhikkhū ārabbha pañ‍ñattaṃ, sambahulā bhantai bhikkhū acchin‍nakāni cīvarāni dhāraisuṃ dantakasāvāni, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

pucchā – kadā āvusau bhagavā anuñ‍ñāsi chin‍nakaṃ saṅghāṭiṃ chin‍nakaṃ uttarāsaṅgaṃ chin‍nakaṃ antaravāsakaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – dakkhiṇāgiritau bhantai pacchāgatakālai bhagavā anuñ‍ñāsi chin‍nakaṃ saṅghāṭiṃ chin‍nakaṃ uttarāsaṅgaṃ chin‍nakaṃ antaravāsakaṃ|

pucchā – aggaḷaṃ  ca āvusau tun‍nañ‍ca auvaṭṭikañ‍ca kaṇḍusakañ‍ca daḷhīkammañ‍ca bhagavatā kattha anuñ‍ñātaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ anuñ‍ñātaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – bārāṇasiyaṃ bhantai añ‍ñataraṃ bhikkhuṃ ārabbha anuñ‍ñātaṃ, añ‍ñatarau bhantai bhikkhu aggaḷaṃ acchupaisi, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ anuñ‍ñātaṃ|

pucchā – vassikasāṭikā  ca āvusau āgantukabhattañ‍ca gamikabhattañ‍ca gilānabhattañ‍ca gilānupaṭṭhākabhattañ‍ca gilānabhaisaj‍jañ‍ca dhuvayāgu ca bhikkhunisaṅghassa udakasāṭikācāti imā aṭṭha bhagavatā kattha anuñ‍ñātā, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ anuñ‍ñātā|

vissaj‍janā – sāvatthiyaṃ bhantai visākhaṃ migāramātaraṃ ārabbha anuñ‍ñātā, visākhā bhantai migāramātā bhagavantaṃ aṭṭhavarāni yāci, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ anuñ‍ñātā|

pucchā – bhagavatā  āvusau mātāpitūnaṃ dānaṃ anujānitvā saddhādaiyyaṃ vinipātaintassa duk‍kaṭaṃ kattha pañ‍ñattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – sāvatthiyaṃ bhantai añ‍ñataraṃ bhikkhuṃ ārabbha pañ‍ñattaṃ, añ‍ñatarassa bhantai bhikkhunau bahuṃ cīvaraṃ uppan‍naṃ ahausi, sau ca taṃcīvaraṃ mātāpitūnaṃ dātukāmau ahausi, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

mātāpitarauti  khau bhikkhavai dadamānai kiṃ vadaiyyāma, anujānāmi bhikkhavai mātāpitūnaṃ dātuṃ, na ca bhikkhavai saddhādaiyyaṃ vinipātaitabbaṃ, yau vinipātaiyya āpatti duk‍kaṭassa –

pucchā – añ‍ñatra vassaṃ vuṭṭhaina āvusau añ‍ñatra cīvarabhāgaṃ sādiyantassa duk‍kaṭaṃ bhagavatā kattha pañ‍ñattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – sāvatthiyaṃ bhantai āyasmantaṃ upanandaṃ sakyaputtaṃ ārabbha pañ‍ñattaṃ, āyasmā bhantai upanandau sakyaputtau añ‍ñatra vassaṃ vuṭṭhau añ‍ñatra cīvarabhāgaṃ sādiyi, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

pucchā – anāthakaṃ  āvusau bhikkhuṃ gilānaṃ na upaṭṭhahantassa duk‍kaṭaṃ bhagavatā kattha pañ‍ñattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – sāvatthiyaṃ bhantai añ‍ñataraṃ bhikkhuṃ ārabbha pañ‍ñattaṃ, bhagavati bhantai sāvatthiyaṃ viharati añ‍ñatarassa bhikkhunau kucchivikārābādhau ahausi, sau sakai muttakarīsai palipan‍nau sayi, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

pucchā – naggiyaṃ  āvusau titthiyasamādānaṃ samādiyantassa thul‍lac‍cayaṃ bhagavatā kattha pañ‍ñattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – sāvatthiyaṃ bhantai añ‍ñataraṃ bhikkhuṃ ārabbha pañ‍ñattaṃ, añ‍ñatarau bhantai bhikkhu naggiyaṃ titthiyasamādānaṃ samādiyi, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

pucchā – sabbanīlakāni  vā āvusau cīvarāni sabbapītakāni vā sabbalauhitakāni vā sabbamañ‍jiṭṭhakāni vā sabbakaṇhānivāti aivarūpāni cīvarāni dhāraintassa duk‍kaṭaṃ bhagavatā kattha pañ‍ñattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – sāvatthiyaṃ  bhantai chabbaggiyai bhikkhū ārabbha pañ‍ñattaṃ, chabbaggiyā bhantai bhikkhū sabbanīlakādīni cīvarāni dhāraisuṃ, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

pucchā – kañ‍cukaṃ  vā āvusau vaiṭhanaṃ vā dhāraintassa duk‍kaṭaṃ bhagavatā kattha pañ‍ñattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – sāvatthiyaṃ bhantai chabbaggiyaiva bhikkhū ārabbha pañ‍ñattaṃ, chabbaggiyā bhantai bhikkhū kañ‍cukampi dhāraisuṃ vaiṭhanampi dhāraisuṃ, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

pucchā – cīvarasañ‍ñuttaṃ pucchissaṃ, sanidānaṃ saniddaisaṃ| samuk‍kaṭṭhapadānaṃ āvusau kati āpattiyau|

vissaj‍janā – cīvarasañ‍ñuttaṃ vissaj‍jissaṃ, sanidānaṃ saniddaisaṃ| samuk‍kaṭṭhapadānaṃ bhantai tissau āpattiyau|

6. campaiyyakkhandhaka

pucchā – suddhaṃ  āvusau bhikkhuṃ anāpattikaṃ avatthusmiṃ akāraṇai ukkhipantassa duk‍kaṭaṃ bhagavatā kattha pañ‍ñattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – campāyaṃ bhantai sambahulai bhikkhū ārabbha pañ‍ñattaṃ, bhagavati bhantai campāyaṃ viharati sambahulā bhikkhū kāsīsu vāsabhagāmai suddhaṃ kassapagauttaṃ bhikkhuṃ anāpattikaṃ avatthusmiṃ akāraṇai ukkhipiṃsu, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

pucchā – campaiyyakaṃ  pucchissaṃ, sanidānaṃ saniddaisaṃ| samuk‍kaṭṭhapadānaṃ āvusau kati āpattiyau|

vissaj‍janā – campaiyyakaṃ vissaj‍jissaṃ, sanidānaṃ saniddaisaṃ| samuk‍kaṭṭhapadānaṃ bhantai aikā āpatti|

10. kausambakakkhandhaka

pucchā – bhaidagarukaihi  bhikkhavai bhikkhūhi na sau bhikkhu āpattiyā adassanai ukkhipitabbauti ca, bhaidagarukaina bhikkhavai bhikkhunā paraisampi saddhāya āpattidaisaitabbāti ca āvusau ayaṃ sāmaggirasaauvādau bhagavatā kattha din‍nau, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ din‍nau|

vissaj‍janā – kausambiyaṃ bhantai sambahulai bhikkhū ārabbha din‍nau, bhagavati bhantai kausambiyaṃ viharati añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu āpattiṃ āpan‍nau ahausi, sau tassā āpattiyā āpatti diṭṭhi ahausi, añ‍ñai bhikkhū tassā āpattiyā anāpatti diṭṭhinau ahaisuṃ| sau aparaina samayaina tassā āpattiyā anāpattidiṭṭhi ahausi, añ‍ñai bhikkhū tassā āpattiyā āpattidiṭṭhinau ahaisuṃ| atha khau tai bhantai bhikkhū sāmaggiṃ labhitvā taṃ bhikkhuṃ āpattiyā adassanai ukkhipiṃsu, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ din‍nau|

pucchā – saṅghabhaidassa  āvusau mūlabhūtai bhikkhumhi ukkhittakai ausāritai tassa vatthussa vūpasamāya saṅghasāmaggiñ‍ca kātuṃ sāmaggī upausathañ‍ca kātuṃ bhagavatā kattha anuñ‍ñātaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ anuñ‍ñātaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – sāvatthiyaṃ bhantai taiyaiva ukkhittakānuvattakai bhikkhū ārabbha anuñ‍ñātaṃ, tai bhantai ukkhittakānuvattakā bhikkhū taṃ ukkhittakaṃ bhikkhuṃ ausāraitvā yaina ukkhaipakā bhikkhū tainupasaṅkamiṃsu upasaṅkamitvā tai ukkhaipakai bhikkhū aikadavaucuṃ ‘‘yasmiṃ āvusau vatthusmiṃ ahausi saṅghassa  bhaṇḍanaṃ kalahau viggahau vivādau saṅghabhaidau saṅgharāji saṅghavavatthānaṃ saṅghanānākaraṇaṃ, sau aisau bhikkhuṃ āpan‍nau ca ukkhittau ca passi ca ausāritau ca, handa mayaṃ āvusau tassa vatthussa vūpasamāya saṅghasāmaggiṃ karaumā’’ti, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ anuñ‍ñātaṃ|

pucchā – kausambakaṃ  pucchissaṃ, sanidānaṃ saniddaisaṃ| samuk‍kaṭṭhapadānaṃ āvusau kati āpattiyau|

vissaj‍janā – kausambakaṃ vissaj‍jissaṃ, sanidānaṃ saniddaisaṃ| samuk‍kaṭṭhapadānaṃ bhantai aikā āpatti|

cūḷavaggapāḷi

1. kambakkhandhaka

pucchā – bhaṇḍanādikārakassa  āvusau bhikkhunau taj‍janīyakammaṃ kātuṃ bhagavatā kattha anuñ‍ñātaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ anuñ‍ñātaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – sāvatthiyaṃ bhantai paṇḍukalauhitakai bhikkhū ārabbha anuñ‍ñātaṃ, paṇḍukalauhitakā bhantai bhikkhū attanā bhaṇḍanakārakā kalahakārakā vivādakārakā bhassakārakā saṅghai adhikaraṇakārakā, yaipi cañ‍ñai bhikkhū bhaṇḍanakārakā kalahakārakā vivādakārakā bhassakārakā saṅghai adhikaraṇakārakā tai upasaṅkamitvā aivamāhaṃsu ‘‘mā khau tumhai āyasmantau aisau ajaisi, balavābalavaṃ paṭimantaitha, tumhai taina paṇḍitatarā ca byattatarā ca bahussutatarā ca alamattatarā ca, mā cassa bhāyittha, mayampi tumhākaṃ pakkhā bhavissāmā’’ti, taina anuppan‍nāni caiva bhaṇḍanāni uppaj‍janti, uppan‍nāni ca bhaṇḍanāni bhiyyaubhāvāya vaipul‍lāya saṃvattanti| tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ anuñ‍ñātaṃ|

pucchā – taj‍janīyakammakatassa  āvusau bhikkhunau katisu vattaisu sammā vattantassa taṃ kammaṃ paṭippassambhaituṃ bhagavatā kattha anuñ‍ñātaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ anuñ‍ñātaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – aṭṭhārasasu  bhantai vattaisu sammā vattantassa taj‍janīyakammakatassa bhikkhunau taṃ kammaṃ paṭippassambhaituṃ bhagavatā sāvatthiyaṃ anuñ‍ñātaṃ, taiyaiva paṇḍukalauhitakā bhikkhū saṅghaina taj‍janīyakammakatā sammā vattitvā saṅghaṃ upasaṅkamitvā yāciṃsu, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ anuñ‍ñātaṃ|

pucchā – bālassa  āvusau abyattassa āpattibahulassa anapadānassa gihisaṃsaṭṭhassa ananulaumikaihi gihisaṃsaggaihi niyassakammaṃ kātuṃ kattha pañ‍ñattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – sāvatthiyaṃ bhantai āyasmantaṃ saiyyasakaṃ ārabbha pañ‍ñattaṃ, āyasmā bhantai saiyyasakau bālau ahausi abyattau āpattibahulau anapadānau gihisaṃsaṭṭhau vihāsi ananulaumikaihi gihisaṃsaggaihi, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

pucchā – niyassakammakatassa  āvusau bhikkhunau katisu vattaisu sammā vattantassa taṃ kammaṃ paṭippassambhaituṃ bhagavatā anuñ‍ñātaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – aṭṭhārasasu bhantai vattaisu sammā vattantassa niyassa kammakatassa bhikkhunau taṃ kammaṃ paṭippassambhaituṃ bhagavatā anuñ‍ñātaṃ, na upasampādaitabbaṃ, na nissayau dātabbau, na sāmaṇairau upaṭṭhāpaitabbau, na bhikkhunauvādakasammuti sāditabbā, sammatainapi bhikkhuniyau na auvaditabbā, aivamādīsu bhantai aṭṭhārasasu vattaisu sammā vattantassa niyassakammakatassa bhikkhunau taṃ kammaṃ paṭippassambhaituṃ bhagavatā anuñ‍ñātaṃ|

pucchā – kuladūsakassa  āvusau pāpasamācārassa bhikkhunau pabbājanīyakammaṃ kātuṃ bhagavatā kattha anuñ‍ñātaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ anuñ‍ñātaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – sāvatthiyaṃ bhantai assajipunabbasukai bhikkhū ārabbha anuñ‍ñātaṃ, assajipunabbasukā bhantai bhikkhū kīṭāgirismiṃ kuladūsakā ahaisuṃ pāpasamācārā, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ anuñ‍ñātaṃ|

pucchā – paṭisāraṇīyakammaṃ  āvusau pucchāmi, saddhaṃ āvusau pasan‍naṃ gahapatiṃ dāyakaṃ kārakaṃ saṅghupaṭṭhākaṃ hīnaina khuṃsaintassa bhikkhunau  paṭisāraṇīyakammaṃ kātuṃ bhagavatā kattha anuñ‍ñātaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ anuñ‍ñātaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – sāvatthiyaṃ bhantai āyasmantaṃ sudhammaṃ ārabbha anuñ‍ñātaṃ, bhagavati bhantai sāvatthiyaṃ viharati āyasmā sudhammau macchikāsaṇḍai cittaṃ gahapatiṃ saddhaṃ pasan‍naṃ dāyakaṃ kārakaṃ saṅghupaṭṭhākaṃ hīnaina khuṃsaisi hīnaina vambhaisi, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ anuñ‍ñātaṃ|

tainahi  bhikkhavai saṅghau sudhammassa bhikkhunau paṭisāraṇīyakammaṃ karautu ‘‘cittau tai gahapati khamāpaitabbau’’ti –

pucchā – paṭisāraṇīyakammakatassa  āvusau bhikkhunau anudūtaṃ dātuñ‍ca taina anudūtaina saddhiṃ gantvā yathākhuṃsitaṃ gahapatiṃ khamāpaituṃ  ca bhagavatā kattha anuñ‍ñātaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ anuñ‍ñātaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – sāvatthiyaṃ bhantai āyasmantaṃyaiva sudhammaṃ ārabbha anuñ‍ñātaṃ, āyasmā bhantai sudhammau macchikāsaṇḍaṃ gantvā maṅkubhūtau nāsakkhi cittaṃ gahapatiṃ khamāpaituṃ, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ anuñ‍ñātaṃ|

pucchā – paṭisāraṇīyakammakatassa  āvusau bhikkhunau katisu vattaisu sammā vattantassa taṃ kammaṃ paṭippassambhaituṃ bhagavatā anuñ‍ñātaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – aṭṭhārasasu bhantai vattaisu sammā vattantassa paṭisāraṇīya kammakatassa bhikkhunau taṃ kammaṃ paṭippassambhaituṃ bhagavatā anuñ‍ñātaṃ|

pucchā – āpattiṃ  āvusau āpaj‍jitvā taṃ āpattiṃ passituṃ vā paṭikātuṃ vā na icchantassa bhikkhunau āpattiyā adassanai vā appaṭikammai vā ukkhaipanīyakammaṃ kātuṃ bhagavatā kattha pañ‍ñattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – kausambiyaṃ bhantai āyasmantaṃ chan‍natthairaṃ ārabbha pañ‍ñattaṃ, āyasmā bhantai chan‍nau āpattiṃ āpaj‍jitvā na icchi taṃ āpattiṃ passituṃ vā paṭikātuṃ vā, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

pucchā – āpattiyā  āvusau adassanaina vā appaṭikammai vā ukkhaipanīya kammakataina bhikkhunā katisu vattaisu sammā vattitabbaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – taicattālīsāya bhantai vattaisu sammā vattitabbaṃ|

pucchā – ukkhaipanīyakammakatassa  āvusau bhikkhunau sammā vattantassa taṃ kammaṃ paṭippassambhaituṃ bhagavatā kattha anuñ‍ñātaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ anuñ‍ñātaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – kausambiyaṃ bhantai āyasmantaṃyaiva chan‍naṃ ārabbha anuñ‍ñātaṃ, āyasmā bhantai chan‍nau saṅghaina āpattiyā adassanai vā appaṭikammai vā ukkhaipanīyakammakatau sammā vattaisi laumaṃpātaisi naitthāraṃ vattaisi saṅghaṃ upasaṅkamitvā tassa kammassa paṭippassaddhiyā yāci, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ anuñ‍ñātaṃ|

pucchā – pāpikaṃ  āvusau diṭṭhiṃ gahaitvā taṃ diṭṭhiṃ na paṭinissaj‍jantassa pāpikāya diṭṭhiyā appaṭinissaggai ukkhaipanīyakammaṃ kātuṃ bhagavatā kattha pañ‍ñattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – sāvatthiyaṃ bhantai ariṭṭhaṃ bhikkhuṃ gaddhabādhipubbaṃ ārabbha pañ‍ñattaṃ, bhagavati bhantai sāvatthiyaṃ viharati ariṭṭhassa nāma bhikkhunau gaddhabādhipubbassa aivarūpaṃ pāpakaṃ diṭṭhigataṃ uppan‍naṃ ahausi ‘‘tathāhaṃ bhagavatā dhammaṃ daisitaṃ ājānāmi, yathāyaimai antarāyikā dhammā vuttā bhagavatā, tai paṭisaivatau  nālaṃ antarāyāyā’’ti, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

pucchā – pāpikāya āvusau diṭṭhiyā appaṭinissaggai ukkhaipanīyakammakataina bhikkhunā katisu vattaisu sammā vattitabbaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – taicattālīsāya  bhantai vattaisu sammā vattitabbaṃ|

pucchā – kammakkhandhakaṃ pucchissaṃ, sanidāni saniddaisaṃ| samuk‍kaṭṭhapadānaṃ āvusau kati āpattiyau|

vissaj‍janā – kammakkhandhakaṃ vissaj‍jissaṃ, sanidānaṃ saniddaisaṃ| samuk‍kaṭṭhapadānaṃ bhantai aikā āpatti|

2. pārivāsikakkhandhaka

pucchā – pārivāsikassa  āvusau pakatattānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ abhivādanādīni sādiyantassa duk‍kaṭaṃ bhagavatā kattha pañ‍ñattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – sāvatthiyaṃ bhantai pārivāsikai bhikkhū ārabbha pañ‍ñattaṃ, pārivāsikā bhantai bhikkhū sādiyiṃsu pakatattānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ abhivādanādīni, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

pucchā – pārivāsikaina  āvusau bhikkhunā katisu vattaisu sammā vattitabbaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – pārivāsikaina bhantai bhikkhunā catunavutiyā vattaisu sammā vattitabbaṃ|

pucchā – pārivāsikaṃ pucchissaṃ, sanidānaṃ saniddaisaṃ| samuk‍kaṭṭhapadānaṃ āvusau kati āpattiyau|

vissaj‍janā – pārivāsikaṃ vissaj‍jissaṃ, sanidānaṃ saniddaisaṃ| samuk‍kaṭṭhapadānaṃ bhantai aikā āpatti|

3. samuc‍cayakkhandhaka
