pucchā – samuc‍cayaṃ  pucchissaṃ, sanidānaṃ saniddaisaṃ| samuk‍kaṭṭhapadānaṃ āvusau kati āpattiyau|

vissaj‍janā – samuc‍cayaṃ vissaj‍jissaṃ, sanidānaṃ saniddaisaṃ| samuk‍kaṭṭhapadānaṃ bhantai aikā āpatti|

4. samathakkhandhaka

pucchā – asammukhībhūtānaṃ  āvusau bhikkhūnaṃ taj‍janīyaṃ vā niyassaṃ vā pabbājanīyaṃ vā paṭisāraṇīyaṃ vā ukkhaipanīyaṃ vā kammaṃ karauntassa duk‍kaṭaṃ bhagavatā kattha pañ‍ñattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – sāvatthiyaṃ bhantai chabbaggiyai bhikkhū ārabbha pañ‍ñattaṃ, chabbaggiyā bhantai bhikkhū asammukhībhūtānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ kammaṃ akaṃsu taj‍janīyampi niyassampi pabbājanīyampi paṭisāraṇīyampi ukkhaipanīyampi, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

pucchā – sativaipul‍lapattassa  āvusau bhikkhunau sativinayaṃ dānaṃ bhagavatā kattha anuñ‍ñātaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ anuñ‍ñātaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – rājagahai bhantai maittiyabhūmajakai bhikkhū ārabbha anuñ‍ñātaṃ maittiyabhūmajakā bhantai bhikkhū āyasmantaṃ dabbaṃ mal‍laputtaṃ amūlikāya sīlavipattiyā anuddhaṃsaisuṃ, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ anuñ‍ñātaṃ|

pucchā – katipanāvusau  aittha dhammikāni sativinayadānāni|

vissaj‍janā – pañ‍cimāni bhantai dhammikāni sativinayassa dānāni, suddhau hauti bhikkhu anāpattikau, anuvadanti ca naṃ, yācati ca, tassa saṅghau sativinayaṃ daiti dhammaina samaggaina, imāni khau bhantai pañ‍ca dhammikāni sati vinayassa dānāni|

pucchā – amūḷhassa  āvusau bhikkhunau amūḷhavinayaṃ dātuṃ bhagavatā kattha anuñ‍ñātaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ anuñ‍ñātaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – rājagahai bhantai sambahulai bhikkhū ārabbha anuñ‍ñātaṃ, sambahulā bhantai bhikkhū gaggaṃ bhikkhuṃ ummattakaina cittavipariyāsakataina  ajjhāciṇṇaina āpattiyā caudaisuṃ ‘‘saratā yasmā aivarūpiṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍jitā’’ti, sau aivaṃ vadaiti ‘‘ahaṃ khau āvusau ummattakau ahausi cittavipariyāsakatau, taina mai ummattakaina cittavipariyāsakataina bahuṃ assāmaṇakaṃ ajjhāciṇṇaṃ bhāsitaparik‍kantaṃ, nāhaṃ taṃ sarāmi, mūḷhaina mai aitaṃ kata’’nti| aivampi naṃ vuc‍camānā caudaintaiva ‘‘saratā yasmā aivarūpiṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍jitā’’ti, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ anuñ‍ñātaṃ|

pucchā – kati  āvusau adhammikāni amūḷhavinayassa dānāni, vibhajitvā kathaihi|

vissaj‍janā – tīṇi bhantai adhammikāni amūḷhavinayassa dānāni, idha bhantai bhikkhu āpattiṃ āpan‍nau hauti, tamainaṃ caudaiti saṅghau vā sambahulā vā aikapuggalau vā ‘‘saratāyasmā aivarūpiṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍jitā’’ti, sau saramānauva aivaṃ vadaiti ‘‘na khau ahaṃ āvusau sarāmi aivarūpiṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍jitā’’ti| añ‍ñau saramānauva aivaṃ vadaiti ‘‘sarāmi khau ahaṃ āvusau yathā supinantainā’’ti| añ‍ñau anummattakauva ummattakālayaṃ karauti ‘‘ahampi khau aivaṃ karaumi, tumhaipi aivaṃ karautha, mayhampi aitaṃ kappati, tumhākampaitaṃ kappatī’’ti| aisaṃ saṅghau amūḷhavinayaṃ daiti, adhammikaṃ bhantai amūḷhavinayassa dānaṃ| imāni khau bhantai tīṇi adhammikāni amūḷhavinayassa dānāni|

pucchā – kati  āvusau dhammikāni amūḷhavinayassa dānāni, vibhajitvā kathaihi|

vissaj‍janā – tīṇi bhantai dhammikāni amūḷhavinayassa dānāni, idha bhantai bhikkhu ummattakau hauti cittavipariyāsakatau taina ummattakaina cittavipariyāsakataina bahuṃ assāmaṇakaṃ ajjhāciṇṇaṃ hauti bhāsitaparik‍kantaṃ, tamainaṃ caudaiti saṅghau vā sambahulā vā aikapuggalau vā ‘‘saratāyasmā aivarūpiṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍jitā’’ti, sau assaramānauva aivaṃ vadaiti ‘‘na khau ahaṃ āvusau sarāmi aivarūpiṃ āpattiṃ āpaj‍jitā’’ti| añ‍ñaupi assaramānauva aivaṃ vadaiti ‘‘sarāmi khau ahaṃ āvusau yathā supinantainā’’ti| añ‍ñaupi ummattakauva ummattakālayaṃ karauti ‘‘ahampi aivaṃ karaumi, tumhaipi aivaṃ karautha, mayhampi aitaṃ kappati, tumhākampaitaṃ kappatī’’ti| aisaṃ bhantai tiṇṇaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ saṅghau amūḷhavinayaṃ daiti, dhammikaṃ bhantai amūḷhavinayassa dānaṃ| imāni khau bhantai tīṇi dhammikāni amūḷhavinayassa dānāni|

pucchā – appaṭiñ‍ñāya  āvusau bhikkhūnaṃ taj‍janīyādīni kammāni karauntassa duk‍kaṭaṃ bhagavatā kattha pañ‍ñattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – rājagahai  bhantai chabbaggiyai bhikkhū ārabbha pañ‍ñattaṃ, chabbaggiyā bhantai bhikkhū appaṭiñ‍ñāya bhikkhūnaṃ kammāni akaṃsu taj‍janīyādīni, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

pucchā – kathaṃ āvusau adhammikaṃ hauti paṭiñ‍ñātakaraṇaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – añ‍ñathā bhantai āpattiṃ āpaj‍jantassa añ‍ñathā paṭijānantassa yathā sau paṭijānāti, tathā saṅghau kāraiti, aivaṃ khau bhantai adhammikaṃ hauti paṭiñ‍ñātakaraṇaṃ|

pucchā – kathaṃ  āvusau dhammikaṃ hauti paṭiñ‍ñātakaraṇaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – yaṃ bhantai āpattiṃ āpaj‍jantassa tamaiva paṭijānantassa tainaiva saṅghau kāraiti, aivaṃ khau bhantai dhammikaṃ paṭiñ‍ñātakaraṇaṃ|

pucchā – yaibhuyyasikāya  āvusau adhikaraṇaṃ vūpasamaituṃ bhagavatā kattha anuñ‍ñātaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ anuñ‍ñātaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – rājagahai  bhantai sambahulai bhikkhū ārabbha anuñ‍ñātaṃ, sambahulā bhantai bhikkhū bhaṇḍanajātā kalahajātā vivādāpan‍nā añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ mukhasattīhi vitudantā vihariṃsu, na sak‍kaunti taṃ adhikaraṇaṃ vūpasamaituṃ, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ anuñ‍ñātaṃ|

pucchā – kathaṃ āvusau adhikaraṇaṃ yaibhuyyasikāya vūpasamaitabbaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – pañ‍cahi bhantai aṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu salākaggāhāpakau samman‍nitabbau, taina bhantai salākaggāhāpakaina  salākā gāhātabbā yathā bahutarā bhikkhū dhammavādinau vadanti tathā taṃ adhikaraṇaṃ vūpasamaitabbaṃ, aivaṃ khau bhantai yaibhuyyasikāya adhikaraṇaṃ vūpasamaitabbaṃ|

pucchā – kati  āvusau adhammikā salākaggāhā|

vissaj‍janā – dasa bhantai adhammikā salākaggāhā, auramattakañ‍ca adhikaraṇaṃ hauti, na ca gatigataṃ hauti, na ca saritasāritaṃ hauti, jānāti ‘adhammavādī bahutarā’ti, appaiva nāma adhammavādī bahutarā assūti, jānāti ‘saṅghau bhij‍jissatī’ti, appaiva nāma saṅghau bhij‍jaiyyāti, adhammaina gaṇhanti, vaggā gaṇhanti, na ca yathādiṭṭhiyā gaṇhanti, imai khau bhantai dasa adhammikā kalākaggāhā|

pucchā – kati  āvusau dhammikā salākaggāhā|

vissaj‍janā – dasa bhantai dhammikā salākaggāhā vuttavipariyāyaina|

pucchā – pāpussan‍nassa āvusau bhikkhussa tassa pāpiyasikā kammaṃ kātuṃ bhagavatā kattha pañ‍ñattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – rājagahai bhantai upavāḷaṃ bhikkhuṃ ārabbha pañ‍ñattaṃ, upavāḷau bhantai bhikkhu saṅghamajjhai āpattiyā anuyuñ‍jiyamānau avajānitvā paṭijānāti, paṭijānitvā avajānāti, añ‍ñainañ‍ñaṃ paṭicarati, sampajānamusā bhāsati, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

pucchā – kati  āvusau dhammikāni tassapāpiyasikā kammassa karaṇāni|

vissaj‍janā – pañ‍cimāni bhantai dhammikāni tassapāpiyasikā kammassa karaṇāni| asuci ca hauti, alaj‍jī ca, sānuvādau ca, tassa saṅghau tassapāpiyasikā kammaṃ karauti dhammaina samaggaina, imāni khau bhantai pañ‍ca dhammikāni tassa pāpiyasikā kammassa karaṇāni|

pucchā – tassa  pāpiyasikākammakataina āvusau bhikkhunā katisu vattaisu sammā vattitabbaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – tassa pāpiyasikākammakataina bhantai bhikkhunā aṭṭhārasasu vattaisu sammā vattitabbaṃ| na upasampādaitabbaṃ, na nissayau dātabbau, na sāmaṇairau upaṭṭhāpaitabbau, aivamādīsu bhantai aṭṭhārasasu vattaisu sammā vattitabbaṃ|

pucchā – kati  āvusau adhikaraṇāni samathaihi vūpasamaitabbāni|

vissaj‍janā – cattārimāni bhantai adhikaraṇāni samathaihi vūpasamaitabbāni, vivādādhikaraṇaṃ anuvādādhikaraṇaṃ āpattādhikaraṇaṃ kic‍cādhikaraṇaṃ, imāni bhantai cattāri adhikaraṇāni samathaihi vūpasamaitabbāni|

pucchā – kiṃ  āvusau vivādādhikaraṇassa mūlaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – cha bhantai vivādamūlāni vivādādhikaraṇassa mūlaṃ, tīṇipi akusalamūlāni vivādādhikaraṇassa mūlaṃ, tīṇipi kusalamūlāni vivādādhikaraṇassa mūlaṃ, idaṃ bhantai vivādādhikaraṇassa mūlaṃ|

pucchā – kiṃ  panāvusau anuvādādhikaraṇassa mūlaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – cha bhantai anuvādamūlāni anuvādādhikaraṇassa mūlaṃ, tīṇipi akusalamūlāni anuvādādhikaraṇassa mūlaṃ, tīṇipi kusalamūlāni anuvādādhikaraṇassa mūlaṃ, kāyaupi anuvādādhikaraṇassa mūlaṃ, vācāpi anuvādādhikaraṇassa mūlaṃ, idaṃ khau bhantai anuvādādhikaraṇassa mūlaṃ|

pucchā – kiṃ  panāvusau āpattādhikaraṇassa mūlaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – cha bhantai āpattisamuṭṭhānā āpattādhikaraṇassa mūlaṃ, atti bhantai āpatti kāyatau samuṭṭhāti na vācatau na cittatau, atthi bhantai āpatti vācatau samuṭṭhāti na kāyatau na cittatau, atthi bhantai āpatti kāyatau  ca vācatau ca samuṭṭhāti na cittatau, atthi bhantai āpatti kāyatau ca cittatau ca samuṭṭhāti na vācatau, atthi bhantai āpatti vācatau ca cittatau ca samuṭṭhāti na kāyatau, atthi bhantai āpatti kāyatau ca vācatau ca cittatau ca samuṭṭhāti, imāni bhantai cha āpatti samuṭṭhānāni āpattādhikaraṇassa mūlaṃ|

pucchā – kiṃ  panāvusau kic‍cādhikaraṇassa mūlaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – kic‍cādhikaraṇassa bhantai aikaṃ mūlaṃ saṅghau|

pucchā – samathaṃ āvusau pucchissaṃ, sanidānaṃ saniddaisaṃ| samuk‍kaṭṭhapadānaṃ āvusau kati āpattiyau|

vissaj‍janā – samathaṃ bhantai vissaj‍jissaṃ, sanidānaṃ saniddaisaṃ| samuk‍kaṭṭhapadānaṃ bhantai dvai āpattiyau|

5. khuddakavatthukkhandhaka

pucchā – nahāyantaina  āvusau bhikkhunā rukkhai vā thambhai vā kuṭṭai vā kāyaṃ ugghaṃsaintassa duk‍kaṭaṃ bhagavatā kattha pañ‍ñattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – rājagahai bhantai chabbaggiyai bhikkhū ārabbha pañ‍ñattaṃ, chabbaggiyā bhantai bhikkhū nahāyamānā rukkhaipi thambhaipi kuṭṭaipi kāyaṃ ugghaṃsaisuṃ, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

pucchā – gandhabbahatthakaina vā āvusau kuruvindakasuttiyā vā mal‍lakaina vā nahāyantassa duk‍kaṭaṃ bhagavatā kattha pañ‍ñattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – rājagahai bhantai chabbaggiyaiva bhikkhū ārabbha pañ‍ñattaṃ, chabbaggiyā bhantai bhikkhū gandhabbahatthakainapi kuruvindakasuttiyāpi  mal‍lakainapi nahāyiṃsu, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

pucchā – pāṇinā āvusau parikammaṃ kātuṃ bhagavatā kattha anuñ‍ñātaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ anuñ‍ñātaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – rājagahai bhantai sambahulai bhikkhū ārabbha anuñ‍ñātaṃ, sambahulā bhantai bhikkhū piṭṭhiparikammaṃ kātuṃ kuk‍kuc‍cāyiṃsu, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ anuñ‍ñātaṃ|

pucchā – val‍likaṃ  vā āvusau pāmaṅgaṃ vā kaṇṭhasuttakaṃ vā kaṭisuttakaṃ vā auvaṭṭikaṃ vā kāyuraṃ vā hatthābharaṇaṃ vā aṅgulimuddikaṃ vā dhāraintassa duk‍kaṭaṃ bhagavatā kattha pañ‍ñattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – rājagahai bhantai chabbaggiyai bhikkhū ārabbha pañ‍ñattaṃ, chabbaggiyā bhantai bhikkhū val‍likādīni dhāraisuṃ, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

pucchā – dīghai  āvusau kaisai dhāraintassa duk‍kaṭaṃ bhagavatā kattha pañ‍ñattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – rājagahai bhantai chabbaggiyai bhikkhū ārabbha pañ‍ñattaṃ, chabbaggiyā bhantai bhikkhū dīghai kaisai dhāraisuṃ, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

pucchā – kaucchainavā  āvusau phaṇakainavā hatthaphaṇakainavā kaisai ausaṇṭhaintassa duk‍kaṭaṃ bhagavatā kattha pañ‍ñattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – rājagahai bhantai chabbaggiyaiva bhikkhū ārabbha pañ‍ñattaṃ, chabbaggiyā bhantai bhikkhū kaucchādīhi kaisai ausaṇṭhaisuṃ, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

pucchā – ādāsai  vā āvusau udakapattai vā mukhanimittaṃ aulaukaintassa duk‍kaṭaṃ bhagavatā kattha pañ‍ñattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – rājagahai bhantai chabbaggiyaiva bhikkhū ārabbha pañ‍ñattaṃ, chabbaggiyā bhantai bhikkhū ādāsaipi udakapattaipi mukhanimittaṃ aulaukaisuṃ, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

pucchā – ābādhapac‍cayā  panāvusau ādāsai vā udakapattai vā mukhanimittaṃ aulaukaituṃ bhagavatā kattha anuñ‍ñātaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ anuñ‍ñātaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – rājagahai bhantai añ‍ñataraṃ bhikkhuṃ ārabbha anuñ‍ñātaṃ, bhagavati bhantai rājagahai viharati añ‍ñatarassa bhikkhunau mukhai vaṇau ahausi, sau bhikkhū aitadavauca ‘‘kīdisau mai āvusau vaṇau’’ti, bhikkhū aivamāhaṃsu ‘‘aidisau tai āvusau vaṇau’’ti, sau na saddahati, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ anuñ‍ñātaṃ|

pucchā – mukhālaipanādīni  āvusau karauntassa duk‍kaṭaṃ bhagavatā kattha pañ‍ñattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – rājagahai bhantai chabbaggiyai bhikkhū ārabbha pañ‍ñattaṃ, chabbaggiyā bhantai bhikkhū mukhālaipanādīni akaṃsu, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

pucchā – nac‍caṃ  vā āvusau gītaṃ vā vāditaṃ vā dassanāya gacchantassa duk‍kaṭaṃ bhagavatā kattha pañ‍ñattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – rājagahai bhantai chabbaggiyai bhikkhū ārabbha pañ‍ñattaṃ, chabbaggiyā bhantai nac‍campi gītampi vāditampi dassanāya gacchiṃsu, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

pucchā – kathaṃ āvusau nac‍cādīni passantassa āpatti hauti, kathaṃ pana anāpatti|

vissaj‍janā – nac‍caṃ vā bhantai gītaṃ vā vāditaṃ vā dassanāya gacchati āpatti duk‍kaṭassa, yattha ṭhitau passati vā suṇāti vā āpatti duk‍kaṭassa, dassanūpacāraṃ vijahitvā punappunaṃ passati āpatti duk‍kaṭassa, ārāmai ṭhitassa passatau anāpatti, vihāratau pana vihāraṃ passissāmīti gacchatau āpattiyaiva, yattha ṭhitau passati vā suṇāti vā āpatti duk‍kaṭassa, āsanasālāya nisin‍nau passati anāpatti, passissāmīti vuṭṭhahitvā gacchati āpatti duk‍kaṭassa, yattha ṭhitau passati vā suṇāti vā āpatti duk‍kaṭassa, patipathaṃ gacchantau passati anāpatti, gīvaṃ parivattaitvā passatau pana āpatti bhantai|

pucchā – āyatakaina  āvusau gītassaraina dhammaṃ gāyantassa duk‍kaṭaṃ bhagavatā kattha pañ‍ñattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – rājagahai bhantai chabbaggiyai bhikkhū ārabbha pañ‍ñattaṃ, chabbaggiyā bhantai bhikkhū āyatakaina gītassaraina dhammaṃ gāyiṃsu, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

pucchā – dārupattaṃ  āvusau dhāraintassa duk‍kaṭaṃ bhagavatā kattha pañ‍ñattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – rājagahai bhantai āyasmantaṃ piṇḍaulabhāradvājaṃ ārabbha pañ‍ñattaṃ, āyasmā bhantai piṇḍaulabhāradvājau chavassa dārupattassa kāraṇā gihīnaṃ uttarimanussadhammaṃ iddhipāṭihāriyaṃ dassaiti, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

na  ca bhikkhavai dārupattau dhāraitabbau, yau dhāraiyya āpatti duk‍kaṭassa –

pucchā – bhagavatā āvusau uc‍cāvacai pattai paṭikkhipitvā ayau pattau bhūmipattauti imaiyaiva dvai pattā kattha anuñ‍ñātā, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ anuñ‍ñātā|

vissaj‍janā – rājagahai bhantai chabbaggiyai bhikkhū ārabbha anuñ‍ñātā, chabbaggiyā bhantai bhikkhū uc‍cāvacai pattai dhāraisuṃ sauvaṇṇamayā  rūpiyamayā, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ anuñ‍ñātā|

pucchā – saudakaṃ  āvusau pattaṃ paṭisāmaintassa ca autāpaintassaca uṇhai pattaṃ nidahantassa ca duk‍kaṭaṃ bhagavatā kattha pañ‍ñattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – rājagahai bhantai sambahulai bhikkhū ārabbha pañ‍ñattaṃ, sambahulā bhantai bhikkhū saudakaṃ pattaṃ paṭisāmaisuṃ, autāpaisuṃ, uṇhai pattaṃ nidahiṃsu, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

pucchā – chavasīsapattaṃ  āvusau dhāraintassa ca sabbapaṃsukūlikassa ca duk‍kaṭaṃ bhagavatā kattha pañ‍ñattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – rājagahai bhantai añ‍ñataraṃ bhikkhuṃ ārabbha pañ‍ñattaṃ, añ‍ñatarau bhantai bhikkhu sabbapaṃsukūlikau ahausi, sau chavasīsassa pattaṃ dhāraisi, añ‍ñatarā itthī passitvā bhītā  vissaramakāsi ‘‘abhuṃ mai pisācau vatāya’’nti, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

pucchā – calakāni vā āvusau aṭṭhikāni vā ucchiṭṭhaudakaṃ vā pattaina nīharantassa duk‍kaṭaṃ bhagavatā kattha pañ‍ñattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – rājagahai bhantai sambahulai bhikkhū ārabbha pañ‍ñattaṃ, sambahulā bhantai bhikkhū calakānipi aṭṭhikānipi ucchiṭṭhau dakampi pattaina nīhariṃsu, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

pucchā – upāhanatthavikā  āvusau bhagavatā kattha anuñ‍ñātā, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ anuñ‍ñātā|

vissaj‍janā – antarā ca bhantai rājagahaṃ antarā ca vaisāliṃ añ‍ñataraṃ bhikkhuṃ ārabbha anuñ‍ñātā, añ‍ñatarau bhantai bhikkhu upāhanāyau kāyabandhanaina bandhitvā gāmaṃ piṇḍāya pāvisi, añ‍ñatarau upāsakau taṃ bhikkhuṃ abhivādaintau upāhanāyau sīsaina ghaṭṭaiti, sau bhikkhu maṅku ahausi, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ anuñ‍ñātā|

pucchā – addhānamaggappaṭipan‍naina  āvusau parissāvanaṃ yāciyamānaina na dadantassa ca appaṭissāvanakaina addhānaṃ paṭipaj‍jantassa ca duk‍kaṭaṃ bhagavatā kattha pañ‍ñattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – antarā ca bhantai rājagahaṃ antarā ca vaisāliṃ añ‍ñataraṃ bhikkhuṃ ārabbha pañ‍ñattaṃ, añ‍ñatarau bhantai bhikkhu parissāvanaṃ yāciyamānau na adāsi, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

pucchā – pupphābhikiṇṇai  āvusau sayanai sayantassa duk‍kaṭaṃ bhagavatā kattha pañ‍ñattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – vaisāliyaṃ bhantai chabbaggiyai bhikkhū ārabbha pañ‍ñattaṃ, chabbaggiyā bhantai bhikkhū pupphābhikiṇṇaisu sayanaisu sayiṃsu, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

pucchā – aikabhājanai vā āvusau bhuñ‍jantānaṃ aikathālakai vā pivantānaṃ aikattharaṇapāvuraṇānaṃ vā tuvaṭṭānaṃ duk‍kaṭaṃ bhagavatā kattha pañ‍ñattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – vaisāliyaṃ bhantai chabbaggiyaiva bhikkhū ārabbha pañ‍ñattaṃ, chabbaggiyā bhantai bhikkhū aikabhājanaipi bhuñ‍jiṃsu, aikathālakaipi  piviṃsu, aikamañ‍cakaipi tuvaṭṭaisuṃ, aikattharaṇāpi tuvaṭṭaisuṃ, aikapāvuraṇāpi tuvaṭṭaisuṃ, aikattharaṇapāvuraṇāpi, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

pucchā – cāmaribījaniṃ āvusau paṭikkhipitvā tissau bījaniyau bhagavatā kattha anuñ‍ñātā, kismiñ‍ca vatthusmiṃ anuñ‍ñātā|

vissaj‍janā – sāvatthiyaṃ bhantai anuñ‍ñātā, sāvatthiyaṃ bhantai bhagavati viharati saṅghassa cāmaribījanī uppan‍nā ahausi, bhagavatau aitamatthaṃ āraucaisuṃ, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ anuñ‍ñātā|

na  bhikkhavai cāmaribījanī dhāraitabbā, yau dhāraiyya, āpatti duk‍kaṭassa| anujānāmi bhikkhavai tissau bījaniyau vākamayaṃ usīramayaṃ maurapiñchā mayaṃ|

pucchā – dīghai āvusau nakhai dhāraintassa duk‍kaṭaṃ bhagavatā kattha pañ‍ñattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – sāvatthiyaṃ  bhantai añ‍ñataraṃ bhikkhuṃ ārabbha pañ‍ñattaṃ, añ‍ñatarau bhantai bhikkhu dīghai nakhai dhāraisi, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

pucchā – kattarikāya  āvusau kaisai chaidāpaintassa duk‍kaṭaṃ bhagavatā kattha pañ‍ñattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – sāvatthiyaṃ bhantai chabbaggiyai bhikkhū ārabbha pañ‍ñattaṃ, chabbaggiyā bhantai bhikkhū kattarikāya kaisai chindiṃsu, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

pucchā – dīghai  āvusau nāsikālaumai dhāraintassa duk‍kaṭaṃ bhagavatā kattha pañ‍ñattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – sāvatthiyaṃ bhantai sambahulai bhikkhū ārabbha pañ‍ñattaṃ, sambahulā bhantai bhikkhū dīghāni nāsikālaumāni dhāraisuṃ, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

pucchā – uc‍cāvacā āvusau kaṇṇamalaharaṇiyau paṭikkhipitvā dasa kaṇṇamalaharaṇiyau bhagavatā kattha anuñ‍ñātā, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ anuñ‍ñātā|

vissaj‍janā – sāvatthiyaṃ bhantai chabbaggiyai bhikkhū ārabbha anuñ‍ñātā, chabbaggiyā bhantai bhikkhū uc‍cāvacā kaṇṇamalaharaṇiyau dhāraisuṃ  sauvaṇṇamayaṃ rūpiyamayaṃ, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ anuñ‍ñātā|

pucchā – saṅghāṭipal‍latthikāya nisīdantassa āvusau duk‍kaṭaṃ bhagavatā kattha pañ‍ñattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – sāvatthiyaṃ bhantai chabbaggiyai bhikkhū ārabbha pañ‍ñattaṃ, chabbaggiyā bhantai bhikkhū saṅghāṭipal‍latthikāya nisīdiṃsu, saṅghāṭiyā  pattā luj‍jiṃsu, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

pucchā – akāyabandhanaina āvusau gāmaṃ pavisantassa duk‍kaṭaṃ bhagavatā kattha pañ‍ñattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – sāvatthiyaṃ bhantai añ‍ñataraṃ bhikkhuṃ ārabbha pañ‍ñattaṃ, añ‍ñatarau bhantai bhikkhu akāyabandhanau gāmaṃ piṇḍāya pāvisi, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

pucchā – gihinivatthaṃ  āvusau nivāsaintassa ca, gihipārutaṃ pārupantassa ca, saṃvail‍liyaṃ nivāsaintassa ca duk‍kaṭaṃ bhagavatā kattha pañ‍ñattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – sāvatthiyaṃ bhantai chabbaggiyai bhikkhū ārabbha pañ‍ñattaṃ, chabbaggiyā bhantai bhikkhū gihinivatthaṃ nivāsiṃsu, gihipārutampi pārupiṃsu, saṃvail‍liyampi nivāsiṃsu, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

pucchā – ubhatau  kājaṃ āvusau dhāraintassa duk‍kaṭaṃ bhagavatā kattha pañ‍ñattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – sāvatthiyaṃ bhantai chabbaggiyai bhikkhū ārabbha pañ‍ñattaṃ, chabbaggiyā bhantai bhikkhū ubhatau kājaṃ dhāraisuṃ, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

pucchā – laukāyataṃ  āvusau pariyāpuṇantassa ca vācaintassa ca duk‍kaṭaṃ bhagavatā kattha pañ‍ñattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – sāvatthiyaṃ bhantai chabbaggiyai bhikkhū ārabbha pañ‍ñattaṃ, chabbaggiyā bhantai bhikkhū laukāyataṃ pariyāpuṇiṃsupi vācaisumpi, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

pucchā – tiracchānavij‍jaṃ  āvusau pariyāpuṇantassa ca vācaintassa ca duk‍kaṭaṃ bhagavatā kattha pañ‍ñattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – sāvatthiyaṃ bhantai chabbaggiyai bhikkhū ārabbha pañ‍ñattaṃ, chabbaggiyā bhantai bhikkhū tiracchānavij‍jaṃ pariyāpuṇiṃsupi vācaisumpi, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

pucchā – khipitai āvusau ‘‘jīvā’’tivadantassa duk‍kaṭaṃ bhagavatā kattha pañ‍ñattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – sāvatthiyaṃ  bhantai sambahulai bhikkhū ārabbha pañ‍ñattaṃ, bhagavā bhantai mahatiyā parisāya parivutau dhammaṃ daisaintau khipi, bhikkhū ‘‘jīvatu bhantai bhagavā, jīvatu sugatauti’’ uc‍cāsaddaṃ mahāsaddaṃ akaṃsu, taina saddaina dhammakathā antarā ahausi, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

pucchā – ārāmai āvusau passāvavac‍cānaṃ tahaṃ tahaṃ karaṇaṃ paṭikkhipitvā aikamantaṃ kātuṃ bhagavatā kattha pañ‍ñattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – sāvatthiyaṃ bhantai sambahulai bhikkhū ārabbha pañ‍ñattaṃ, sambahulā bhantai bhikkhū ārāmai tahaṃ tahaṃ passāvaṃ akaṃsu, tahaṃ tahaṃ vac‍caṃ akaṃsu, ārāmau dussi, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

khuddakavatthukkhandhaka

pucchā – khuddakavatthuṃ  pucchissaṃ, sanidānaṃ saniddaisaṃ| samuk‍kaṭṭhapadānaṃ āvusau kati āpattiyau|

vissaj‍janā – khuddakaṃ vissaj‍jissaṃ, sanidānaṃ saniddaisaṃ| samuk‍kaṭṭhapadānaṃ bhantai tissau āpattiyau|

6. saināsanakkhandhaka

pucchā – pañ‍ca  āvusau laiṇāni bhagavatā kattha anuñ‍ñātāni, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ anuñ‍ñātāni|

vissaj‍janā – rājagahai bhantai sambahulai bhikkhū ārabbha anuñ‍ñātāni, sambahulā bhantai bhikkhū bhagavantaṃ upasaṅkamitvā aitadavaucuṃ ‘‘rājagahakau bhantai saiṭṭhī vihārai kārāpaitukāmau, kathaṃ nu khau bhantai paṭipaj‍jitabba’’nti, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ anuñ‍ñātāni|

pucchā – kathaṃ  āvusau vihārā paṭhamaṃ uppan‍nā, kathañ‍ca tai patiṭṭhāpi tā|

vissaj‍janā – aikāhainaiva  bhantai rājagahakaina saiṭṭhinā chaṭṭhivihārā patiṭṭhāpitā, tai idha bhantai saṭṭhivihārā buddhappamukhassa āgatānāgatassa cātuddisassa saṅghassa patiṭṭhāpitā|

pucchā – vihārai  āvusau paṭibhānakammaṃ paṭikkhipitvā mālākammādīni bhagavatā kattha anuñ‍ñātāni, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ anuñ‍ñātāni|

vissaj‍janā – rājagahai bhantai chabbaggiyai bhikkhū ārabbha anuñ‍ñātāni| chabbaggiyā bhantai bhikkhū vihārai paṭibhānacittaṃ kārāpaisuṃ itthirūpakaṃ purisarūpakaṃ, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ anuñ‍ñātāni|

pucchā – yathāvuḍḍhaṃ  āvusau abhivādanādīni ca aggāsanādīni ca anujānitvā saṅghikaṃ yathāvuḍḍhaṃ paṭibāhantassa duk‍kaṭaṃ bhagavatā kattha pañ‍ñattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – antarā ca bhantai vaisāliṃ antarā ca sāvatthiṃ chabbaggiyānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ antaivāsikai bhikkhū ārabbha pañ‍ñattaṃ, chabbaggiyānaṃ bhantai bhikkhūnaṃ antaivāsikā bhikkhū buddhappamukhassa saṅghassa puratau puratau gantvā vihārai paṭiggahaisuṃ, saiyyāyau paṭiggahaisuṃ ‘‘idaṃ amhākaṃ upajjhāyānaṃ bhavissati, idaṃ amhākaṃ ācariyānaṃ bhavissati, idaṃ amhākaṃ bhavissatī’’ti, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

pucchā – aikaina  āvusau dvai paṭibāhantassa duk‍kaṭaṃ bhagavatā kattha pañ‍ñattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – sāvatthiyaṃ bhantai āyasmantaṃ upanandaṃ sakyaputtaṃ ārabbha pañ‍ñattaṃ, āyasmā bhantai upanandau sakyaputtau aikau dvai paṭibāhi, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

pucchā – navakaina  āvusau uddisantaina samakai vā āsanai nisīdituṃ uc‍catarai vā dhammagāravaina, thairaina pana bhikkhunā uddisāpaintaina samakai vā āsanai nisīdituṃ nīcatarai vā dhammagāravaina bhagavatā kattha anuñ‍ñātaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ anuñ‍ñātaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – sāvatthiyaṃ bhantai sambahulai bhikkhū ārabbha anuñ‍ñātaṃ, sambahulā bhantai bhikkhū āyasmatau upālissa santikai vinayaṃ pariyāpuṇiṃsu, āyasmā bhantai upāli ṭhitakauva uddisati thairānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ gāravaina, tattha bhantai thairā caiva bhikkhū kilamiṃsu āyasmā ca upāli kilami, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ anuñ‍ñātaṃ|

pucchā – tivassantaraina  āvusau saha nisīdituṃ bhagavatā kattha anuñ‍ñātaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ anuñ‍ñātaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – sāvatthiyaṃ bhantai sambahulai bhikkhū ārabbha anuñ‍ñātaṃ, sambahulānaṃ bhantai bhikkhūnaṃ aitadahausi ‘‘kittāvatānu khau samānāsanikau hautī’’ti, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ anuñ‍ñātaṃ|

pucchā – mañ‍cai  ca āvusau pīṭhai ca dvin‍naṃyaiva nisīdituṃ bhagavatā kattha anuñ‍ñātaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ anuñ‍ñātaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – sāvatthiyaṃ bhantai sambahulai samānāsanikai ārabbha pañ‍ñattaṃ, sambahulā bhantai bhikkhū samānāsanikā mañ‍cai nisīditvā mañ‍caṃ bhindiṃsu, pīṭhai nisīditvā pīṭhaṃ bhindiṃsu, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ anuñ‍ñātaṃ|

pucchā – dīghāsanai  panāvusau asamānāsanikaihipi nisīdituṃ bhagavatā kattha anuñ‍ñātaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ anuñ‍ñātaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – sāvatthiyaṃ bhantai sambahulai bhikkhū ārabbha anuñ‍ñātaṃ, sambahulā bhantai bhikkhū dīghāsanai asamānāsanikaihi saha nisīdituṃ kuk‍kuc‍cāyiṃsu, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ anuñ‍ñātaṃ|

pucchā – pañ‍ca  āvusau avissaj‍jiyāni bhagavatā kattha pañ‍ñattāni, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattāni|

vissaj‍janā – sāvatthiyaṃ bhantai sambahulai bhikkhū ārabbha pañ‍ñattāni, sambahulā bhantai bhikkhū saṅghikaṃ saināsanaṃ vissaj‍jaisuṃ, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

pucchā – pañ‍ca  āvusau avaibhaṅgiyāni bhagavatā kattha pañ‍ñattāni, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattāni|

vissaj‍janā – kīṭāgirismiṃ bhantai assajipunabbasukai bhikkhū ārabbha pañ‍ñattāni, assajipunabbasukā bhantai bhikkhū saṅghikaṃ saināsanaṃ vibhajiṃsu, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattāni|

pucchā – añ‍ñatra  paribhaugaṃ āvusau añ‍ñatra paribhuñ‍jantassa duk‍kaṭaṃ bhagavatā kattha pañ‍ñattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – āḷaviyaṃ bhantai sambahulai bhikkhū ārabbha pañ‍ñattaṃ, sambahulā bhantai bhikkhū añ‍ñatarassa upāsakassa vihāraparibhaugaṃ saināsanaṃ añ‍ñatra paribhuñ‍jiṃsu, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

pucchā – adhautaihi ca āvusau al‍laihi ca pādaihi saināsanaṃ ak‍kamantassa ca saupāhanaina saināsanaṃ ak‍kamantassa ca duk‍kaṭaṃ bhagavatā kattha pañ‍ñattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – āḷaviyaṃ  bhantai sambahulai bhikkhū ārabbha pañ‍ñattaṃ, sambahulā bhantai bhikkhū adhautaihi pādaihi saināsanaṃ ak‍kamiṃsu, al‍laihi ca pādaihi saināsanaṃ ak‍kamiṃsu, saupāhanāpi saināsanaṃ ak‍kamiṃsu, saināsanaṃ dussi, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

pucchā – parikammakatāya āvusau bhūmiyā niṭṭhubhantassa ca parikammakataṃ bhittiṃ apassayantassa ca duk‍kaṭaṃ bhagavatā kattha pañ‍ñattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – āḷaviyaṃ  bhantai sambahulaiva bhikkhū ārabbha pañ‍ñattaṃ, sambahulā bhantai bhikkhū parikammakatāya bhūmiyā niṭṭhubhiṃsu, parikammakataṃ bhittiṃ apassayiṃsu, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

pucchā – saināsanakkhandhakaṃ pucchissaṃ, sanidānaṃ saniddaisaṃ| samuk‍kaṭṭhapadānaṃ āvusau kati āpattiyau|

vissaj‍janā – saināsanakkhandhakaṃ  vissaj‍jissaṃ, sanidānaṃ saniddaisaṃ| samuk‍kaṭṭhapadānaṃ bhantai tissau āpattiyau|

7. saṅghataidakakkhandhaka

pucchā – kathaṃ  āvusau chan‍naṃ sakyakumārānaṃ saha upālikappakaina pabbaj‍jā ahausi, kathañ‍ca naisaṃ visaisau udapādi|

vissaj‍janā – bhagavā bhantai upāliṃ kappakaṃ paṭhamaṃ pabbājaisi, pacchā tai sakyakumārai, atha bhantai āyasmā bhaddiyau tainaiva antaravassaina tissau vij‍jā sacchākāsi, āyasmā anuruddhau dibbacakkhuṃ uppādaisi, āyasmā ānandau sautāpattiphalaṃ sacchākāsi, daivadattau pauthuj‍janikaṃ iddhiṃ abhinipphādaisi| aivaṃ khau bhantai chan‍naṃ sakyakumārānaṃ upālikappakaina saha pabbaj‍jā ahausi, aivañ‍ca pana bhantai taisaṃ visaisādhigamau ahausi|

pakāsanīya

pucchā – pakāsanīyakammaṃ  āvusau kātuṃ bhagavatā kattha pañ‍ñattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – rājagahai bhantai daivadattaṃ ārabbha pañ‍ñattaṃ, daivadattau bhantai ‘‘sarājikāya maṃ bhagavā parisāya khaiḷāsakavādaina apasādaiti, sāriputtamauggal‍lānaiva uk‍kaṃsatī’’ti kupitau anattamanau bhagavati āghātaṃ bandhi, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

pakāsanīya

pucchā – daivadattassa  āvusau pakāsanīyakammaṃ kārāpaitvā kathaṃ bhagavā pakāsaituṃ āṇāpaisi|

vissaj‍janā – bhagavā bhantai daivadattassa pakāsanīyakammaṃ kātuṃ pañ‍ñapaitvā āyasmantaṃ sāriputtaṃ āmantaisi ‘‘taina hi tvaṃ sāriputta daivadattaṃ rājagahai pakāsaihī’’ti, aivaṃ khau bhantai daivadattassa pakāsanīyakammaṃ katvā daivadattaṃ rājagahai pakāsaituṃ bhagavā āṇāpaisi|

daivadatta

pucchā – kathaṃ  āvusau daivadattaina duṭṭhacittaina vadhakacittaina tathā gatassa ruhiraṃ uppādaitvā paṭhamaṃ ānantariyaṃ kammaṃ upacitaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – bhagavā bhantai gijjhakūṭassa pabbatassa chāyāyaṃ caṅkami, atha bhantai daivadattau gijjhakūṭaṃ pabbataṃ āruhitvā mahatiṃ silaṃ pavijjhi ‘‘imāya samaṇaṃ gautamaṃ jīvitā vauraupaissāmī’’ti, atha khau bhantai dvai pabbatakūṭāni samāgantvā taṃ silaṃ sampaṭicchiṃsu, tatau papatikā uppatitvā bhagavatau pādai ruhiraṃ uppādaisi| aivaṃ khau bhantai daivadattaina duṭṭhaina vadhakacittaina tathāgatassa ruhiraṃ uppādaitvā paṭhamaṃ ānantariyakammaṃ upacitaṃ|

pucchā – kulaisu  āvusau tikabhaujanaṃ bhagavatā kattha pañ‍ñattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – rājagahai bhantai daivadattaṃ ārabbha pañ‍ñattaṃ, daivadattau bhantai parihīnalābhasak‍kārau saparisau kulaisu viñ‍ñāpaitvā viñ‍ñāpaitvā bhuñ‍ji, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

pucchā – alaṃ  daivadatta, mā tai ruc‍ci saṅghabhaidau, garukau khau daivadatta saṅghabhaidautiādikau āvusau auvādau bhagavatā kattha din‍nau, kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ din‍nau|

vissaj‍janā – rājagahai bhantai daivadattaṃ ārabbha din‍nau, daivadattau bhantai saṅghabhaidāya parak‍kami cak‍kabhaidāya, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ din‍nau|

alaṃ  daivadatta, mā tai ruc‍ci saṅghabhaidau, garukau khau daivadatta saṅghabhaidau –

pucchā – ‘‘sukaraṃ sādhunā sādhuṃ, sādhuṃ pāpaina duk‍karaṃ| pāpaṃ pāpaina sukaraṃ, pāpamariyaihi duk‍kara’’nti- āvusau  idaṃ udānaṃ bhagavatā kattha udānitaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ udānitaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – rājagahai bhantai daivadattaṃyaiva ārabbha udānitaṃ, āyasmā bhantai ānandau pubbaṇhasamayaṃ nivāsaitvā pattacīvaramādāya rājagahaṃ piṇḍāya pāvisi, atha khau bhantai daivadattau yaināyasmā ānandau tainupasaṅkami, upasaṅkamitvā āyasmantaṃ ānandaṃ aitadavauca ‘‘aj‍jataggai dānāhaṃ āvusau ānanda añ‍ñatraiva bhagavatā añ‍ñatraiva bhikkhusaṅghā upausathaṃ karissāmi saṅghakammaṃ karissāmī’’ti, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ udānitaṃ|

sukaraṃ sādhunā sādhuṃ, sādhuṃ pāpaina duk‍karaṃ|

pāpaṃ pāpaina sukaraṃ, pāpamariyaihi duk‍karaṃ||

pucchā – bhaidānuvattakānaṃ  āvusau bhikkhūnaṃ thul‍lac‍cayaṃ bhagavatā kattha pañ‍ñattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – rājagahai bhantai sambahulai bhikkhū ārabbha pañ‍ñattaṃ, āyasmā bhantai sāriputtau bhagavantaṃ upasaṅkamitvā aitadavauca ‘‘sādhu bhantai bhaidānuvattakā bhikkhū puna upasampaj‍jaiyyu’’nti, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

‘‘sādhu  bhantai bhaidānuvattakā bhikkhū puna upasampaj‍jaiyyu’’nti –

pucchā – kittāvatā  nu khau āvusau saṅgharāji hauti nau ca saṅghabhaidau, kittāvatā ca pana saṅgharāji caiva hauti saṅghabhaidau ca|

vissaj‍janā – aikatau bhantai aikau hauti, aikatau dvai catutthau anussāvaiti, salākaṃ gāhaiti ‘‘ayaṃ dhammau ayaṃ vinayau idaṃ satthusāsanaṃ imaṃ gaṇhatha imaṃ raucaithāti’’, aitainaiva bhantai nayaina catun‍naṃ vā pañ‍can‍naṃ vā chan‍naṃ vā sattan‍naṃ vā aṭṭhan‍naṃ vā saṅgharāji hauti, nau ca saṅghabhaidau, aikatau bhantai cattārau haunti, aikatau cattārau, navamau anussāvaiti, salākaṃ gāhaiti ‘‘ayaṃ dhammau ayaṃ vinayau idaṃ satthusāsanaṃ imaṃ gaṇhatha imaṃ raucaithā’’ti, aivaṃ khau bhantai saṅgharāji caiva hauti saṅghabhaidau ca| aivaṃ khau bhantai saṅgharāji hauti, nau ca saṅghabhaidau, aivañ‍ca pana bhantai saṅgharāji caiva hauti saṅghabhaidau ca|

pucchā – kittāvatā  nu khau āvusau saṅghau bhin‍nau hauti|

vissaj‍janā – idha bhantai bhikkhū adhammādiṃ dhammādīnīti dīpainti, tai imaihi aṭṭhārasahi vatthūhi apakassanti, avapakassanti, āvainiṃ  upausathaṃ karaunti, āvainiṃ pavāraṇaṃ karaunti, āvainiṃ saṅghakammaṃ karaunti, aivaṃ khau bhantai saṅghau bhin‍nau hauti|

pucchā – kittāvatā nu khau āvusau saṅghau samaggau hauti|

vissaj‍janā – idha bhantai bhikkhū adhammaṃ adhammauti dīpainti, dhammaṃ dhammauti dīpainti, avinayaṃ avinayauti dīpainti…pai॰… duṭṭhul‍laṃ āpattiṃ duṭṭhul‍lā āpattīti dīpainti, aduṭṭhul‍laṃ āpattiṃ aduṭṭhul‍lā āpattīti dīpainti, tai imaihi aṭṭhārasahi vatthūhi na apakassanti, na avapakassanti, na āvainiṃ upausathaṃ karaunti, na āvainiṃ pavāraṇaṃ karaunti, na āvainiṃ saṅghakammaṃ karaunti, aittāvatā khau bhantai saṅghau samaggau hauti|

pucchā – samaggaṃ  āvusau saṅghaṃ bhinditvā kiṃ sau pasavati|

vissaj‍janā – samaggaṃ khau bhantai saṅghaṃ bhinditvā kappaṭṭhitikaṃ kibbisaṃ pasavati, kappaṃ nirayamhi pac‍cati|

pucchā – bhin‍naṃ  khau āvusau saṅghaṃ samaggaṃ katvā kiṃ sau pasavati|

vissaj‍janā – bhin‍naṃ khau bhantai saṅghaṃ samaggaṃ katvā brahmaṃ puñ‍ñaṃ pasavati, kappaṃ saggamhi maudati|

pucchā – saṅghabhaidaṃ  pucchissaṃ, sanidānaṃ saniddaisaṃ| samuk‍kaṭṭhapadānaṃ āvusau kati āpattiyau|

vissaj‍janā – saṅghabhaidaṃ vissaj‍jissaṃ, sanidānaṃ saniddaisaṃ| samuk‍kaṭṭhapadānaṃ bhantai dvai āpattiyau|

8. vattakkhandhaka

pucchā – āgantukānaṃ  āvusau bhikkhūnaṃ vattaṃ bhagavatā kattha pañ‍ñattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – sāvatthiyaṃ bhantai sambahulai āgantukai bhikkhū ārabbha pañ‍ñattaṃ, āgantukā bhantai bhikkhū saupāhanāpi ārāmaṃ pavisiṃsu, chattapaggahitāpi ārāmaṃ pavisiṃsu, auguṇṭhitāpi ārāmaṃ pavisiṃsu, sīsaipi cīvaraṃ karitvā ārāmaṃ pavisiṃsu, pānīyainapi pādai dauviṃsu, vuḍḍhataraipi āvāsikai bhikkhū nābhivādaisuṃ, napi saināsanaṃ pucchiṃsu, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ ‘‘taina hi bhikkhavai āgantukānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ vattaṃ pañ‍ñapaissāmi, yathā āgantukaihi bhikkhūhi sammā vattitabbaṃ, āgantukaina bhikkhavai bhikkhunā idāni ārāmaṃ pavisissāmīti upāhanā aumuñ‍citvā nīcaṃ katvā papphauṭaitvā gahaitvā chattaṃ apanāmaitvā sīsaṃ vivaritvā sīsai cīvaraṃ khandhai katvā sādhukaṃ ataramānaina ārāmau pavisitabbau’’ aivamādinā bhantai āgantukānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ vattaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

pucchā – āvāsikānaṃ  āvusau bhikkhūnaṃ vattaṃ bhagavatā kattha kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ, kathañ‍ca pana pañ‍ñattaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – sāvatthiyaṃ bhantai sambahulai āvāsikai bhikkhū ārabbha pañ‍ñattaṃ, sambahulā bhantai āvāsikā bhikkhū āgantukai bhikkhū disvā naiva āsanaṃ pañ‍ñapaisuṃ, na pādaudakaṃ pādapīṭhaṃ pādakathalikaṃ upanikkhipiṃsu, na pac‍cuggantvā pattacīvaraṃ paṭiggahaisuṃ, na saināsanaṃ pañ‍ñapaisuṃ, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ –

‘‘āvāsikaina bhikkhavai bhikkhunā āgantukaṃ bhikkhuṃ vuḍḍhataraṃ disvā āsanaṃ pañ‍ñapaitabbaṃ, pādaudakaṃ pādapīṭhaṃ pādakathalikaṃ upanikkhipitabbaṃ, pac‍cuggantvā pattacīvaraṃ paṭiggahaitabbaṃ  pānīyaina pucchitabbau’’ aivamādinā bhantai āvāsikānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ vattaṃ bhagavatā pañ‍ñattaṃ|

pucchā – gamikānaṃ  āvusau bhikkhūnaṃ vattaṃ bhagavatā kattha pañ‍ñattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ, kathañ‍capana pañ‍ñattaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – sāvatthiyaṃ bhantai sambahulai gamikai bhikkhū ārabbha pañ‍ñattaṃ, sambahulā bhantai gamikā bhikkhū dārubhaṇḍaṃ mattikābhaṇḍaṃ appaṭisāmaitvā dvāravātapānaṃ vivaritvā saināsanaṃ anāpucchā pak‍kamiṃsu, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ ‘‘gamikaina bhikkhavai bhikkhunā dārubhaṇḍaṃ paṭisāmaitvā dvāravātapānaṃ thakaitvā saināsanaṃ āpucchā pak‍kamitabbaṃ’’ aivamādinā bhantai ākāraina bhagavatā gamikānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ vattaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

pucchā – bhattaggai  āvusau anumaudituṃ bhagavatā kattha anuñ‍ñātaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ anuñ‍ñātaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – sāvatthiyaṃ  bhantai sambahulai bhikkhū ārabbha anuñ‍ñātaṃ, sambahulā bhantai bhikkhū bhattaggai nānumaudiṃsu, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ anuñ‍ñātaṃ|

pucchā – bhikkhūnaṃ āvusau bhattaggavattaṃ bhagavatā kattha pañ‍ñattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – sāvatthiyaṃ bhantai chabbaggiyai bhikkhū ārabbha pañ‍ñattaṃ, chabbaggiyā bhantai bhikkhū dun‍nivatthā duppārutā anākappasampan‍nā bhattaggaṃ gacchiṃsu, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ ‘‘sacai ārāmai kālau āraucitau hauti, timaṇḍalaṃ paṭicchādaintaina parimaṇḍalaṃ nivāsaitvā kāyabandhanaṃ bandhitvā saguṇaṃ katvā saṅghāṭiyau pārupitvā gaṇṭhikaṃ paṭimuñ‍citvā dhauvitvā  pattaṃ gahaitvā sādhukaṃ ataramānaina gāmau pavisitabbau’’ti aivamādinā bhantai bhattaggavattaṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

pucchā – bhikkhūnaṃ  āvusau saināsanavattaṃ bhagavatā kattha pañ‍ñattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ, kathañ‍ca taṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – sāvatthiyaṃ bhantai chabbaggiyai bhikkhū ārabbha pañ‍ñattaṃ, chabbaggiyā bhantai bhikkhū bhikkhūsu ajjhaukāsai cīvaraṃ karauntaisu paṭivātai aṅgaṇai saināsanaṃ papphauṭaisuṃ, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ ‘‘yasmiṃ vihārai viharati, sacai sau vihārau uk‍lāpau hauti, sacai ussahati saudhaitabbau’’ti aivamādinā bhantai bhikkhūnaṃ saināsanavattaṃ bhagavatā pañ‍ñattaṃ|

pucchā – samācāraṃ pucchissaṃ, sanidānaṃ saniddaisaṃ| samuk‍kaṭṭhapadānaṃ āvusau kati āpattiyau|

vissaj‍janā – samācāraṃ vissaj‍jissaṃ, sanidānaṃ saniddaisaṃ| samuk‍kaṭṭhapadānaṃ bhantai aikā āpatti|

9. pātimaukkhaṭṭhapanakkhandhaka

pucchā – sāpattikaina  āvusau pātimaukkhaṃ suṇantassa pātimaukkhaṃ ṭhapaituṃ bhagavatā kattha pañ‍ñattaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – sāvatthiyaṃ bhantai añ‍ñataraṃ puggalaṃ dussīlaṃ pāpadhammaṃ ārabbha pañ‍ñattaṃ, añ‍ñatarau bhantai puggalau dussīlau pāpadhammau tadahupausathai saṅghamajjhai nisin‍nau ahausi, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ pañ‍ñattaṃ|

pucchā – ṭhapanaṃ  āvusau pucchissaṃ, sanidānaṃ saniddaisaṃ| samuk‍kaṭṭhapadānaṃ āvusau kati āpattiyau|

vissaj‍janā – ṭhapanaṃ vissaj‍jissaṃ, sanidānaṃ saniddaisaṃ| samuk‍kaṭṭhapadānaṃ bhantai aikā āpatti|

10. bhikkhunikkhandhaka

pucchā – aṭṭhahi  āvusau garudhammaihi upasampadā bhagavatā kattha anuñ‍ñātā, kassa anuñ‍ñātā, kismiñ‍ca vatthusmiṃ anuñ‍ñātā|

vissaj‍janā – vaisāliyaṃ bhantai mahāpajāpatiyā gautamiyā anuñ‍ñātā, mahāpajāpati bhantai gautamī sūnaihi pādaihi rajaukiṇṇaina gattaina dukkhī dummanā assumukhī rudamānā bahidvārakauṭṭhakai aṭṭhāsi, atha khau bhantai āyasmā ānandau mahāpajāpatiṃ gautamiṃ taṃ kāraṇaṃ pucchitvā bhagavantaṃ catukkhattuṃ yāci mātugāmassa tathāgatappavaiditai dhammavinayai āgārasmā anāgāriyaṃ pabbaj‍jaṃ, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ anuñ‍ñātā|

pucchā – bhikkhūhi  āvusau bhikkhuniyau upasampādaituṃ bhagavatā kattha anuñ‍ñātaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ anuñ‍ñātaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – vaisāliyaṃ bhantai mahāpajāpatiṃ gautamiṃ ārabbha anuñ‍ñātaṃ, mahāpajāpati bhantai gautamī bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca ‘‘kathāhaṃ bhantai imāsu sākiyānīsu paṭipaj‍jāmī’’ti, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ anuñ‍ñātaṃ|

pucchā – aikatau  upasampan‍nāya āvusau bhikkhunisaṅghai visuddhāya bhikkhusaṅghai upasampādaituṃ bhagavatā kattha anuñ‍ñātaṃ, kaṃ ārabbha kismiṃ vatthusmiṃ anuñ‍ñātaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – sāvatthiyaṃ bhantai sambahulai bhikkhū ārabbha anuñ‍ñātaṃ, sambahulā bhantai bhikkhū bhikkhunīnaṃ antarāyikai dhammai pucchiṃsu, upasampadāpaikkhāyau vitthāyiṃsu, maṅkū ahaisuṃ, na sakkhiṃsu vissaj‍jaituṃ, tasmiṃ bhantai vatthusmiṃ anuñ‍ñātaṃ|

pucchā – bhikkhunikkhandhakaṃ  pucchissaṃ, sanidānaṃ saniddaisaṃ| samuk‍kaṭṭhapadānaṃ āvusau kati āpattiyau|

vissaj‍janā – bhikkhunikkhandhakaṃ vissaj‍jissaṃ, sanidānaṃ saniddaisaṃ| samuk‍kaṭṭhapadānaṃ bhantai dvai āpattiyau|

11. pañ‍casatikakkhandhaka

paṭhama saṃgāyanā

pucchā – pañ‍casatikaṃ  pucchissaṃ, sanidānaṃ saniddaisaṃ| samuk‍kaṭṭhapadānaṃ āvusau kati āpattiyau|

vissaj‍janā – pañ‍casatikaṃ vissaj‍jissaṃ, sanidānaṃ saniddaisaṃ| samuk‍kaṭṭhapadānaṃ bhantai na katamā āpatti|

12. sattasatikakkhandhaka

dutiya saṃgāyanā

pucchā – sattasatikaṃ  pucchissaṃ, sanidānaṃ saniddaisaṃ| samuk‍kaṭṭhapadānaṃ āvusau kati āpattiyau|

vissaj‍janā – sattasatikaṃ vissaj‍jissaṃ, sanidānaṃ saniddaisaṃ| samuk‍kaṭṭhapadānaṃ bhantai na katamā āpatti|

pucchā – kassa  āvusau vacanaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – bhagavatau bhantai vacanaṃ arahatau sammāsambuddhassa|

pucchā – kai āvusau sikkhanti|

vissaj‍janā – saikkhā ca bhantai puthuj‍janakalyāṇakā ca sikkhanti|

pucchā – kai  āvusau sikkhitasikkhā|

vissaj‍janā – arahantau bhantai sikkhitasikkhā|

pucchā – kattha āvusau ṭhitaṃ|

vissaj‍janā – sikkhākāmaisu bhantai ṭhitaṃ|

pucchā – kaināvusau ābhataṃ|

vissaj‍janā – paramparāya bhantai ābhataṃ|
